257def _get_parameter_for_reload(model: nn.Module, name: str) -> nn.Parameter:
258 """Resolve checkpoint names without changing the model's module tree."""
259 module_name, _, parameter_name = name.rpartition(".")
260 module = model.get_submodule(module_name)
261 if isinstance(module, BaseLayerWithLoRA):
262 module = module.base_layer
263 return module.get_parameter(parameter_name)

266AttnMetadataDict: TypeAlias = dict[str, AttentionMetadata]
267# list when ubatching is enabled
268PerLayerAttnMetadata: TypeAlias = list[AttnMetadataDict] | AttnMetadataDict

270
271def count_nans_per_row(logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
272 """Per-row NaN counts, left on device."""
273 return logits.isnan().sum(dim=-1, dtype=torch.int32)
274
275
276def nans_to_dict(counts: list[int], req_id_to_index: dict[str, int]) -> dict[str, int]:
277 """Map per-row NaN counts onto request ids.
278
279 Rows and requests need not correspond one-to-one, so requests without a
280 row are reported as 0.
281 """
282 return {
283 req_id: int(counts[i]) if i < len(counts) else 0
284 for req_id, i in req_id_to_index.items()
285 }

288# Wrapper for ModelRunnerOutput to support overlapped execution.
289class AsyncGPUModelRunnerOutput(AsyncModelRunnerOutput):
290 def __init__(
291 self,
292 model_runner_output: ModelRunnerOutput,
293 sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
294 logprobs_tensors: LogprobsTensors | None,
295 invalid_req_indices: list[int],
296 async_output_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream,
297 vocab_size: int,
298 routed_experts: RoutedExpertsTensors | None = None,
299 check_ep_fault: bool = False,
300 num_nans: torch.Tensor | None = None,
301 ):
302 self._model_runner_output = model_runner_output
303 self._invalid_req_indices = invalid_req_indices
304
305 # Event on the copy stream so we can synchronize the non-blocking copy.
306 # Blocking (sleep) event to avoid busy-polling the CUDA driver lock.
307 self.async_copy_ready_event = torch.cuda.Event(blocking=True)
308
309 # Keep a reference to the device tensor to avoid it being
310 # deallocated until we finish copying it to the host.
311 self._sampled_token_ids = sampled_token_ids
312 self.vocab_size = vocab_size
313 self._logprobs_tensors = logprobs_tensors
314 self._routed_experts = routed_experts
315 self._num_nans = num_nans
316 self._has_fault: torch.Tensor | None = None
317
318 # Initiate the copy on a separate stream, but do not synchronize it.
319 default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
320 with torch.cuda.stream(async_output_copy_stream):
321 async_output_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream)
322 self.sampled_token_ids_cpu = self._sampled_token_ids.to(
323 "cpu", non_blocking=True
324 )
325 self._logprobs_tensors_cpu = (
326 self._logprobs_tensors.to_cpu_nonblocking()
327 if self._logprobs_tensors
328 else None
329 )
330 self._routed_experts_cpu = (
331 self._routed_experts.to_cpu_nonblocking()
332 if self._routed_experts is not None
333 else None
334 )
335 self._num_nans_cpu = (
336 self._num_nans.to("cpu", non_blocking=True)
337 if self._num_nans is not None
338 else None
339 )
340 if check_ep_fault:
341 has_fault = get_ep_all2all_manager().query_fault()
342 self._has_fault = has_fault.to("cpu", non_blocking=True)
343 self.async_copy_ready_event.record()
344
345 def get_output(self) -> ModelRunnerOutput:
346 """Copy the device tensors to the host and return a ModelRunnerOutput.
347
348 This function blocks until the copy is finished.
349 """
350 max_gen_len = self.sampled_token_ids_cpu.shape[-1]
351 self.async_copy_ready_event.synchronize()
352
353 # Release the device tensors once the copy has completed.
354 del self._logprobs_tensors
355 del self._sampled_token_ids
356 if max_gen_len == 1:
357 valid_sampled_token_ids = self.sampled_token_ids_cpu.tolist()
358 for i in self._invalid_req_indices:
359 valid_sampled_token_ids[i].clear()
360 logprobs_lists = None
361 if self._logprobs_tensors_cpu is not None:
362 logprobs_lists = self._logprobs_tensors_cpu.tolists()
363 else:
364 valid_sampled_token_ids, logprobs_lists = RejectionSampler.parse_output(
365 self.sampled_token_ids_cpu,
366 self.vocab_size,
367 self._invalid_req_indices,
368 logprobs_tensors=self._logprobs_tensors_cpu,
369 )
370
371 output = self._model_runner_output
372 output.sampled_token_ids = valid_sampled_token_ids
373 output.logprobs = logprobs_lists
374
375 if self._routed_experts_cpu is not None:
376 output.routed_experts = self._routed_experts_cpu.tolists()
377 del self._routed_experts
378
379 if self._num_nans_cpu is not None:
380 output.num_nans_in_logits = nans_to_dict(
381 self._num_nans_cpu.tolist(), output.req_id_to_index
382 )
383 if envs.VLLM_RAISE_ON_LOGIT_NANS:
384 raise_if_nan_logits(output.num_nans_in_logits)
385 del self._num_nans
386
387 if self._has_fault is not None and self._has_fault.item():
388 mask = get_ep_all2all_manager().query_active_mask()
389 raise RuntimeError(
390 "Fault detected in EP all2all communication: "
391 "one or more ranks timed out during dispatch/combine. "
392 f"Mask: {mask.cpu().tolist()}"
393 )
394
395 return output
396
397
398def _copy_pooler_output_to_cpu(
399 raw_pooler_output: PoolerOutput, finished_mask: list[bool]
400) -> list[torch.Tensor | None]:
401 num_reqs = len(finished_mask)
402
403 if isinstance(raw_pooler_output, torch.Tensor):
404 if raw_pooler_output.shape[0] != num_reqs:
405 raise ValueError(
406 "Pooler output batch size does not match finished mask size: "
407 f"{raw_pooler_output.shape[0]} != {num_reqs}."
408 )
409
410 num_finished = sum(finished_mask)
411 if num_finished == 0:
412 return [None] * num_reqs
413 if num_finished == num_reqs:
414 return list(raw_pooler_output.to("cpu", non_blocking=True))
415
416 # partial finished
417 finished_indices = [i for i, include in enumerate(finished_mask) if include]
418 index_tensor = torch.tensor(
419 finished_indices, device=raw_pooler_output.device, dtype=torch.long
420 )
421 finished_outputs = raw_pooler_output.index_select(0, index_tensor).to(
422 "cpu", non_blocking=True
423 )
424 partial_pooler_output: list[torch.Tensor | None] = [None] * num_reqs
425 for i, out in zip(finished_indices, finished_outputs):
426 partial_pooler_output[i] = out
427 return partial_pooler_output
428
429 assert isinstance(raw_pooler_output, list)
430 if len(raw_pooler_output) != num_reqs:
431 raise ValueError(
432 "Pooler output batch size does not match finished mask size: "
433 f"{len(raw_pooler_output)} != {num_reqs}."
434 )
435
436 pooler_output: list[torch.Tensor | None] = [None] * num_reqs
437 for i, (out, include) in enumerate(zip(raw_pooler_output, finished_mask)):
438 if include and out is not None:
439 pooler_output[i] = out.to("cpu", non_blocking=True)
440 return pooler_output
441
442
443class AsyncGPUPoolingModelRunnerOutput(AsyncModelRunnerOutput):
444 def __init__(
445 self,
446 model_runner_output: ModelRunnerOutput,
447 raw_pooler_output: PoolerOutput,
448 finished_mask: list[bool],
449 async_output_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream,
450 ):
451 self._model_runner_output = model_runner_output
452
453 # Event on the copy stream so we can synchronize the non-blocking copy.
454 # Blocking (sleep) event to avoid busy-polling the CUDA driver lock.
455 self.async_copy_ready_event = torch.cuda.Event(blocking=True)
456
457 # Keep a reference to the device tensors to avoid them being
458 # deallocated until we finish copying it to the host.
459 self._raw_pooler_output = raw_pooler_output
460
461 # Initiate the copy on a separate stream, but do not synchronize it.
462 default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
463 with torch.cuda.stream(async_output_copy_stream):
464 async_output_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream)
465 self._model_runner_output.pooler_output = _copy_pooler_output_to_cpu(
466 raw_pooler_output=self._raw_pooler_output,
467 finished_mask=finished_mask,
468 )
469 self.async_copy_ready_event.record()
470
471 def get_output(self) -> ModelRunnerOutput:
472 """Copy the device tensors to the host and return a ModelRunnerOutput.
473 This function blocks until the copy is finished.
474 """
475 self.async_copy_ready_event.synchronize()
476
477 # Release the device tensors once the copy has completed.
478 del self._raw_pooler_output
479 return self._model_runner_output

482class ExecuteModelState(NamedTuple):
483 """Ephemeral cached state transferred between execute_model() and
484 sample_tokens(), after execute_model() returns None."""
485
486 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"
487 logits: torch.Tensor
488 spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata | None
489 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata | None
490 hidden_states: torch.Tensor
491 sample_hidden_states: torch.Tensor
492 aux_hidden_states: list[torch.Tensor] | None
493 ec_connector_output: ECConnectorOutput | None
494 cudagraph_stats: CUDAGraphStat | None
495 slot_mappings: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None
496
497
498class GPUModelRunner(
499 LoRAModelRunnerMixin, KVConnectorModelRunnerMixin, ECConnectorModelRunnerMixin
500):
501 @JitWarmupRegistry.capture
502 def __init__(
503 self,
504 vllm_config: VllmConfig,
505 device: torch.device,
506 ):
507 self.vllm_config = vllm_config
508 self.model_config = vllm_config.model_config
509 self.cache_config = vllm_config.cache_config
510 self.offload_config = vllm_config.offload_config
511 self.compilation_config = vllm_config.compilation_config
512 self.lora_config = vllm_config.lora_config
513 self.load_config = vllm_config.load_config
514 self.parallel_config = vllm_config.parallel_config
515 self.scheduler_config = vllm_config.scheduler_config
516 self.speculative_config = vllm_config.speculative_config
517 self.observability_config = vllm_config.observability_config
518
519 model_config = self.model_config
520 cache_config = self.cache_config
521 scheduler_config = self.scheduler_config
522 parallel_config = self.parallel_config
523 self.device = device
524 self.dtype = self.model_config.dtype
525
526 self.check_ep_fault = False
527 if parallel_config.data_parallel_size > 1 and self.model_config.is_moe:
528 self.check_ep_fault = get_ep_all2all_manager().support_fault_tolerance
529
530 self.kv_cache_dtype = kv_cache_dtype_str_to_dtype(
531 cache_config.cache_dtype, self.model_config
532 )
533
534 self.is_pooling_model = model_config.runner_type == "pooling"
535 self.enable_prompt_embeds = model_config.enable_prompt_embeds
536 self.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model = (
537 model_config.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model
538 )
539 # These will be overridden in load_model()
540 self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled = False
541 self.requires_sequential_video_encoding = False
542 # Set to True after init_routed_experts_capturer() completes.
543 # Prevents routed experts code from running during profiling/dummy run.
544 self.routed_experts_initialized = False
545 self.max_model_len = model_config.max_model_len
546
547 # Always set to false after the first forward pass
548 self.dcp_world_size = self.parallel_config.decode_context_parallel_size
549 self.dcp_rank = 0 if self.dcp_world_size <= 1 else get_dcp_group().rank_in_group
550 self.max_num_tokens = scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens
551 self.max_num_reqs = scheduler_config.max_num_seqs
552
553 # Broadcast PP output for external_launcher (torchrun)
554 # to make sure we are synced across pp ranks
555 # TODO: Support overlapping micro-batches
556 # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/18019
557 self.broadcast_pp_output = (
558 self.parallel_config.distributed_executor_backend == "external_launcher"
559 and len(get_pp_group().ranks) > 1
560 )
561
562 # Model-related.
563 self.num_query_heads = model_config.get_num_attention_heads(parallel_config)
564 self.inputs_embeds_size = model_config.get_inputs_embeds_size()
565 # Only relevant for models using ALiBi (e.g, MPT)
566 self.use_alibi = model_config.uses_alibi
567
568 self.cascade_attn_enabled = not self.model_config.disable_cascade_attn
569 self.is_mm_prefix_lm = self.model_config.is_mm_prefix_lm
570
571 # Multi-modal data support
572 self.mm_registry = MULTIMODAL_REGISTRY
573 self.uses_mrope = model_config.uses_mrope
574 self.mrope_num_dims = model_config.mrope_num_dims
575 self.supports_mm_inputs = self.mm_registry.supports_multimodal_inputs(
576 model_config
577 )
578
579 if self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder:
580 # Maximum length of the encoder input, only for encoder-decoder
581 # models.
582 self.max_encoder_len = scheduler_config.max_num_encoder_input_tokens
583 else:
584 self.max_encoder_len = 0
585
586 # Async scheduling
587 self.use_async_scheduling = self.scheduler_config.async_scheduling
588
589 # Async PP broadcast of sampled token ids, waited on in _prepare_input_ids.
590 self._pp_recv_work: torch.distributed.Work | None = None

592 # Sampler
593 self.sampler = Sampler(
594 logprobs_mode=self.model_config.logprobs_mode,

596 )

598 self.eplb_state: EplbState | None = None
599 self._moe_model: MixtureOfExperts | None = None

601 self.eep_eplb_suppressed = False
602 """
603 State of the expert parallelism load balancer.
604
605 Will be lazily initialized when the model is loaded.
606 """
607
608 # Lazy initializations
609 # self.model: nn.Module # Set after load_model

624 # Set up speculative decoding.
625 # NOTE(Jiayi): currently we put the entire draft model on
626 # the last PP rank. This is not ideal if there are many
627 # layers in the draft model.
628 if self.speculative_config and get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
629 self.drafter: (
630 NgramProposer # noqa: F823
631 | NgramProposerGPU
632 | SuffixDecodingProposer
633 | EagleProposer
634 | DFlashProposer
635 | DraftModelProposer

646 from vllm.v1.spec_decode.ngram_proposer import NgramProposer

649 elif self.speculative_config.uses_draft_model():
650 self.drafter = DraftModelProposer(

652 device=self.device,
653 runner=self,
654 )
655 elif self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu():
656 self.drafter = NgramProposerGPU(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
657 self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu = torch.zeros(
658 self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, device=device
659 )

662 self.max_model_len,
663 dtype=torch.int32,

667 self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.long, pin_memory=True
668 )

670 self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, pin_memory=True
671 )
672 elif self.speculative_config.use_gemma4_mtp():
673 self.drafter = Gemma4Proposer(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
674 elif self.speculative_config.use_step3p5_mtp():
675 self.drafter = Step3p5MTPProposer(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
676 elif self.speculative_config.use_dflash():
677 self.drafter = DFlashProposer(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)

679 elif self.speculative_config.method == "suffix":

681 elif self.speculative_config.use_eagle():

729 # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18298, due to some unknown
730 # reasons, we have to initialize the input batch before `load_model`,
731 # quantization + weight offloading will fail otherwise. As a temporary

884 # - arange_np: immutable [0, 1, 2, ...] used as source for batched computation
885 # - query_pos: CpuGpuBuffer for the computed batched arange result

901 self.max_num_tokens, dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device
902 )
903
904 self.uniform_decode_query_len = 1 + self.num_spec_tokens

998 def update_max_model_len(self, max_model_len: int) -> None:
999 self.max_model_len = max_model_len
1000 if self.speculative_config:
1001 draft_config = self.speculative_config.draft_model_config

1005 def reset_mm_cache(self) -> None:
1006 """
1007 Clear the multi-modal cache that was used during profiling,
1008 but no longer needed during inference.
1009 """
1010 if self.mm_budget:
1011 self.mm_budget.reset_cache()
1012 self.late_interaction_runner.clear()
1013
1014 def reset_encoder_cache(self) -> None:
1015 """Clear the GPU-side encoder cache storing vision embeddings.
1016
1017 This should be called when model weights are updated to ensure
1018 stale embeddings computed with old weights are not reused.
1019 """
1020 self.encoder_cache.clear()
1021 self.late_interaction_runner.clear()

1023 def _get_positions(self, num_tokens: Any):
1024 if isinstance(num_tokens, int):
1025 if self.uses_mrope:
1026 return self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_tokens]
1027 return self.positions[:num_tokens]
1028 else:
1029 if self.uses_mrope:
1030 return self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, num_tokens]
1031 return self.positions[num_tokens]
1032
1033 def _make_buffer(
1034 self, *size: int | torch.SymInt, dtype: torch.dtype, numpy: bool = True
1035 ) -> CpuGpuBuffer:
1036 return CpuGpuBuffer(
1037 *size,
1038 dtype=dtype,
1039 device=self.device,
1040 with_numpy=numpy,
1041 )
1042
1043 def _get_mamba_state_copy_funcs(self) -> MambaStateCopyFuncsByType:
1044 if self._mamba_state_copy_funcs is None:
1045 mamba_groups = mamba_utils.get_mamba_groups(self.kv_cache_config)
1046 mamba_types = {spec.mamba_type for spec in mamba_groups}
1047 copy_funcs = self.model.get_mamba_state_copy_funcs(mamba_types)
1048 mamba_utils.validate_mamba_state_copy_funcs(mamba_groups, copy_funcs)
1049 self._mamba_state_copy_funcs = copy_funcs
1050 return self._mamba_state_copy_funcs
1051
1052 def _get_mamba_bufs(self) -> mamba_utils.MambaBuffers:
1053 # Only reachable on the ``mamba_cache_mode == "align"`` path.
1054 # The postprocess sub-object is additionally gated on spec
1055 # decode + hybrid model.
1056 assert self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "align"
1057 if self._mamba_bufs is None:
1058 self._mamba_bufs = mamba_utils.MambaBuffers.create(
1059 max_num_reqs=self.max_num_reqs,
1060 kv_cache_config=self.kv_cache_config,
1061 copy_funcs=self._get_mamba_state_copy_funcs(),
1062 make_buffer=self._make_buffer,
1063 device=self.device,
1064 with_postprocess_align=(
1065 self.speculative_config is not None and self.model_config.is_hybrid
1066 ),
1067 )
1068 return self._mamba_bufs
1069
1070 def _prepare_lookback_token_ids(self, num_reqs: int) -> torch.Tensor:
1071 """Gather, per request, the `depth` prompt token ids preceding its
1072 first scheduled token (column j is position start - 1 - j); -1 where
1073 the position is before the prompt or already past it. Generated
1074 positions are left to the model: under async scheduling the CPU token
1075 table holds placeholders for them."""
1076 buf = self.lookback_token_ids
1077 assert buf is not None
1078 buf.np.fill(-1)
1079 if num_reqs > 0:
1080 depth = buf.np.shape[1]
1081 starts = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs, None]
1082 pos = starts - np.arange(1, depth + 1)
1083 num_prompt = self.input_batch.num_prompt_tokens[:num_reqs, None]
1084 valid = (pos >= 0) & (pos < num_prompt)
1085 rows = np.arange(num_reqs)[:, None]
1086 ids = self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu[rows, np.clip(pos, 0, None)]
1087 buf.np[:num_reqs] = np.where(valid, ids, -1)
1088 return buf.copy_to_gpu()
1089
1090 def _init_model_kwargs(self, num_reqs: int | None = None):
1091 model_kwargs = dict[str, Any]()
1092
1093 if self.lookback_token_ids is not None:
1094 if num_reqs is None:
1095 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
1096 model_kwargs["lookback_token_ids"] = self._prepare_lookback_token_ids(
1097 num_reqs
1098 )
1099
1100 if not self.is_pooling_model:
1101 return model_kwargs
1102
1103 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
1104 pooling_params = self.input_batch.get_pooling_params()
1105
1106 token_type_id_requests = dict[int, Any]()
1107 for i, param in enumerate(pooling_params):
1108 if (
1109 param.extra_kwargs is not None
1110 and (token_types := param.extra_kwargs.get("compressed_token_type_ids"))
1111 is not None
1112 ):
1113 token_type_id_requests[i] = token_types
1114
1115 if len(token_type_id_requests) == 0:
1116 return model_kwargs
1117
1118 # Build ids on CPU using the CPU-resident upper bound for seq_lens;
1119 # `torch.arange(seq_lens[i])` with a GPU scalar would force a sync.
1120 seq_lens_cpu = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs].tolist()
1121 token_type_ids = []
1122
1123 for i in range(num_reqs):
1124 seq_len_i = seq_lens_cpu[i]
1125 pos = token_type_id_requests.get(i, seq_len_i)
1126 ids = (torch.arange(seq_len_i) >= pos).int()
1127 token_type_ids.append(ids)
1128
1129 token_type_ids_cpu = torch.empty(
1130 sum(seq_lens_cpu), dtype=torch.int32, pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY
1131 )
1132 torch.cat(token_type_ids, out=token_type_ids_cpu)
1133 model_kwargs["token_type_ids"] = token_type_ids_cpu.to(
1134 device=self.device, non_blocking=True
1135 )
1136 return model_kwargs
1137
1138 def _may_reorder_batch(self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput") -> None:
1139 """
1140 Update the order of requests in the batch based on the attention
1141 backend's needs. For example, some attention backends (namely MLA) may
1142 want to separate requests based on if the attention computation will be
1143 compute-bound or memory-bound.
1144
1145 Args:
1146 scheduler_output: The scheduler output.
1147 """
1148 # Attention free models have zero kv_cache_groups, however models
1149 # like Mamba are also attention free but use the kv_cache for
1150 # keeping its internal state. This is why we check the number
1151 # of kv_cache groups instead of solely checking
1152 # for self.model_config.is_attention_free.
1153 if len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups) == 0:
1154 return
1155
1156 if self.reorder_batch_threshold is not None:
1157 reorder_batch_to_split_decodes_and_prefills(
1158 self.input_batch,
1159 scheduler_output,
1160 decode_threshold=self.reorder_batch_threshold,
1161 )
1162
1163 def _init_kv_zero_meta(self) -> None:
1164 """One-time precomputation for _zero_block_ids.
1165
1166 Called from gpu_worker.py outside the CuMem pool context.
1167 """
1168 self._kv_block_zeroer = KVBlockZeroer(
1169 self.device,
1170 attn_groups_iter=self._kv_cache_spec_attn_group_iterator(),
1171 kernel_block_sizes=self._kernel_block_sizes,
1172 runner_only_attn_layers=self.runner_only_attn_layers,
1173 static_forward_context=self.compilation_config.static_forward_context,
1174 num_blocks=self.kv_cache_config.num_blocks,
1175 )
1176
1177 def _zero_block_ids(self, block_ids: list[int]) -> None:
1178 """Zero the KV cache memory for the given block IDs."""
1179 if hasattr(self, "_kv_block_zeroer"):
1180 self._kv_block_zeroer.zero_block_ids(block_ids)
1181
1182 # Note: used for model runner override.
1183 def _init_device_properties(self) -> None:
1184 """Initialize attributes from torch.cuda.get_device_properties"""
1185
1186 self.num_sms = num_compute_units(self.device.index)
1187
1188 # Note: used for model runner override.
1189 def _sync_device(self) -> None:
1190 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
1191
1192 def _get_or_create_async_output_copy_stream(self) -> torch.cuda.Stream:
1193 stream = self.async_output_copy_stream
1194 if stream is None:
1195 stream = torch.cuda.Stream()
1196 self.async_output_copy_stream = stream
1197 return stream
1198
1199 def _on_request_state_removed(
1200 self,
1201 req_id: str,
1202 req_state: CachedRequestState | None,
1203 ) -> None:
1204 """Hook for platform runners to clean request-scoped side caches."""
1205 del req_id, req_state
1206
1207 def _process_encoder_cache_scheduler_output(
1208 self,
1209 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
1210 ) -> None:
1211 """Apply scheduler-side encoder cache lifecycle updates."""
1212 for mm_hash in scheduler_output.free_encoder_mm_hashes:
1213 self.encoder_cache.pop(mm_hash, None)
1214
1215 def _update_states(self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput") -> Callable | None:
1216 """Update the cached states and the persistent batch with the scheduler
1217 output.
1218
1219 The updated states are used by the `_prepare_inputs` function to create
1220 the input GPU tensors for the model.
1221
1222 The SamplingMetadata is updated and copied to the GPU if there is a
1223 new/resumed/paused/finished request in the batch.
1224 """
1225 # Remove finished requests from the cached states.
1226 for req_id in scheduler_output.finished_req_ids:
1227 req_state = self.requests.pop(req_id, None)
1228 self._on_request_state_removed(req_id, req_state)
1229 self.num_prompt_logprobs.pop(req_id, None)
1230 self.late_interaction_runner.on_requests_finished(
1231 scheduler_output.finished_req_ids
1232 )
1233 # Remove the finished requests from the persistent batch.
1234 # NOTE(woosuk): There could be an edge case where finished_req_ids and
1235 # scheduled_req_ids overlap. This happens when a request is aborted and
1236 # then resubmitted with the same ID. In this case, we treat them as two
1237 # distinct requests - clearing the cached states for the first request
1238 # and handling the second as a new request.
1239 for req_id in scheduler_output.finished_req_ids:
1240 self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)
1241
1242 # Zero GPU memory for freshly allocated cache blocks to prevent
1243 # stale NaN/data from corrupting attention or SSM computation.
1244 if scheduler_output.new_block_ids_to_zero:
1245 self._zero_block_ids(scheduler_output.new_block_ids_to_zero)
1246 if scheduler_output.kv_cache_block_copies:
1247 copy_kv_cache_blocks_inplace(
1248 self.kv_caches,
1249 self.kv_cache_config.num_blocks,
1250 scheduler_output.kv_cache_block_copies,
1251 )
1252
1253 # Free the cached encoder outputs.
1254 self._process_encoder_cache_scheduler_output(scheduler_output)
1255
1256 # Remove the unscheduled requests from the persistent batch.
1257 # NOTE(woosuk): The unscheduled requests are either preempted requests
1258 # or running requests that are not scheduled in this step. We remove
1259 # them from the persistent batch but keep their cached states since
1260 # they will be scheduled again sometime in the future.
1261 scheduled_req_ids = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens.keys()
1262 cached_req_ids = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.keys()
1263 resumed_req_ids = scheduler_output.scheduled_cached_reqs.resumed_req_ids
1264 # NOTE(zhuohan): cached_req_ids and resumed_req_ids are usually disjoint,
1265 # so `(scheduled_req_ids - resumed_req_ids) == scheduled_req_ids` holds
1266 # apart from the forced-preemption case in reset_prefix_cache. And in
1267 # that case we include the resumed_req_ids in the unscheduled set so
1268 # that they get cleared from the persistent batch before being re-scheduled
1269 # in the normal resumed request path.
1270 unscheduled_req_ids = cached_req_ids - (scheduled_req_ids - resumed_req_ids)
1271 # NOTE(woosuk): The persistent batch optimization assumes that
1272 # consecutive batches contain mostly the same requests. If batches
1273 # have low request overlap (e.g., alternating between two distinct
1274 # sets of requests), this optimization becomes very inefficient.
1275 for req_id in unscheduled_req_ids:
1276 self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)
1277
1278 is_ngram_gpu = (
1279 self.speculative_config is not None
1280 and self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu()
1281 )
1282 if is_ngram_gpu:
1283 ngram_gpu_new_reqs: list[CachedRequestState] = []
1284
1285 reqs_to_add: list[CachedRequestState] = []
1286 deferred_spec_decode_corrections = []
1287
1288 # Add new requests to the cached states.
1289 for new_req_data in scheduler_output.scheduled_new_reqs:
1290 req_id = new_req_data.req_id
1291 if req_id in self.requests:
1292 # For streaming case only.
1293 req_state = self._update_streaming_request(req_id, new_req_data)
1294 reqs_to_add.append(req_state)
1295 continue
1296
1297 sampling_params = new_req_data.sampling_params
1298 pooling_params = new_req_data.pooling_params
1299
1300 if (
1301 sampling_params
1302 and sampling_params.sampling_type == SamplingType.RANDOM_SEED
1303 ):
1304 generator = torch.Generator(device=self.device)
1305 generator.manual_seed(sampling_params.seed)
1306 else:
1307 generator = None
1308
1309 if self.is_pooling_model:
1310 assert pooling_params is not None
1311 task = pooling_params.task
1312 assert task is not None, "You did not set `task` in the API"
1313
1314 model = cast(VllmModelForPooling, self.get_model())
1315 to_update = model.pooler.get_pooling_updates(task)
1316 to_update.apply(pooling_params)
1317
1318 req_state = CachedRequestState(
1319 req_id=req_id,
1320 prompt_token_ids=new_req_data.prompt_token_ids,
1321 prompt_embeds=new_req_data.prompt_embeds,
1322 prompt_is_token_ids=new_req_data.prompt_is_token_ids,
1323 mm_features=new_req_data.mm_features,
1324 sampling_params=sampling_params,
1325 pooling_params=pooling_params,
1326 generator=generator,
1327 block_ids=new_req_data.block_ids,
1328 num_computed_tokens=new_req_data.num_computed_tokens,
1329 output_token_ids=[],
1330 lora_request=new_req_data.lora_request,
1331 )
1332 self.requests[req_id] = req_state
1333 self.late_interaction_runner.register_request(req_id, pooling_params)
1334
1335 if sampling_params and sampling_params.prompt_logprobs is not None:
1336 self.num_prompt_logprobs[req_id] = (
1337 self.input_batch.vocab_size
1338 if sampling_params.prompt_logprobs == -1
1339 else sampling_params.prompt_logprobs
1340 )
1341
1342 # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)
1343 if self.uses_mrope:
1344 self._init_mrope_positions(req_state)
1345
1346 reqs_to_add.append(req_state)
1347 # Track new requests for ngram_gpu full tensor copy
1348 if is_ngram_gpu:
1349 ngram_gpu_new_reqs.append(req_state)
1350
1351 # Update the states of the running/resumed requests.
1352 is_last_rank = get_pp_group().is_last_rank
1353 req_data = scheduler_output.scheduled_cached_reqs
1354 scheduled_spec_tokens = scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens
1355
1356 # Save scheduler-allocated spec lengths before trimming so
1357 # prev_num_draft_len keeps the optimistic count for rejection correction.
1358 original_num_spec_per_req: dict[str, int] = {}
1359 if (
1360 self.speculative_config is not None
1361 and self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu()
1362 ):
1363 for req_id, toks in scheduled_spec_tokens.items():
1364 original_num_spec_per_req[req_id] = len(toks)
1365 update_scheduler_for_invalid_drafts(
1366 self._num_valid_draft_tokens_event,
1367 self._num_valid_draft_tokens_cpu,
1368 scheduler_output,
1369 self.input_batch.req_id_to_index,
1370 )
1371 if self.use_async_spec_decode:
1372 self.prev_num_draft_tokens.np.fill(0)
1373
1374 for i, req_id in enumerate(req_data.req_ids):
1375 req_state = self.requests[req_id]
1376 num_computed_tokens = req_data.num_computed_tokens[i]
1377 new_block_ids = req_data.new_block_ids[i]
1378 resumed_from_preemption = req_id in req_data.resumed_req_ids
1379 num_output_tokens = req_data.num_output_tokens[i]
1380 req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1381
1382 if req_state.prev_num_draft_len and self.use_async_scheduling:
1383 # prev_num_draft_len is used in async scheduling mode with
1384 # spec decode. it indicates if need to update num_computed_tokens
1385 # of the request. for example:
1386 # first step: num_computed_tokens = 0, spec_tokens = [],
1387 # prev_num_draft_len = 0.
1388 # second step: num_computed_tokens = 100(prompt length),
1389 # spec_tokens = [a,b], prev_num_draft_len = 0.
1390 # third step: num_computed_tokens = 100 + 2, spec_tokens = [c,d],
1391 # prev_num_draft_len = 2.
1392 # num_computed_tokens in first step and second step doesn't contain
1393 # the spec tokens length, but in third step it contains the
1394 # spec tokens length. we only need to update num_computed_tokens
1395 # when prev_num_draft_len > 0.
1396 if req_index is None:
1397 req_state.prev_num_draft_len = 0
1398 else:
1399 # Optimistically assume all accepted; queue up a correction
1400 # to be called after the model forward to preserve async
1401 # scheduling. Corrected on GPU in _prepare_inputs.
1402 optimistic_num_accepted = req_state.prev_num_draft_len
1403 req_state.output_token_ids.extend([-1] * optimistic_num_accepted)
1404
1405 deferred_spec_decode_corrections.append(
1406 (req_id, optimistic_num_accepted, req_state)
1407 )
1408
1409 prev_req_index = (
1410 self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1411 if self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
1412 else None
1413 )
1414 if prev_req_index is not None:
1415 self.prev_num_draft_tokens.np[prev_req_index] = (
1416 optimistic_num_accepted
1417 )
1418
1419 if is_ngram_gpu and optimistic_num_accepted > 0:
1420 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index] += (
1421 optimistic_num_accepted
1422 )
1423
1424 # Update the cached states.
1425 req_state.num_computed_tokens = num_computed_tokens
1426
1427 if not is_last_rank:
1428 if not req_data.new_token_ids:
1429 # Async scheduled PP: Sampled tokens propagated via GPU broadcast.
1430 new_token_ids: list[int] = []
1431 else:
1432 # Non-async scheduling with PP: The scheduler sends
1433 # sampled token ids back because there's no direct communication
1434 # between the first-stage worker and the last-stage worker.
1435 new_token_ids = req_data.new_token_ids[i]
1436 # Add the sampled token(s) from the previous step (if any).
1437 # This doesn't include "unverified" tokens like spec tokens.
1438 num_new_tokens = (
1439 num_computed_tokens + len(new_token_ids) - req_state.num_tokens
1440 )
1441 if num_new_tokens == 1:
1442 # Avoid slicing list in most common case.
1443 req_state.output_token_ids.append(new_token_ids[-1])
1444 elif num_new_tokens > 0:
1445 req_state.output_token_ids.extend(
1446 new_token_ids[-num_new_tokens:]
1447 )
1448 elif num_output_tokens < len(req_state.output_token_ids):
1449 # Some output tokens were discarded due to a sync-KV-load
1450 # failure, or output_token_ids was inflated by the optimistic
1451 # extend above (async spec decode). Align the cached state.
1452 del req_state.output_token_ids[num_output_tokens:]
1453 if req_index is not None:
1454 end_idx = (
1455 self.input_batch.num_prompt_tokens[req_index]
1456 + num_output_tokens
1457 )
1458 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index] = end_idx
1459
1460 # Update the block IDs.
1461 if not resumed_from_preemption:
1462 if new_block_ids is not None:
1463 # Append the new blocks to the existing block IDs.
1464 for block_ids, new_ids in zip(req_state.block_ids, new_block_ids):
1465 block_ids.extend(new_ids)
1466 else:
1467 assert req_index is None
1468 assert new_block_ids is not None
1469 # The request is resumed from preemption.
1470 # Replace the existing block IDs with the new ones.
1471 req_state.block_ids = new_block_ids
1472
1473 if req_index is None:
1474 # The request is not in the persistent batch.
1475 # The request was either preempted and resumed later, or was not
1476 # scheduled in the previous step and needs to be added again.
1477
1478 if self.use_async_scheduling and num_output_tokens > 0:
1479 # We must recover the output token ids for resumed requests in the
1480 # async scheduling case, so that correct input_ids are obtained.
1481 resumed_token_ids = req_data.all_token_ids[req_id]
1482 req_state.output_token_ids = resumed_token_ids[-num_output_tokens:]
1483
1484 reqs_to_add.append(req_state)
1485 # Track resumed requests for ngram_gpu full tensor copy
1486 if is_ngram_gpu:
1487 ngram_gpu_new_reqs.append(req_state)
1488 continue
1489
1490 # Update the persistent batch.
1491 self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_index] = num_computed_tokens
1492 if new_block_ids is not None:
1493 self.input_batch.block_table.append_row(new_block_ids, req_index)
1494
1495 # For the last rank, we don't need to update the token_ids_cpu
1496 # because the sampled tokens are already cached.
1497 if not is_last_rank:
1498 start_token_index = self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index]
1499 # For chunked prefill, num_computed_tokens may less
1500 # than num_tokens_no_spec.
1501 # Async scheduled PP: no new_token_ids, advance num_tokens_no_spec
1502 # according to num_computed_tokens.
1503 end_token_index = max(
1504 start_token_index,
1505 num_computed_tokens + len(new_token_ids),
1506 )
1507 if end_token_index > start_token_index:
1508 if new_token_ids:
1509 # Add new_token_ids to token_ids_cpu.
1510 num_new_tokens = end_token_index - start_token_index
1511 tokens_to_append = new_token_ids[-num_new_tokens:]
1512 self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu[
1513 req_index, start_token_index:end_token_index
1514 ] = tokens_to_append
1515 self.input_batch.is_token_ids[
1516 req_index, start_token_index:end_token_index
1517 ] = True
1518 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index] = end_token_index
1519
1520 # Add spec_token_ids to token_ids_cpu.
1521 self.input_batch.update_req_spec_token_ids(req_state, scheduled_spec_tokens)
1522 # Restore scheduler-side draft count after ngram trimming.
1523 if original_num_spec_per_req:
1524 orig = original_num_spec_per_req.get(req_id, 0)
1525 if orig != req_state.prev_num_draft_len:
1526 req_state.prev_num_draft_len = orig
1527
1528 # Add the new or resumed requests to the persistent batch.
1529 # The smaller empty indices are filled first.
1530 for request in reqs_to_add:
1531 self.input_batch.add_request(request)
1532 self.input_batch.update_req_spec_token_ids(request, scheduled_spec_tokens)
1533
1534 # Condense the batched states if there are gaps left by removed requests
1535 self.input_batch.condense()
1536 # Allow attention backend to reorder the batch, potentially
1537 self._may_reorder_batch(scheduler_output)
1538 # Refresh batch metadata with any pending updates.
1539 self.input_batch.refresh_metadata()
1540
1541 # Incrementally update ngram_gpu tensors after batch is stable
1542 if is_ngram_gpu:
1543 update_ngram_gpu_tensors_incremental(
1544 self.input_batch,
1545 self.token_ids_gpu_tensor,
1546 self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu,
1547 ngram_gpu_new_reqs,
1548 self.device,
1549 _pinned_idx_buf=self._ngram_pinned_idx_buf,
1550 _pinned_val_buf=self._ngram_pinned_val_buf,
1551 )
1552
1553 if deferred_spec_decode_corrections:
1554
1555 def correct_spec_decode_token_counts():
1556 valid_sampled_token_count = self._get_valid_sampled_token_count()
1557 if not valid_sampled_token_count:
1558 return
1559 prev_req_id_to_index = self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
1560 if not prev_req_id_to_index:
1561 return
1562 for (
1563 req_id,
1564 optimistic_num_accepted,
1565 req_state,
1566 ) in deferred_spec_decode_corrections:
1567 prev_req_index = prev_req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1568 if prev_req_index is None:
1569 continue
1570 num_accepted = valid_sampled_token_count[prev_req_index] - 1
1571 correction = optimistic_num_accepted - num_accepted
1572 req_state.num_computed_tokens -= correction
1573 cur_req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1574 if cur_req_index is None:
1575 continue
1576 self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[cur_req_index] -= (
1577 correction
1578 )
1579 if is_ngram_gpu and correction > 0:
1580 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[cur_req_index] -= correction
1581 self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu[cur_req_index] -= correction
1582
1583 return correct_spec_decode_token_counts
1584 else:
1585 return None
1586
1587 def _update_states_after_model_execute(
1588 self, output_token_ids: torch.Tensor, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"
1589 ) -> None:
1590 """Update the cached states after model execution.
1591
1592 This is used for MTP/EAGLE for hybrid models, as in linear attention,
1593 only the last token's state is kept. In MTP/EAGLE, for draft tokens
1594 the state are kept util we decide how many tokens are accepted for
1595 each sequence, and a shifting is done during the next iteration
1596 based on the number of accepted tokens.
1597 """
1598 if not self.speculative_config or not self.model_config.is_hybrid:
1599 return
1600
1601 # Count the number of accepted tokens for each sequence.
1602 # Valid tokens are contiguous from position 0, so counting non-(-1)
1603 # tokens gives us the first -1 position (i.e., number of accepted).
1604 num_reqs = output_token_ids.size(0)
1605 self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs] = (output_token_ids != -1).sum(dim=1)
1606
1607 if self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "align":
1608 # Fused GPU postprocess: state copies + per-request accepted-token
1609 # update without CPU-GPU sync. The metadata
1610 # (num_scheduled_tokens, num_draft_tokens, num_computed_tokens) is
1611 # pre-staged to GPU buffers in _prepare_inputs.
1612 mamba_utils.postprocess_mamba_align_gpu(
1613 bufs=self._get_mamba_bufs(),
1614 num_reqs=num_reqs,
1615 num_accepted_tokens_gpu=self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu,
1616 num_accepted_tokens_cpu_tensor=(
1617 self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu_tensor
1618 ),
1619 input_batch=self.input_batch,
1620 kv_cache_config=self.kv_cache_config,
1621 forward_context=self.compilation_config.static_forward_context,
1622 mamba_state_copy_funcs=self._get_mamba_state_copy_funcs(),
1623 )
1624
1625 assert self.num_accepted_tokens_event is not None
1626 self.num_accepted_tokens_event.record()
1627 else:
1628 self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs].copy_(
1629 self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs], non_blocking=True
1630 )
1631 assert self.num_accepted_tokens_event is not None
1632 self.num_accepted_tokens_event.record()
1633
1634 if self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "all":
1635 mamba_utils.postprocess_mamba_all(
1636 scheduler_output,
1637 self.kv_cache_config,
1638 self.input_batch,
1639 self.requests,
1640 self.mamba_state_idx,
1641 self.num_spec_tokens,
1642 num_reqs,
1643 )
1644
1645 def _update_streaming_request(
1646 self, req_id: str, new_req_data: NewRequestData
1647 ) -> CachedRequestState:
1648 """Updates streaming session request from `scheduled_new_reqs`.
1649
1650 Removes the request from InputBatch (if present), updates the cached
1651 state, and prepares it for re-addition to the batch.
1652
1653 NOTE: prompt_token_ids includes intermediate output tokens - tokens
1654 previously generated but now are input context (part of the prompt).
1655 """
1656 self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)
1657 req_state = self.requests[req_id]
1658
1659 req_state.prompt_token_ids = new_req_data.prompt_token_ids
1660 req_state.mm_features = new_req_data.mm_features
1661 req_state.prompt_embeds = new_req_data.prompt_embeds
1662 req_state.sampling_params = new_req_data.sampling_params
1663 req_state.pooling_params = new_req_data.pooling_params
1664 self.late_interaction_runner.register_request(req_id, req_state.pooling_params)
1665 req_state.block_ids = new_req_data.block_ids
1666 req_state.num_computed_tokens = new_req_data.num_computed_tokens
1667 req_state.num_prompt_tokens = length_from_prompt_token_ids_or_embeds(
1668 req_state.prompt_token_ids, req_state.prompt_embeds
1669 )
1670
1671 # Clear `output_token_ids` as previous output tokens are now part of
1672 # `prompt_token_ids`.
1673 req_state.output_token_ids.clear()
1674
1675 if self.uses_mrope:
1676 self._init_mrope_positions(req_state)
1677
1678 return req_state
1679
1680 def _init_mrope_positions(self, req_state: CachedRequestState):
1681 model = self.get_model()
1682 assert supports_mrope(model), "M-RoPE support is not implemented."
1683 mrope_model = cast(SupportsMRoPE, model)
1684
1685 # `prompt_embeds` is a passthrough modality (no grid_thw), models'
1686 # M-RoPE code assumes per-feature grid info, so filter it out. The
1687 # prompt_embeds positions are treated as text positions for M-RoPE.
1688 mrope_features = [
1689 f for f in req_state.mm_features if f.modality != "prompt_embeds"
1690 ]
1691
1692 if req_state.prompt_token_ids is not None:
1693 input_tokens = req_state.prompt_token_ids
1694 elif req_state.prompt_embeds is not None:
1695 # For embeddings-only inputs, get_mrope_input_positions only
1696 # needs the sequence length when mm_features is empty (which is
1697 # the case here since prompt_embeds are filtered out above).
1698 seq_len = req_state.prompt_embeds.shape[0]
1699 input_tokens = list(range(seq_len))
1700 else:
1701 raise ValueError(
1702 "M-RoPE requires either prompt_token_ids or prompt_embeds."
1703 )
1704
1705 req_state.mrope_positions, req_state.mrope_position_delta = (
1706 mrope_model.get_mrope_input_positions(
1707 input_tokens,
1708 mrope_features,
1709 )
1710 )
1711
1712 def _extract_mm_kwargs(
1713 self,
1714 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
1715 ) -> BatchedTensorInputs:
1716 if not scheduler_output or not self.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model:
1717 return {}
1718
1719 mm_kwargs = list[tuple[str, MultiModalKwargsItem]]()
1720 for req in scheduler_output.scheduled_new_reqs:
1721 for feature in req.mm_features:
1722 if feature.data is not None:
1723 mm_kwargs.append((feature.modality, feature.data))
1724
1725 # Input all modalities at once
1726 mm_kwargs_combined: BatchedTensorInputs = {}
1727 for _, _, mm_kwargs_batch in group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
1728 mm_kwargs,
1729 device=self.device,
1730 pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY,
1731 ):
1732 mm_kwargs_combined.update(mm_kwargs_batch)
1733
1734 return mm_kwargs_combined
1735
1736 def _dummy_mm_kwargs(self, num_seqs: int) -> BatchedTensorInputs:
1737 if not self.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model:
1738 return {}
1739
1740 mm_budget = self.mm_budget
1741 assert mm_budget is not None
1742
1743 if not mm_budget.mm_max_toks_per_item:
1744 return {} # No tower modalities (embed-only mode)
1745
1746 dummy_modality = mm_budget.get_modality_with_max_tokens()
1747 return self._get_mm_dummy_batch(dummy_modality, num_seqs)
1748
1749 def _get_cumsum_and_arange(
1750 self,
1751 num_tokens: np.ndarray,
1752 arange_out: np.ndarray,
1753 cumsum_dtype: np.dtype | None = None,
1754 ) -> np.ndarray:
1755 """Get the cumulative sum and batched arange of the given array.
1756 E.g., [2, 5, 3] -> [2, 7, 10], arange written to
1757 arange_out[:10] as [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2].
1758 Equivalent to but faster than:
1759 np.concatenate([np.arange(n) for n in num_tokens])
1760 """
1761 # Step 1. [2, 5, 3] -> [2, 7, 10]
1762 cu_num_tokens = np.cumsum(num_tokens, dtype=cumsum_dtype)
1763 total_num_tokens = cu_num_tokens[-1]
1764 # Step 2. [2, 7, 10] -> [0, 0, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 7, 7, 7]
1765 cumsums_offsets = np.repeat(cu_num_tokens - num_tokens, num_tokens)
1766 # Step 3. [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]
1767 np.subtract(
1768 self.arange_np[:total_num_tokens],
1769 cumsums_offsets,
1770 out=arange_out[:total_num_tokens],
1771 )
1772
1773 return cu_num_tokens
1774
1775 def _compute_prev_positions(self, num_reqs: int) -> None:
1776 """Build prev_positions mapping: current pos -> previous pos (-1 if new).
1777
1778 Populates self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs] with the mapping.
1779 """
1780 prev_req_id_to_index = self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
1781 prev_positions = self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs]
1782
1783 if not prev_req_id_to_index:
1784 prev_positions.fill(-1)
1785 return
1786
1787 for i, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids[:num_reqs]):
1788 prev_positions[i] = prev_req_id_to_index.get(req_id, -1)
1789
1790 def _prepare_input_ids(
1791 self,
1792 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
1793 num_reqs: int,
1794 total_num_scheduled_tokens: int,
1795 cu_num_tokens: np.ndarray,
1796 ) -> None:
1797 """Prepare the input IDs for the current batch.
1798
1799 Carefully handles the `prev_sampled_token_ids` which can be cached
1800 from the previous engine iteration, in which case those tokens on the
1801 GPU need to be copied into the corresponding slots into input_ids.
1802
1803 Uses self.prev_positions[:num_reqs] which maps current pos -> prev pos
1804 (-1 for new requests).
1805 """
1806
1807 # Sync the async PP broadcast before reading sampled tokens.
1808 if self._pp_recv_work is not None:
1809 self._pp_recv_work.wait()
1810 self._pp_recv_work = None
1811
1812 if self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids is None:
1813 # Normal scheduling case
1814 self.input_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1815 if self.enable_prompt_embeds:
1816 self.inputs_embeds.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1817 self.is_token_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1818 return
1819
1820 # Async scheduling case, where some decode requests from the previous
1821 # iteration won't have entries in input_ids_cpu and need to be copied
1822 # on the GPU from prev_sampled_token_ids.
1823 prev_positions = self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs]
1824 scheduled_spec_tokens = scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens
1825 sample_flattened_indices: list[int] = []
1826 spec_flattened_indices: list[int] = []
1827 prev_draft_token_indices: list[int] = []
1828 prev_indices: list[int] = []
1829 common_indices_match = True
1830 max_flattened_index = -1
1831 total_num_spec_tokens = 0
1832
1833 for cur_index in range(num_reqs):
1834 prev_index = prev_positions[cur_index]
1835 if prev_index < 0:
1836 continue
1837 prev_indices.append(prev_index)
1838 req_id = self.input_batch.req_ids[cur_index]
1839 # We need to compute the flattened input_ids index of the
1840 # last token in each common request.
1841 draft_len = len(scheduled_spec_tokens.get(req_id, ()))
1842 total_num_spec_tokens += draft_len
1843 flattened_index = cu_num_tokens[cur_index].item() - 1
1844 # example: cu_num_tokens = [2, 5, 8], draft_tokens = [1, 2, 2]
1845 # sample_flattened_indices = [0, 2, 5]
1846 # spec_flattened_indices = [1, 3, 4, 6, 7]
1847 sample_flattened_indices.append(flattened_index - draft_len)
1848 spec_flattened_indices.extend(
1849 range(flattened_index - draft_len + 1, flattened_index + 1)
1850 )
1851 start = prev_index * self.prev_num_spec_tokens
1852 # prev_draft_token_indices is used to find which draft_tokens_id
1853 # should be copied to input_ids
1854 # example: prev draft_tokens_id [[1,2], [3,4], [5, 6]]
1855 # flatten draft_tokens_id [1,2,3,4,5,6]
1856 # draft_len of each request [1, 2, 1]
1857 # then prev_draft_token_indices is [0, 2, 3, 4]
1858 prev_draft_token_indices.extend(range(start, start + draft_len))
1859 common_indices_match &= prev_index == flattened_index
1860 max_flattened_index = max(max_flattened_index, flattened_index)
1861
1862 num_common_tokens = len(sample_flattened_indices)
1863 total_without_spec = total_num_scheduled_tokens - total_num_spec_tokens
1864 if self.enable_prompt_embeds:
1865 # The multimodal embed path reads is_token_ids.gpu; its .cpu copy is
1866 # refreshed every step but the async fast paths below only scatter
1867 # input_ids.gpu, so refresh is_token_ids.gpu here too.
1868 self.is_token_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1869 if num_common_tokens < total_without_spec:
1870 # If not all requests are decodes from the last iteration,
1871 # we need to copy the input_ids_cpu to the GPU first.
1872 self.input_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1873 if self.enable_prompt_embeds:
1874 self.inputs_embeds.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1875 if num_common_tokens == 0:
1876 # No requests in common with the previous iteration
1877 # So input_ids.cpu will have all the input ids.
1878 return
1879 if common_indices_match and max_flattened_index == (num_common_tokens - 1):
1880 # Common-case optimization: the batch is unchanged
1881 # and no reordering happened.
1882 # The indices are both the same permutation of 0..N-1 so
1883 # we can copy directly using a single slice.
1884 self.input_ids.gpu[:num_common_tokens].copy_(
1885 self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids[:num_common_tokens, 0],
1886 non_blocking=True,
1887 )
1888 return
1889 # Upload the index tensors asynchronously so the scatter can be non-blocking.
1890 sampled_tokens_index_tensor = torch.tensor(
1891 sample_flattened_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY
1892 ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1893 prev_common_req_indices_tensor = torch.tensor(
1894 prev_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY
1895 ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1896 self.input_ids.gpu.scatter_(
1897 dim=0,
1898 index=sampled_tokens_index_tensor,
1899 src=self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids[
1900 prev_common_req_indices_tensor, 0
1901 ],
1902 )
1903
1904 # Scatter the draft tokens after the sampled tokens are scattered.
1905 if self._draft_token_ids is None or not spec_flattened_indices:
1906 return
1907
1908 assert isinstance(self._draft_token_ids, torch.Tensor)
1909 draft_tokens_index_tensor = torch.tensor(
1910 spec_flattened_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY
1911 ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1912 prev_draft_token_indices_tensor = torch.tensor(
1913 prev_draft_token_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY
1914 ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1915
1916 # because input_ids dtype is torch.int32,
1917 # so convert draft_token_ids to torch.int32 here.
1918 draft_token_ids = self._draft_token_ids.to(dtype=torch.int32)
1919
1920 self.input_ids.gpu.scatter_(
1921 dim=0,
1922 index=draft_tokens_index_tensor,
1923 src=draft_token_ids.flatten()[prev_draft_token_indices_tensor],
1924 )
1925
1926 def _get_encoder_seq_lens(
1927 self,
1928 num_scheduled_tokens: dict[str, int],
1929 kv_cache_spec: KVCacheSpec,
1930 num_reqs: int,
1931 for_cudagraph_capture: bool = False,
1932 ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, np.ndarray | None]:
1933 if not isinstance(kv_cache_spec, CrossAttentionSpec):
1934 return None, None
1935
1936 # Zero out buffer for padding requests that are not actually scheduled (CGs)
1937 self.encoder_seq_lens.np[:num_reqs] = 0
1938
1939 # Build encoder_seq_lens array mapping request indices to
1940 # encoder lengths for inputs scheduled in this batch
1941 for req_id in num_scheduled_tokens:
1942 req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
1943 req_state = self.requests[req_id]
1944 if req_state.mm_features is None:
1945 self.encoder_seq_lens.np[req_index] = 0
1946 continue
1947
1948 # Get the total number of encoder input tokens for running encoder requests
1949 # whether encoding is finished or not so that cross-attention knows how
1950 # many encoder tokens to attend to.
1951 encoder_input_tokens = sum(
1952 feature.mm_position.length for feature in req_state.mm_features
1953 )
1954 self.encoder_seq_lens.np[req_index] = encoder_input_tokens
1955 if for_cudagraph_capture:
1956 # During CUDA graph capture, we need to use realistic encoder lengths
1957 # so that max_seqlen_k is captured with the correct value.
1958 max_encoder_len = getattr(
1959 self.model_config.hf_config,
1960 "max_source_positions",
1961 self.max_encoder_len,
1962 )
1963 self.encoder_seq_lens.np[:num_reqs] = max_encoder_len
1964
1965 self.encoder_seq_lens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
1966 encoder_seq_lens = self.encoder_seq_lens.gpu[:num_reqs]
1967 encoder_seq_lens_cpu = self.encoder_seq_lens.np[:num_reqs]
1968
1969 return encoder_seq_lens, encoder_seq_lens_cpu
1970
1971 def _prepare_inputs(
1972 self,
1973 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
1974 num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
1975 ) -> tuple[
1976 torch.Tensor,
1977 SpecDecodeMetadata | None,
1978 int,
1979 ]:
1980 """
1981 Returns:
1982 tuple[logits_indices, spec_decode_metadata, max_num_sampled_tokens]
1983 """
1984 total_num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
1985 assert total_num_scheduled_tokens > 0
1986 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
1987 assert num_reqs > 0
1988
1989 # OPTIMIZATION: Start copying the block table first.
1990 # This way, we can overlap the copy with the following CPU operations.
1991 self.input_batch.block_table.commit_block_table(num_reqs)
1992
1993 # Get request indices.
1994 # E.g., [2, 5, 3] -> [0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 2]
1995 req_indices = np.repeat(self.arange_np[:num_reqs], num_scheduled_tokens)
1996
1997 # cu_num_tokens: [2, 5, 3] -> [2, 7, 10]
1998 # self.query_pos.np[:10]: [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]
1999 cu_num_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
2000 num_scheduled_tokens, self.query_pos.np
2001 )
2002
2003 # Get positions.
2004 positions_np = (
2005 self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_indices]
2006 + self.query_pos.np[: cu_num_tokens[-1]]
2007 )
2008
2009 # Calculate M-RoPE positions.
2010 # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)
2011 if self.uses_mrope:
2012 self._calc_mrope_positions(scheduler_output)
2013
2014 # Get token indices.
2015 # E.g., [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]
2016 # -> [0, 1, M, M + 1, M + 2, M + 3, M + 4, 2 * M, 2 * M + 1, 2 * M + 2]
2017 # where M is the max_model_len.
2018 token_indices = (
2019 positions_np + req_indices * self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu.shape[1]
2020 )
2021 token_indices_tensor = torch.from_numpy(token_indices)
2022
2023 # NOTE(woosuk): We use torch.index_select instead of np.take here
2024 # because torch.index_select is much faster than np.take for large
2025 # tensors.
2026 torch.index_select(
2027 self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu_tensor.flatten(),
2028 0,
2029 token_indices_tensor,
2030 out=self.input_ids.cpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens],
2031 )
2032 if self.enable_prompt_embeds:
2033 is_token_ids = self.input_batch.is_token_ids_tensor.flatten()
2034 torch.index_select(
2035 is_token_ids,
2036 0,
2037 token_indices_tensor,
2038 out=self.is_token_ids.cpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens],
2039 )
2040
2041 # Because we did not pre-allocate a massive prompt_embeds CPU tensor on
2042 # the InputBatch, we need to fill in the prompt embeds into the expected
2043 # spots in the GpuModelRunner's pre-allocated prompt_embeds tensor.
2044 if self.input_batch.req_prompt_embeds:
2045 output_idx = 0
2046 for req_idx in range(num_reqs):
2047 num_sched = num_scheduled_tokens[req_idx]
2048
2049 # Skip if this request doesn't have embeddings
2050 if req_idx not in self.input_batch.req_prompt_embeds:
2051 output_idx += num_sched
2052 continue
2053
2054 # Skip if no tokens scheduled
2055 if num_sched <= 0:
2056 output_idx += num_sched
2057 continue
2058
2059 req_embeds = self.input_batch.req_prompt_embeds[req_idx]
2060 start_pos = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_idx]
2061
2062 # Skip if trying to read beyond available embeddings
2063 if start_pos >= req_embeds.shape[0]:
2064 output_idx += num_sched
2065 continue
2066
2067 # Copy available embeddings
2068 end_pos = start_pos + num_sched
2069 actual_end = min(end_pos, req_embeds.shape[0])
2070 actual_num_sched = actual_end - start_pos
2071
2072 if actual_num_sched > 0:
2073 self.inputs_embeds.cpu[
2074 output_idx : output_idx + actual_num_sched
2075 ].copy_(req_embeds[start_pos:actual_end])
2076
2077 output_idx += num_sched
2078
2079 # Prepare the attention metadata.
2080 self.query_start_loc.np[0] = 0
2081 self.query_start_loc.np[1 : num_reqs + 1] = cu_num_tokens
2082 # Note: pad query_start_loc to be non-decreasing, as kernels
2083 # like FlashAttention requires that
2084 self.query_start_loc.np[num_reqs + 1 :].fill(cu_num_tokens[-1])
2085 self.query_start_loc.copy_to_gpu()
2086 query_start_loc = self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs + 1]
2087
2088 # Compute optimistic seq_lens (assumes all draft tokens from previous
2089 # iteration accepted). Store in optimistic_seq_lens_cpu for use by
2090 # _build_attention_metadata (max_seq_len) and discard_request_mask.
2091 # seq_lens (GPU) will be computed later using the same optimistic values.
2092 torch.add(
2093 self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs],
2094 torch.from_numpy(num_scheduled_tokens),
2095 out=self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs],
2096 )
2097 self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
2098
2099 # Build prev_positions mapping: current pos -> prev pos (-1 if new).
2100 # Used for gathering from previous iteration's GPU tensors.
2101 prev_req_id_to_index = self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
2102 self._compute_prev_positions(num_reqs)
2103
2104 num_tokens = [self.requests[r].num_tokens for r in self.input_batch.req_ids]
2105 num_tokens_np = np.array(num_tokens, dtype=np.int32)
2106
2107 # Record which requests should not be sampled,
2108 # so that we could clear the sampled tokens before returning
2109 self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs] = (
2110 self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs].numpy() < num_tokens_np
2111 )
2112 self.discard_request_mask.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
2113
2114 # Sync num_accepted_tokens from CPU (set by
2115 # _update_states_after_model_execute for hybrid models).
2116 # Skipped under async scheduling (non-align): the CPU copy races with
2117 # the in-flight D2H copy and with input-batch row moves.
2118 needs_cpu_accepted_counts = self.num_accepted_tokens_event is not None and not (
2119 self.use_async_scheduling and self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode != "align"
2120 )
2121 if needs_cpu_accepted_counts:
2122 assert self.num_accepted_tokens_event is not None
2123 self.num_accepted_tokens_event.synchronize()
2124 # Async mode: condense() reordered indices, use prev_positions mapping
2125 if self.use_async_scheduling and prev_req_id_to_index:
2126 prev_idx = self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs]
2127 new_mask = prev_idx < 0
2128 self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = (
2129 self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[
2130 np.where(new_mask, 0, prev_idx)
2131 ]
2132 )
2133 self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs][new_mask] = 1
2134 self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs] = (
2135 self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs]
2136 )
2137 else:
2138 # Non-async mode: use values directly
2139 self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = (
2140 self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs]
2141 )
2142 self.num_accepted_tokens.np[num_reqs:].fill(1)
2143 self.num_accepted_tokens.copy_to_gpu()
2144 else:
2145 # Default to 1; update_num_computed_tokens_for_batch_change below
2146 # corrects rows that had drafts from valid_sampled_token_count.
2147 self.num_accepted_tokens.np.fill(1)
2148 self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu.fill_(1)
2149
2150 if self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx is not None:
2151 mamba_utils.preprocess_mamba_all_specdec(
2152 scheduler_output,
2153 self.input_batch,
2154 self.mamba_state_idx,
2155 num_reqs,
2156 self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx,
2157 )
2158
2159 # Update num_computed_tokens on GPU. In async spec decode,
2160 # CPU values are optimistic (all drafts accepted). The kernel
2161 # corrects on GPU using the previous step's
2162 # valid_sampled_token_count_gpu. Otherwise, just copy from CPU.
2163 if (
2164 self.use_async_spec_decode
2165 and self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu is not None
2166 and prev_req_id_to_index
2167 ):
2168 self.prev_positions.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
2169 self.prev_num_draft_tokens.copy_to_gpu()
2170 cpu_values = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs].to(
2171 device=self.device, non_blocking=True
2172 )
2173 update_num_computed_tokens_for_batch_change(
2174 self.num_computed_tokens,
2175 self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs],
2176 self.prev_positions.gpu[:num_reqs],
2177 self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu,
2178 self.prev_num_draft_tokens.gpu,
2179 cpu_values,
2180 )
2181 else:
2182 self.num_computed_tokens[:num_reqs].copy_(
2183 self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs],
2184 non_blocking=True,
2185 )
2186
2187 self.req_indices.np[:total_num_scheduled_tokens] = req_indices
2188 self.req_indices.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
2189 req_indices_gpu = self.req_indices.gpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens]
2190
2191 self.query_pos.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
2192 self.num_scheduled_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = num_scheduled_tokens
2193 self.num_scheduled_tokens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
2194 num_scheduled_tokens_gpu = self.num_scheduled_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs]
2195 self.positions[:total_num_scheduled_tokens] = (
2196 self.num_computed_tokens[req_indices_gpu].to(torch.int64)
2197 + self.query_pos.gpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens]
2198 )
2199 self.seq_lens[:num_reqs] = (
2200 self.num_computed_tokens[:num_reqs] + num_scheduled_tokens_gpu
2201 )
2202 self.seq_lens[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
2203
2204 self.input_batch.block_table.compute_slot_mapping(
2205 num_reqs,
2206 self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs + 1],
2207 self.positions[:total_num_scheduled_tokens],
2208 )
2209
2210 # Copy the tensors to the GPU.
2211 self._prepare_input_ids(
2212 scheduler_output,
2213 num_reqs,
2214 total_num_scheduled_tokens,
2215 cu_num_tokens,
2216 )
2217
2218 if self.uses_mrope:
2219 # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)
2220 # Copy one row at a time. mrope_positions is allocated as
2221 # [num_dims, max_num_tokens + 1] with a dummy trailing column to keep it
2222 # non-contiguous for torch.compile, so cpu[:, :N] is a strided view.
2223 # copy_() cannot express a strided source as a single
2224 # cudaMemcpyAsync, so it first gathers into a contiguous *pageable*
2225 # temporary, and a pageable H2D ignores non_blocking=True and
2226 # synchronizes the stream before the transfer starts. Each row is
2227 # contiguous within the pinned allocation, so per-row copies stay on
2228 # the pinned path and are genuinely asynchronous.
2229 for row in range(self.mrope_positions.gpu.shape[0]):
2230 self.mrope_positions.gpu[row, :total_num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(
2231 self.mrope_positions.cpu[row, :total_num_scheduled_tokens],
2232 non_blocking=True,
2233 )
2234 if self.use_async_spec_decode and self.uses_mrope:
2235 drift = self.num_computed_tokens[req_indices_gpu].to(
2236 torch.int64
2237 ) - async_tensor_h2d(
2238 self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[req_indices],
2239 device=self.device,
2240 dtype=torch.int64,
2241 )
2242 self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens] += drift
2243
2244 use_spec_decode = len(scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens) > 0
2245 if not use_spec_decode:
2246 # NOTE(woosuk): Due to chunked prefills, the batch may contain
2247 # partial requests. While we should not sample any token
2248 # from these partial requests, we do so for simplicity.
2249 # We will ignore the sampled tokens from the partial requests.
2250 # TODO: Support prompt logprobs.
2251 logits_indices = query_start_loc[1:] - 1
2252 spec_decode_metadata = None
2253 num_sampled_tokens = np.ones(num_reqs, dtype=np.int32)
2254 else:
2255 # Get the number of draft tokens for each request.
2256 # Iterate over the dictionary rather than all requests since not all
2257 # requests have draft tokens.
2258 num_draft_tokens = np.zeros(num_reqs, dtype=np.int32)
2259 # For chunked prefills, use -1 as mask rather than 0, as guided
2260 # decoding may rollback speculative tokens.
2261 num_decode_draft_tokens = np.full(num_reqs, -1, dtype=np.int32)
2262 for (
2263 req_id,
2264 draft_token_ids,
2265 ) in scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens.items():
2266 req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
2267 draft_len = len(draft_token_ids)
2268 num_draft_tokens[req_idx] = draft_len
2269 if num_scheduled_tokens[req_idx] == draft_len + 1:
2270 num_decode_draft_tokens[req_idx] = draft_len
2271 spec_decode_metadata = self._calc_spec_decode_metadata(
2272 num_draft_tokens, cu_num_tokens
2273 )
2274 logits_indices = spec_decode_metadata.logits_indices
2275 num_sampled_tokens = num_draft_tokens + 1
2276 # For DECODE only cuda graph of some attention backends (e.g., GDN).
2277 self.num_decode_draft_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = num_decode_draft_tokens
2278 self.num_decode_draft_tokens.np[num_reqs:].fill(-1)
2279 self.num_decode_draft_tokens.copy_to_gpu()
2280
2281 # Hot-Swap lora model
2282 if self.lora_config:
2283 assert (
2284 np.sum(num_sampled_tokens)
2285 <= self.vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens
2286 )
2287 self.set_active_loras(
2288 self.input_batch, num_scheduled_tokens, num_sampled_tokens
2289 )
2290
2291 return (
2292 logits_indices,
2293 spec_decode_metadata,
2294 int(num_sampled_tokens.max()),
2295 )
2296
2297 def _build_attention_metadata(
2298 self,
2299 num_tokens: int,
2300 num_reqs: int,
2301 max_query_len: int,
2302 num_tokens_padded: int | None = None,
2303 num_reqs_padded: int | None = None,
2304 ubatch_slices: UBatchSlices | None = None,
2305 logits_indices: torch.Tensor | None = None,
2306 max_num_sampled_tokens: int | None = None,
2307 use_spec_decode: bool = False,
2308 for_cudagraph_capture: bool = False,
2309 num_scheduled_tokens: dict[str, int] | None = None,
2310 cascade_attn_prefix_lens: list[list[int]] | None = None,
2311 slot_mappings: dict[int, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
2312 ) -> tuple[PerLayerAttnMetadata, CommonAttentionMetadata | None]:
2313 """
2314 Returns:
2315 tuple[attn_metadata, spec_decode_common_attn_metadata]
2316 """
2317 # Attention metadata is not needed for attention free models
2318 if len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups) == 0:
2319 return {}, None
2320
2321 num_tokens_padded = num_tokens_padded or num_tokens
2322 num_reqs_padded = num_reqs_padded or num_reqs
2323 assert num_reqs_padded is not None and num_tokens_padded is not None
2324
2325 attn_metadata: PerLayerAttnMetadata = {}
2326 if ubatch_slices is not None:
2327 attn_metadata = [dict() for _ in range(len(ubatch_slices))]
2328
2329 if for_cudagraph_capture:
2330 # For some attention backends (e.g. FA) with sliding window models we need
2331 # to make sure the backend see a max_seq_len that is larger to the sliding
2332 # window size when capturing to make sure the correct kernel is selected.
2333 max_seq_len = self.max_model_len
2334 else:
2335 max_seq_len = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu.numpy()[:num_reqs].max().item()
2336
2337 kv_cache_groups = self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups
2338
2339 def _get_block_table(kv_cache_gid: int):
2340 assert num_reqs_padded is not None and num_tokens_padded is not None
2341 kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_groups[kv_cache_gid].kv_cache_spec
2342 if isinstance(kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec):
2343 blk_table_tensor = torch.zeros(
2344 (num_reqs_padded, 1),
2345 dtype=torch.int32,
2346 device=self.device,
2347 )
2348 else:
2349 blk_table = self.input_batch.block_table[kv_cache_gid]
2350 blk_table_tensor = blk_table.get_device_tensor(num_reqs_padded)
2351
2352 # Fill unused block table entries with NULL_BLOCK_ID (null block)
2353 # for CUDAGraph padding. Block 0 is reserved for padding.
2354 blk_table_tensor[num_reqs:num_reqs_padded].fill_(NULL_BLOCK_ID)
2355 return blk_table_tensor
2356
2357 assert slot_mappings is not None
2358 block_table_gid_0 = _get_block_table(0)
2359 slot_mapping_gid_0 = slot_mappings[0]
2360
2361 if self.routed_experts_initialized:
2362 # Copy this step's attention slot_mapping into our private
2363 # device buffer. The shared ``slot_mappings[attn_gid]`` is
2364 # owned by the attention block table and will be overwritten
2365 # by the next ``_prepare_inputs``; we need a stable snapshot
2366 # because the async D2H may still be in flight on the copy
2367 # stream when the next step runs.
2368 slot_mapping_attn = slot_mappings[self.routed_experts_capturer.attn_gid]
2369 self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device[:num_tokens].copy_(
2370 slot_mapping_attn[:num_tokens]
2371 )
2372
2373 num_computed_tokens_cpu = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[
2374 :num_reqs_padded
2375 ]
2376 num_prompt_tokens_cpu = self.input_batch.num_prompt_tokens_cpu_tensor[
2377 :num_reqs_padded
2378 ]
2379 seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs_padded]
2380
2381 # is_prefilling: True if request is still in prefill phase.
2382 # Used by mamba backends to distinguish actual decodes from
2383 # short extends.
2384 is_prefilling = num_computed_tokens_cpu < num_prompt_tokens_cpu
2385 # Zero out padded rows so stale data from condense() doesn't
2386 # misclassify padding as prefill in CUDA graph mode.
2387 is_prefilling[num_reqs:] = False
2388
2389 # Compute mm_prefix bidirectional ranges before building
2390 # attention metadata so builders handle them during build().
2391 # By default, ranges exceeding sliding_window are skipped to prevent
2392 # early tokens from attending across the entire image span. Models that
2393 # clamp mm_prefix to the sliding window *in-kernel* (e.g. Gemma4, which
2394 # needs HF's (causal OR blockwise) AND sliding_window on sliding layers)
2395 # opt out of the skip so the bidirectional range survives for images
2396 # larger than the window; the kernel then bounds it per-query.
2397 req_doc_ranges: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int]]] | None = None
2398 if self.is_mm_prefix_lm:
2399 req_doc_ranges = {}
2400 hf_text_config = self.model_config.hf_text_config
2401 _bidi_sw = getattr(hf_text_config, "sliding_window", None)
2402 _clamps_in_kernel = getattr(
2403 self.model, "mm_prefix_clamp_sliding_window", False
2404 ) or getattr(hf_text_config, "mm_prefix_clamp_sliding_window", False)
2405 # Some models (DeepSeek-V4 vision) define the bidirectional span
2406 # over the whole sentinel block ([IMAGE_START, IMAGE_END]) rather
2407 # than the embed tokens, and prepend a position-dependent
2408 # alignment pad before the first sentinel. For those, derive the
2409 # span from the full placeholder range and strip the pad.
2410 # TODO(Isotr0py): Refactor mm_prefix_lm implementation
2411 # for better readability and maintainability.
2412 _span_pad_modulus = getattr(
2413 hf_text_config, "mm_prefix_span_leading_pad_modulus", 0
2414 )
2415 for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids:
2416 image_doc_ranges = []
2417 req_state = self.requests[req_id]
2418 for mm_feature in req_state.mm_features:
2419 if mm_feature.modality == "audio":
2420 continue
2421 pos_info = mm_feature.mm_position
2422 if _span_pad_modulus:
2423 pad = (
2424 _span_pad_modulus - 1 - pos_info.offset % _span_pad_modulus
2425 )
2426 img_doc_range = [
2427 (
2428 pos_info.offset + pad,
2429 pos_info.offset + pos_info.length - 1,
2430 )
2431 ]
2432 else:
2433 img_doc_range = pos_info.extract_embeds_range()
2434 for r in img_doc_range:
2435 if (
2436 not _clamps_in_kernel
2437 and _bidi_sw is not None
2438 and (r[1] - r[0] + 1) > _bidi_sw
2439 ):
2440 continue
2441 image_doc_ranges.append(r)
2442 req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
2443 req_doc_ranges[req_idx] = image_doc_ranges
2444
2445 # Reference Sliding Window Attention (R-SWA): pass per-request prompt
2446 # lengths so the attention backend can keep the prefix globally visible.
2447 # The backend owns the persistent CUDA-graph-safe GPU buffer.
2448 rswa_prefix_lens = None
2449 if self.model_config.rswa_window is not None:
2450 rswa_prefix_lens = num_prompt_tokens_cpu
2451
2452 replayssm_decode_base_cpu = None
2453 if self.cache_config.use_replayssm:
2454 replayssm_decode_base_cpu = (
2455 self.input_batch.replayssm_decode_base_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs_padded]
2456 )
2457
2458 cm_base = CommonAttentionMetadata(
2459 query_start_loc=self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs_padded + 1],
2460 query_start_loc_cpu=self.query_start_loc.cpu[: num_reqs_padded + 1],
2461 seq_lens=self.seq_lens[:num_reqs_padded],
2462 seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound=seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound,
2463 replayssm_decode_base_cpu=replayssm_decode_base_cpu,
2464 num_reqs=num_reqs_padded,
2465 num_actual_tokens=num_tokens_padded,
2466 max_query_len=max_query_len,
2467 max_seq_len=max_seq_len,
2468 block_table_tensor=block_table_gid_0,
2469 slot_mapping=slot_mapping_gid_0,
2470 causal=True,
2471 is_prefilling=is_prefilling,
2472 positions=self.positions[:num_tokens_padded],
2473 mm_req_doc_ranges=req_doc_ranges,
2474 rswa_prefix_lens=rswa_prefix_lens,
2475 )
2476
2477 if self.dcp_world_size > 1:
2478 self.dcp_local_seq_lens.cpu[:num_reqs] = get_dcp_local_seq_lens(
2479 self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs],
2480 self.dcp_world_size,
2481 self.dcp_rank,
2482 self.parallel_config.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size,
2483 )
2484 self.dcp_local_seq_lens.cpu[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
2485 self.dcp_local_seq_lens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs_padded)
2486
2487 cm_base.dcp_local_seq_lens = self.dcp_local_seq_lens.gpu[:num_reqs_padded]
2488 cm_base.dcp_local_seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound = self.dcp_local_seq_lens.cpu[
2489 :num_reqs_padded
2490 ]
2491
2492 if logits_indices is not None and self.cache_config.kv_sharing_fast_prefill:
2493 cm_base.num_logits_indices = logits_indices.size(0)
2494 cm_base.max_logits_per_req = max_num_sampled_tokens
2495 cm_base.logits_indices_padded = self._prepare_kv_sharing_fast_prefill(
2496 logits_indices
2497 )
2498
2499 # Cache attention metadata builds across hybrid KV-cache groups
2500 # The only thing that changes between different hybrid KV-cache groups when the
2501 # same metadata builder and KVCacheSpec is the same is the block table, so we
2502 # can cache the attention metadata builds and just update the block table using
2503 # `builder.update_block_table` if the builder supports it.
2504 cached_attn_metadata: dict[
2505 tuple[KVCacheSpec, type[AttentionMetadataBuilder]], AttentionMetadata
2506 ] = {}
2507
2508 def _build_attn_group_metadata(
2509 kv_cache_gid: int,
2510 attn_gid: int,
2511 common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata,
2512 ubid: int | None = None,
2513 ) -> None:
2514 attn_group = self.attn_groups[kv_cache_gid][attn_gid]
2515 builder = attn_group.get_metadata_builder(ubid or 0)
2516 kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_groups[kv_cache_gid].kv_cache_spec
2517 if isinstance(kv_cache_spec, UniformTypeKVCacheSpecs):
2518 kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_spec.kv_cache_specs[attn_group.layer_names[0]]
2519 cache_key = (kv_cache_spec, type(builder))
2520
2521 cascade_attn_prefix_len = (
2522 cascade_attn_prefix_lens[kv_cache_gid][attn_gid]
2523 if cascade_attn_prefix_lens
2524 else 0
2525 )
2526
2527 extra_attn_metadata_args = {}
2528 if use_spec_decode and isinstance(
2529 builder,
2530 (
2531 Mamba2AttentionMetadataBuilder,
2532 GDNAttentionMetadataBuilder,
2533 BailingLinearAttentionMetadataBuilder,
2534 ShortConvAttentionMetadataBuilder,
2535 ),
2536 ):
2537 assert ubid is None, (
2538 "UBatching not supported with GDN or linear attn yet"
2539 )
2540 extra_attn_metadata_args = dict(
2541 num_accepted_tokens=self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs_padded],
2542 num_decode_draft_tokens_cpu=self.num_decode_draft_tokens.cpu[
2543 :num_reqs_padded
2544 ],
2545 )
2546 if (
2547 isinstance(builder, Mamba2AttentionMetadataBuilder)
2548 and self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx is not None
2549 ):
2550 extra_attn_metadata_args["prev_last_scheduled_idx"] = (
2551 self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx.gpu[:num_reqs_padded]
2552 )
2553
2554 if for_cudagraph_capture:
2555 attn_metadata_i = builder.build_for_cudagraph_capture(
2556 common_attn_metadata
2557 )
2558 elif (
2559 cache_key in cached_attn_metadata
2560 and builder.supports_update_block_table
2561 ):
2562 attn_metadata_i = builder.update_block_table(
2563 cached_attn_metadata[cache_key],
2564 common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor,
2565 common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping,
2566 )
2567 else:
2568 attn_metadata_i = builder.build(
2569 common_prefix_len=cascade_attn_prefix_len,
2570 common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,
2571 **extra_attn_metadata_args,
2572 )
2573 if builder.supports_update_block_table:
2574 cached_attn_metadata[cache_key] = attn_metadata_i
2575
2576 if ubid is None:
2577 assert isinstance(attn_metadata, dict)
2578 attn_metadata_dict = attn_metadata
2579 else:
2580 assert isinstance(attn_metadata, list)
2581 attn_metadata_dict = attn_metadata[ubid]
2582
2583 for layer_name in attn_group.layer_names:
2584 attn_metadata_dict[layer_name] = attn_metadata_i
2585
2586 # Prepare the attention metadata for each KV cache group and make layers
2587 # in the same group share the same metadata.
2588 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = None
2589 for kv_cache_gid, kv_cache_group in enumerate(kv_cache_groups):
2590 cm = copy(cm_base) # shallow copy
2591
2592 # Basically only the encoder seq_lens, block_table and slot_mapping change
2593 # for each kv_cache_group.
2594 cm.encoder_seq_lens, cm.encoder_seq_lens_cpu = self._get_encoder_seq_lens(
2595 num_scheduled_tokens or {},
2596 kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec,
2597 num_reqs_padded,
2598 for_cudagraph_capture=for_cudagraph_capture,
2599 )
2600 if kv_cache_gid > 0:
2601 cm.block_table_tensor = _get_block_table(kv_cache_gid)
2602 cm.slot_mapping = slot_mappings[kv_cache_gid]
2603
2604 if self.speculative_config and spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is None:
2605 if isinstance(
2606 self.drafter,
2607 (
2608 EagleProposer,
2609 DFlashProposer,
2610 Gemma4Proposer,
2611 ExtractHiddenStatesProposer,
2612 ),
2613 ):
2614 if self.drafter.kv_cache_gid == kv_cache_gid:
2615 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = cm
2616 else:
2617 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = cm
2618 # Capture per-group block tables for multi-group proposers.
2619 if self.speculative_config and isinstance(self.drafter, Step3p5MTPProposer):
2620 self.drafter.set_per_group_attn_metadata(
2621 kv_cache_gid, cm.block_table_tensor, cm.slot_mapping
2622 )
2623 elif self.speculative_config and isinstance(self.drafter, Gemma4Proposer):
