2624 self.drafter.set_per_group_block_table(
2625 kv_cache_gid, cm.block_table_tensor
2626 )
2627
2628 for attn_gid in range(len(self.attn_groups[kv_cache_gid])):
2629 if ubatch_slices is not None:
2630 for ubid, _cm in enumerate(split_attn_metadata(ubatch_slices, cm)):
2631 _build_attn_group_metadata(kv_cache_gid, attn_gid, _cm, ubid)
2632
2633 else:
2634 _build_attn_group_metadata(kv_cache_gid, attn_gid, cm)
2635
2636 if spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None and (
2637 num_reqs != num_reqs_padded or num_tokens != num_tokens_padded
2638 ):
2639 # Currently the drafter still only uses piecewise cudagraphs (and modifies
2640 # the attention metadata in directly), and therefore does not want to use
2641 # padded attention metadata.
2642 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = (
2643 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata.unpadded(num_tokens, num_reqs)
2644 )
2645
2646 return attn_metadata, spec_decode_common_attn_metadata
2647
2648 def _compute_cascade_attn_prefix_lens(
2649 self,
2650 num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
2651 num_computed_tokens: np.ndarray,
2652 num_common_prefix_blocks: list[int],
2653 ) -> list[list[int]] | None:
2654 """
2655 Returns:
2656 Optional[cascade_attn_prefix_lens]
2657 cascade_attn_prefix_lens is 2D:
2658 ``[kv_cache_group_id][attn_group_idx]``,
2659 None if we should not use cascade attention
2660 """
2661
2662 use_cascade_attn = False
2663 num_kv_cache_groups = len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)
2664 cascade_attn_prefix_lens: list[list[int]] = [
2665 [] for _ in range(num_kv_cache_groups)
2666 ]
2667
2668 for kv_cache_gid in range(num_kv_cache_groups):
2669 for attn_group in self.attn_groups[kv_cache_gid]:
2670 if isinstance(attn_group.kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec):
2671 cascade_attn_prefix_len = 0
2672 else:
2673 # 0 if cascade attention should not be used
2674 cascade_attn_prefix_len = self._compute_cascade_attn_prefix_len(
2675 num_scheduled_tokens,
2676 num_computed_tokens,
2677 num_common_prefix_blocks[kv_cache_gid],
2678 attn_group.kv_cache_spec,
2679 attn_group.get_metadata_builder(),
2680 )
2681 cascade_attn_prefix_lens[kv_cache_gid].append(cascade_attn_prefix_len)
2682 use_cascade_attn |= cascade_attn_prefix_len > 0
2683
2684 return cascade_attn_prefix_lens if use_cascade_attn else None
2685
2686 def _compute_cascade_attn_prefix_len(
2687 self,
2688 num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
2689 num_computed_tokens: np.ndarray,
2690 num_common_prefix_blocks: int,
2691 kv_cache_spec: KVCacheSpec,
2692 attn_metadata_builder: AttentionMetadataBuilder,
2693 ) -> int:
2694 """Compute the length of the common prefix for cascade attention.
2695
2696 NOTE(woosuk): The common prefix length returned by this function
2697 represents the length used specifically for cascade attention, not the
2698 actual number of tokens shared between requests. When cascade attention
2699 is disabled (use_cascade=False), this function returns 0 even if
2700 requests share common tokens. Additionally, the common prefix length is
2701 truncated to a multiple of the block size and may be further truncated
2702 due to implementation details explained below.
2703
2704 Args:
2705 num_scheduled_tokens: Number of tokens scheduled per request.
2706 num_common_prefix_blocks: Number of shared KV cache blocks.
2707
2708 Returns:
2709 int: Length of common prefix in tokens.
2710 """
2711
2712 common_prefix_len = num_common_prefix_blocks * kv_cache_spec.block_size
2713 if common_prefix_len == 0:
2714 # Common case.
2715 return 0
2716
2717 # NOTE(woosuk): Cascade attention uses two attention kernels: one
2718 # for the common prefix and the other for the rest. For the first
2719 # kernel, we concatenate all the query tokens (possibly from
2720 # different requests) and treat them as if they are from the same
2721 # request. Then, we use bi-directional attention to process the
2722 # common prefix in the KV cache. Importantly, this means that the
2723 # first kernel does not do any masking.
2724
2725 # Consider the following example:
2726 # Request 1's input query: [D, E, X]
2727 # Request 1's kv cache: [A, B, C, D, E, X]
2728 # Request 1's num_computed_tokens: 3 (i.e., [A, B, C])
2729 # Request 2's input query: [E, Y]
2730 # Request 2's kv cache: [A, B, C, D, E, Y]
2731 # Request 2's num_computed_tokens: 4 (i.e., [A, B, C, D])
2732
2733 # If we use [A, B, C, D, E] as the common prefix, then the
2734 # first kernel will compute the bi-directional attention between
2735 # input query [D, E, X, E, Y] and common prefix [A, B, C, D, E].
2736 # However, this is wrong because D in Request 1 should not attend to
2737 # E in the common prefix (i.e., we need masking).
2738 # To avoid this, [A, B, C, D] should be the common prefix.
2739 # That is, the common prefix should be capped by the minimum
2740 # num_computed_tokens among the requests, and plus one to include
2741 # the first token of the query.
2742
2743 # In practice, we use [A, B, C] as the common prefix, instead of
2744 # [A, B, C, D] (i.e., the common prefix is capped by the minimum
2745 # num_computed_tokens, without plus one).
2746 # This is because of an implementation detail: We want to always
2747 # use two kernels for cascade attention. Let's imagine:
2748 # Request 3's input query: [D]
2749 # Request 3's kv cache: [A, B, C, D]
2750 # Request 3's num_computed_tokens: 3 (i.e., [A, B, C])
2751 # If we use [A, B, C, D] as the common prefix for Request 1-3,
2752 # then Request 3 will be processed only by the first kernel,
2753 # and the second kernel will get an empty input. While this is not
2754 # a fundamental problem, our current implementation does not support
2755 # this case.
2756 common_prefix_len = min(common_prefix_len, num_computed_tokens.min())
2757 # common_prefix_len should be a multiple of the block size.
2758 common_prefix_len = (
2759 common_prefix_len // kv_cache_spec.block_size * kv_cache_spec.block_size
2760 )
2761 use_sliding_window = isinstance(kv_cache_spec, SlidingWindowSpec) or (
2762 isinstance(kv_cache_spec, FullAttentionSpec)
2763 and kv_cache_spec.sliding_window is not None
2764 )
2765 use_local_attention = isinstance(kv_cache_spec, ChunkedLocalAttentionSpec) or (
2766 isinstance(kv_cache_spec, FullAttentionSpec)
2767 and kv_cache_spec.attention_chunk_size is not None
2768 )
2769 assert isinstance(kv_cache_spec, AttentionSpec)
2770 use_cascade = attn_metadata_builder.use_cascade_attention(
2771 common_prefix_len=common_prefix_len,
2772 query_lens=num_scheduled_tokens,
2773 num_query_heads=self.num_query_heads,
2774 num_kv_heads=kv_cache_spec.num_kv_heads,
2775 use_alibi=self.use_alibi,
2776 use_sliding_window=use_sliding_window,
2777 use_local_attention=use_local_attention,
2778 num_sms=self.num_sms,
2779 dcp_world_size=self.dcp_world_size,
2780 )
2781 return common_prefix_len if use_cascade else 0
2782
2783 def _calc_mrope_positions(self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"):
2784 mrope_pos_ptr = 0
2785 for index, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids):
2786 req = self.requests[req_id]
2787 assert req.mrope_positions is not None
2788
2789 num_computed_tokens = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[index]
2790 num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id]
2791 num_prompt_tokens = length_from_prompt_token_ids_or_embeds(
2792 req.prompt_token_ids, req.prompt_embeds
2793 )
2794
2795 if num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens > num_prompt_tokens:
2796 prompt_part_len = max(0, num_prompt_tokens - num_computed_tokens)
2797 completion_part_len = max(0, num_scheduled_tokens - prompt_part_len)
2798 else:
2799 prompt_part_len = num_scheduled_tokens
2800 completion_part_len = 0
2801
2802 assert num_scheduled_tokens == prompt_part_len + completion_part_len
2803
2804 if prompt_part_len > 0:
2805 # prompt's mrope_positions are pre-computed
2806 dst_start = mrope_pos_ptr
2807 dst_end = mrope_pos_ptr + prompt_part_len
2808 src_start = num_computed_tokens
2809 src_end = num_computed_tokens + prompt_part_len
2810
2811 self.mrope_positions.cpu[:, dst_start:dst_end] = req.mrope_positions[
2812 :, src_start:src_end
2813 ]
2814 mrope_pos_ptr += prompt_part_len
2815
2816 if completion_part_len > 0:
2817 # compute completion's mrope_positions on-the-fly
2818 dst_start = mrope_pos_ptr
2819 dst_end = mrope_pos_ptr + completion_part_len
2820
2821 assert req.mrope_position_delta is not None
2822 MRotaryEmbedding.get_next_input_positions_tensor(
2823 out=self.mrope_positions.np,
2824 out_offset=dst_start,
2825 mrope_position_delta=req.mrope_position_delta,
2826 context_len=num_computed_tokens + prompt_part_len,
2827 num_new_tokens=completion_part_len,
2828 )
2829
2830 mrope_pos_ptr += completion_part_len
2831
2832 def _calc_spec_decode_metadata(
2833 self,
2834 num_draft_tokens: np.ndarray,
2835 cu_num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
2836 ) -> SpecDecodeMetadata:
2837 # Inputs:
2838 # cu_num_scheduled_tokens: [ 4, 104, 107, 207, 209]
2839 # num_draft_tokens: [ 3, 0, 2, 0, 1]
2840 # Outputs:
2841 # cu_num_draft_tokens: [ 3, 3, 5, 5, 6]
2842 # logits_indices: [ 0, 1, 2, 3, 103, 104, 105, 106,
2843 # 206, 207, 208]
2844 # target_logits_indices: [ 0, 1, 2, 5, 6, 9]
2845 # bonus_logits_indices: [ 3, 4, 7, 8, 10]
2846
2847 # Compute the logits indices.
2848 # [4, 1, 3, 1, 2]
2849 num_sampled_tokens = num_draft_tokens + 1
2850
2851 # Step 1.
2852 # cu_num_sampled_tokens: [4, 5, 8, 9, 11]
2853 # _arange_scratch[:11]: [0, 1, 2, 3, 0, 0, 1, 2, 0, 0, 1]
2854 cu_num_sampled_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
2855 num_sampled_tokens, self._arange_scratch, cumsum_dtype=np.int32
2856 )
2857 # Step 2. [0, 0, 0, 0, 103, 104, 104, 104, 206, 207, 207]
2858 logits_indices = np.repeat(
2859 cu_num_scheduled_tokens - num_sampled_tokens, num_sampled_tokens
2860 )
2861 # Step 3. [0, 1, 2, 3, 103, 104, 105, 106, 206, 207, 208]
2862 logits_indices += self._arange_scratch[: cu_num_sampled_tokens[-1]]
2863
2864 # Compute the bonus logits indices.
2865 bonus_logits_indices = cu_num_sampled_tokens - 1
2866
2867 # Compute the draft logits indices.
2868 # cu_num_draft_tokens: [3, 3, 5, 5, 6]
2869 # _arange_scratch[:6]: [0, 1, 2, 0, 1, 0]
2870 cu_num_draft_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
2871 num_draft_tokens, self._arange_scratch, cumsum_dtype=np.int32
2872 )
2873 # [0, 0, 0, 5, 5, 9]
2874 target_logits_indices = np.repeat(
2875 cu_num_sampled_tokens - num_sampled_tokens, num_draft_tokens
2876 )
2877 # [0, 1, 2, 5, 6, 9]
2878 target_logits_indices += self._arange_scratch[: cu_num_draft_tokens[-1]]
2879
2880 cu_num_draft_tokens = async_tensor_h2d(cu_num_draft_tokens, device=self.device)
2881 cu_num_sampled_tokens = async_tensor_h2d(
2882 cu_num_sampled_tokens, device=self.device
2883 )
2884 logits_indices = async_tensor_h2d(logits_indices, device=self.device)
2885 target_logits_indices = async_tensor_h2d(
2886 target_logits_indices, device=self.device
2887 )
2888 bonus_logits_indices = async_tensor_h2d(
2889 bonus_logits_indices, device=self.device
2890 )
2891
2892 # Compute the draft token ids.
2893 # draft_token_indices: [ 1, 2, 3, 105, 106, 208]
2894 draft_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[logits_indices]
2895 draft_token_ids = draft_token_ids[target_logits_indices + 1]
2896
2897 return SpecDecodeMetadata(
2898 draft_token_ids=draft_token_ids,
2899 num_draft_tokens=num_draft_tokens.tolist(),
2900 cu_num_draft_tokens=cu_num_draft_tokens,
2901 cu_num_sampled_tokens=cu_num_sampled_tokens,
2902 target_logits_indices=target_logits_indices,
2903 bonus_logits_indices=bonus_logits_indices,
2904 logits_indices=logits_indices,
2905 )
2906
2907 def _prepare_kv_sharing_fast_prefill(
2908 self,
2909 logits_indices: torch.Tensor,
2910 ) -> torch.Tensor:
2911 assert self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices is not None
2912 num_logits = logits_indices.shape[0]
2913 assert num_logits > 0
2914 self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices[:num_logits].copy_(logits_indices)
2915 # There might have leftover indices in logits_indices[num_logits:]
2916 # from previous iterations, whose values may be greater than the
2917 # batch size in the current iteration. To ensure indices are always
2918 # valid, fill the padded indices with the last index. Broadcast the
2919 # scalar GPU-side to avoid a D2H sync on `.item()`.
2920 self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices[num_logits:] = logits_indices[-1]
2921 # Dispatch for the decoder portion of the model.
2922 _, batch_desc = self.cudagraph_dispatcher.dispatch(
2923 num_logits, invalid_modes={CUDAGraphMode.FULL}
2924 )
2925 num_logits_padded = batch_desc.num_tokens
2926 logits_indices_padded = self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices[
2927 :num_logits_padded
2928 ]
2929 return logits_indices_padded
2930
2931 def _batch_mm_inputs_from_scheduler(
2932 self,
2933 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
2934 ) -> tuple[
2935 list[str],
2936 list[tuple[str, MultiModalKwargsItem]],
2937 list[tuple[str, PlaceholderRange]],
2938 ]:
2939 """Batch multimodal inputs from scheduled encoder inputs.
2940
2941 Args:
2942 scheduler_output: The scheduler output containing scheduled encoder
2943 inputs.
2944
2945 Returns:
2946 A tuple of (mm_hashes, mm_kwargs, mm_lora_refs) where:
2947 - mm_hashes: List of multimodal hashes for each item
2948 - mm_kwargs: List of multimodal kwargs for each item
2949 - mm_lora_refs: List of (req_id, placeholder_range) for each item
2950 """
2951 scheduled_encoder_inputs = scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs
2952 if not scheduled_encoder_inputs:
2953 return [], [], []
2954
2955 mm_hashes = list[str]()
2956 mm_kwargs = list[tuple[str, MultiModalKwargsItem]]()
2957 # Multimodal LoRA reference info to map each multimodal item
2958 # back to its request & position
2959 mm_lora_refs = list[tuple[str, PlaceholderRange]]()
2960 for req_id, encoder_input_ids in scheduled_encoder_inputs.items():
2961 req_state = self.requests[req_id]
2962
2963 for mm_input_id in encoder_input_ids:
2964 mm_feature = req_state.mm_features[mm_input_id]
2965 if mm_feature.data is None:
2966 continue
2967
2968 mm_hashes.append(mm_feature.identifier)
2969 mm_kwargs.append((mm_feature.modality, mm_feature.data))
2970 mm_lora_refs.append((req_id, mm_feature.mm_position))
2971
2972 return mm_hashes, mm_kwargs, mm_lora_refs
2973
2974 def _cache_encoder_output(
2975 self,
2976 mm_hash: str,
2977 output: torch.Tensor,
2978 ec_manager_metadata: "EncoderCacheManagerMetadata | None",
2979 free_encoder_mm_hashes: list[str],
2980 ) -> None:
2981 """Store an encoder output for later multimodal embedding gather."""
2982 del ec_manager_metadata, free_encoder_mm_hashes
2983 self.encoder_cache[mm_hash] = output
2984 self.maybe_save_ec_to_connector(self.encoder_cache, mm_hash)
2985
2986 def _execute_mm_encoder(
2987 self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"
2988 ) -> list[torch.Tensor]:
2989 mm_hashes, mm_kwargs, mm_lora_refs = self._batch_mm_inputs_from_scheduler(
2990 scheduler_output
2991 )
2992
2993 if not mm_kwargs:
2994 return []
2995
2996 # `prompt_embeds` is a passthrough modality, the tensor is already in
2997 # the model embedding space, so no encoder runs. Inject each
2998 # `prompt_embeds` tensor directly into the encoder cache here so that
2999 # `_gather_mm_embeddings` can splice it via the standard `is_mm_embed`
3000 # path.
3001 pe_indices = [
3002 i
3003 for i, (modality, _) in enumerate(mm_kwargs)
3004 if modality == "prompt_embeds"
3005 ]
3006 if pe_indices:
3007 for i in pe_indices:
3008 pe_tensor = mm_kwargs[i][1]["embedding"].data
3009 assert isinstance(pe_tensor, torch.Tensor)
3010
3011 self._cache_encoder_output(
3012 mm_hashes[i],
3013 async_tensor_h2d(pe_tensor, device=self.device),
3014 scheduler_output.ec_manager_metadata,
3015 scheduler_output.free_encoder_mm_hashes,
3016 )
3017 # Filter out `prompt_embeds` items from mm_kwargs/mm_hashes/mm_lora_refs
3018 # since they don't require further encoder processing.
3019 mm_hashes = [h for i, h in enumerate(mm_hashes) if i not in pe_indices]
3020 mm_kwargs = [k for i, k in enumerate(mm_kwargs) if i not in pe_indices]
3021 mm_lora_refs = [
3022 r for i, r in enumerate(mm_lora_refs) if i not in pe_indices
3023 ]
3024 if not mm_kwargs:
3025 return [] # nothing left to encode after filtering out `prompt_embeds`
3026
3027 should_time = bool(
3028 self.observability_config
3029 and self.observability_config.enable_mm_processor_stats
3030 and scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs
3031 )
3032
3033 # Batch mm inputs as much as we can: if a request in the batch has
3034 # multiple modalities or a different modality than the previous one,
3035 # we process it separately to preserve item order.
3036 # FIXME(ywang96): This is a hacky way to deal with multiple modalities
3037 # in the same batch while still being able to benefit from batching
3038 # multimodal inputs. The proper solution should be reordering the
3039 # encoder outputs.
3040 model = cast(SupportsMultiModal, self.model)
3041
3042 if self.lora_config and self.lora_manager.supports_tower_connector_lora():
3043 # Build LoRA mappings independently for encoder inputs
3044 # (encoder batch structure is different from main batch)
3045 prompt_lora_mapping = []
3046 token_lora_mapping = []
3047 lora_requests = set()
3048 encoder_token_counts = []
3049 connector_token_counts = []
3050
3051 for (req_id, pos_info), (modality, mm_item) in zip(
3052 mm_lora_refs,
3053 mm_kwargs,
3054 ):
3055 req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
3056 lora_id = int(self.input_batch.request_lora_mapping[req_idx])
3057
3058 tower_tokens, connector_tokens = self.model.get_mm_lora_token_counts( # type: ignore[attr-defined]
3059 modality=modality,
3060 mm_kwargs=mm_item,
3061 num_mm_embeds=pos_info.get_num_embeds(),
3062 )
3063 prompt_lora_mapping.append(lora_id)
3064 token_lora_mapping.extend([lora_id] * tower_tokens)
3065 encoder_token_counts.append(tower_tokens)
3066 connector_token_counts.append(connector_tokens)
3067
3068 if lora_id > 0:
3069 lora_request = self.input_batch.lora_id_to_lora_request.get(lora_id)
3070 if lora_request is not None:
3071 lora_requests.add(lora_request)
3072
3073 # Set tower adapter mapping
3074 tower_mapping = LoRAMapping(
3075 tuple(token_lora_mapping),
3076 tuple(prompt_lora_mapping),
3077 is_prefill=True,
3078 type=LoRAMappingType.TOWER,
3079 )
3080 self.lora_manager.set_active_adapters(lora_requests, tower_mapping)
3081
3082 # Only set connector mapping if the model actually has a connector.
3083 # Some multimodal models inherit a stub `get_num_mm_connector_tokens`
3084 # from `SupportsMultiModal`, which returns None and should not be
3085 # treated as a signal that connector LoRA is supported.
3086 mm_mapping = (
3087 self.model.get_mm_mapping() # type: ignore[attr-defined]
3088 if hasattr(self.model, "get_mm_mapping")
3089 else None
3090 )
3091 if (
3092 mm_mapping is not None
3093 and mm_mapping.connector
3094 and all(count is not None for count in connector_token_counts)
3095 ):
3096 connector_token_mapping = np.repeat(
3097 np.array(prompt_lora_mapping, dtype=np.int32),
3098 np.array(connector_token_counts, dtype=np.int32),
3099 )
3100 connector_mapping = LoRAMapping(
3101 index_mapping=tuple(connector_token_mapping.tolist()),
3102 prompt_mapping=tuple(prompt_lora_mapping),
3103 is_prefill=True,
3104 type=LoRAMappingType.CONNECTOR,
3105 )
3106
3107 self.lora_manager.set_active_adapters(
3108 lora_requests,
3109 connector_mapping,
3110 )
3111
3112 encoder_outputs: list[torch.Tensor] = []
3113 # Track the current index in mm_kwargs/mm_lora_refs to map groups to request IDs
3114 current_item_idx = 0
3115 for modality, num_items, mm_kwargs_batch in group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
3116 mm_kwargs, device=self.device, pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY
3117 ):
3118 batch_outputs: MultiModalEmbeddings
3119
3120 # EVS and dynamic res video related change.
3121 # (ekhvedchenia): Temporary hack to limit peak memory usage when
3122 # processing multimodal data. This solves the issue with scheduler
3123 # putting too many video samples into a single batch. Scheduler
3124 # uses pruned vision tokens count to compare it versus compute
3125 # budget which is incorrect (Either input media size or non-pruned
3126 # output vision tokens count should be considered)
3127 # dynamic res video for nemotron temporarily uses this hack via
3128 # requires_sequential_video_encoding
3129 # because it doesn't yet support video batching.
3130 # TODO(ywang96): Fix memory profiling to take EVS into account and
3131 # remove this hack.
3132 if (
3133 (
3134 self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled
3135 or self.requires_sequential_video_encoding
3136 )
3137 and modality == "video"
3138 and num_items > 1
3139 ):
3140 batch_outputs_lst = list[torch.Tensor]()
3141 for video_idx in range(num_items):
3142 video_mm_kwargs_item = mm_kwargs[current_item_idx + video_idx]
3143 with self.timed_encoder_operation(
3144 should_time, mm_lora_refs, current_item_idx + video_idx, 1
3145 ):
3146 _, _, micro_batch_mm_inputs = next(
3147 group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
3148 [video_mm_kwargs_item],
3149 device=self.device,
3150 pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY,
3151 )
3152 )
3153
3154 micro_batch_outputs = model.embed_multimodal(
3155 **micro_batch_mm_inputs
3156 )
3157
3158 batch_outputs_lst.extend(micro_batch_outputs)
3159
3160 batch_outputs = batch_outputs_lst
3161 else:
3162 # Run the encoder.
3163 # `batch_outputs` is either of the following:
3164 # 1. A tensor of shape (num_items, feature_size, hidden_size)
3165 # in case feature_size is fixed across all multimodal items.
3166 # 2. A list or tuple (length: num_items) of tensors,
3167 # each of shape (feature_size, hidden_size) in case the feature
3168 # size is dynamic depending on the input multimodal items.
3169
3170 with self.timed_encoder_operation(
3171 should_time, mm_lora_refs, current_item_idx, num_items
3172 ):
3173 cudagraph_output = None
3174 if (
3175 self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None
3176 and self.encoder_cudagraph_manager.supports_modality(modality)
3177 ):
3178 cudagraph_output = self.encoder_cudagraph_manager.execute(
3179 mm_kwargs_batch,
3180 )
3181
3182 if cudagraph_output is not None:
3183 batch_outputs = cudagraph_output
3184 else:
3185 batch_outputs = model.embed_multimodal(**mm_kwargs_batch)
3186
3187 sanity_check_mm_encoder_outputs(batch_outputs, expected_num_items=num_items)
3188 encoder_outputs.extend(batch_outputs)
3189
3190 current_item_idx += num_items
3191
3192 # Cache the encoder outputs by mm_hash
3193 for mm_hash, output in zip(mm_hashes, encoder_outputs):
3194 self._cache_encoder_output(
3195 mm_hash,
3196 output,
3197 scheduler_output.ec_manager_metadata,
3198 scheduler_output.free_encoder_mm_hashes,
3199 )
3200 logger.debug("Finish execute for mm hash %s", mm_hash)
3201
3202 return encoder_outputs
3203
3204 def _get_encoder_output_from_cache(self, mm_hash: str) -> torch.Tensor | None:
3205 """Return a cached encoder output for multimodal
3206 embedding gather."""
3207 return self.encoder_cache.get(mm_hash, None)
3208
3209 def _gather_mm_embeddings(
3210 self,
3211 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
3212 shift_computed_tokens: int = 0,
3213 ) -> tuple[list[torch.Tensor], torch.Tensor]:
3214 total_num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
3215
3216 mm_embeds = list[torch.Tensor]()
3217 is_mm_embed = torch.zeros(
3218 total_num_scheduled_tokens,
3219 dtype=torch.bool,
3220 device="cpu",
3221 pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY,
3222 )
3223
3224 req_start_idx = 0
3225 should_sync_mrope_positions = False
3226
3227 for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids:
3228 mm_embeds_req: list[torch.Tensor] = []
3229
3230 num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id]
3231 req_state = self.requests[req_id]
3232 num_computed_tokens = req_state.num_computed_tokens + shift_computed_tokens
3233
3234 mm_features = req_state.mm_features
3235 lo, hi = get_mm_features_in_window(
3236 mm_features,
3237 start=num_computed_tokens,
3238 end=num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens,
3239 )
3240 for i in range(lo, hi):
3241 mm_feature = mm_features[i]
3242 pos_info = mm_feature.mm_position
3243 start_pos = pos_info.offset
3244 num_encoder_tokens = pos_info.length
3245
3246 start_idx = max(num_computed_tokens - start_pos, 0)
3247 end_idx = min(
3248 num_computed_tokens - start_pos + num_scheduled_tokens,
3249 num_encoder_tokens,
3250 )
3251 assert start_idx < end_idx
3252 curr_embeds_start, curr_embeds_end = (
3253 pos_info.get_embeds_indices_in_range(start_idx, end_idx)
3254 )
3255 # If there are no embeddings in the current range, we skip
3256 # gathering the embeddings.
3257 if curr_embeds_start == curr_embeds_end:
3258 continue
3259
3260 mm_hash = mm_feature.identifier
3261 encoder_output = self._get_encoder_output_from_cache(mm_hash)
3262 if encoder_output is None:
3263 # A feature starting at/after the processed boundary is only
3264 # reached via the drafter's +1 look-ahead and might not be
3265 # encoded yet; fall back to the token embedding for drafting.
3266 if (
3267 start_pos
3268 >= req_state.num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens
3269 ):
3270 continue
3271 raise RuntimeError(f"Encoder cache miss for {mm_hash}.")
3272
3273 if (is_embed := pos_info.is_embed) is not None:
3274 is_embed = is_embed[start_idx:end_idx]
3275 mm_embeds_item = encoder_output[curr_embeds_start:curr_embeds_end]
3276 else:
3277 mm_embeds_item = encoder_output[start_idx:end_idx]
3278
3279 req_start_pos = req_start_idx + start_pos - num_computed_tokens
3280 # OR mask for overlapping mm_features (use_audio_in_video)
3281 if is_embed is None:
3282 is_mm_embed[req_start_pos + start_idx : req_start_pos + end_idx] = (
3283 True
3284 )
3285 else:
3286 is_mm_embed[
3287 req_start_pos + start_idx : req_start_pos + end_idx
3288 ] |= is_embed
3289 set_mm_embedding_modality(mm_embeds_item, mm_feature.modality)
3290 mm_embeds_req.append(mm_embeds_item)
3291
3292 if self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled and self.uses_mrope:
3293 assert req_state.mrope_positions is not None
3294 should_sync_mrope_positions = True
3295 old_mm_embeds_req = mm_embeds_req
3296 mm_embeds_req, new_mrope_positions, new_delta = (
3297 self.model.recompute_mrope_positions(
3298 input_ids=req_state.prompt_token_ids,
3299 multimodal_embeddings=mm_embeds_req,
3300 mrope_positions=req_state.mrope_positions,
3301 num_computed_tokens=req_state.num_computed_tokens,
3302 )
3303 )
3304 mm_embeds_req = [
3305 copy_mm_embedding_modality(src, dst)
3306 for src, dst in zip(old_mm_embeds_req, mm_embeds_req)
3307 ]
3308 req_state.mrope_positions.copy_(new_mrope_positions)
3309 req_state.mrope_position_delta = new_delta
3310
3311 mm_embeds.extend(mm_embeds_req)
3312 req_start_idx += num_scheduled_tokens
3313
3314 if should_sync_mrope_positions:
3315 self._calc_mrope_positions(scheduler_output)
3316 self.mrope_positions.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
3317
3318 return mm_embeds, is_mm_embed
3319
3320 def get_model(self) -> nn.Module:
3321 if not hasattr(self, "model"):
3322 raise ValueError("Cannot get model before model has been initialized")
3323 if isinstance(
3324 self.model, (CUDAGraphWrapper, UBatchWrapper, BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper)
3325 ):
3326 # get raw model out of the cudagraph wrapper.
3327 return self.model.unwrap()
3328 return self.model
3329
3330 def get_draft_model(self) -> nn.Module | None:
3331 drafter = getattr(self, "drafter", None)
3332 if drafter is None:
3333 return None
3334 model = getattr(drafter, "model", None)
3335 if isinstance(
3336 model, (CUDAGraphWrapper, UBatchWrapper, BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper)
3337 ):
3338 return cast(nn.Module, model.unwrap())
3339 return cast(nn.Module | None, model)
3340
3341 def get_supported_generation_tasks(self) -> list[GenerationTask]:
3342 model = self.get_model()
3343 supported_tasks = list[GenerationTask]()
3344
3345 if is_text_generation_model(model):
3346 supported_tasks.append("generate")
3347
3348 if supports_transcription(model):
3349 if model.supports_transcription_only:
3350 return ["transcription"]
3351
3352 supported_tasks.append("transcription")
3353
3354 if supports_realtime(model):
3355 supported_tasks.append("realtime")
3356
3357 return supported_tasks
3358
3359 def get_supported_pooling_tasks(self) -> list[PoolingTask]:
3360 model = self.get_model()
3361 if not is_pooling_model(model):
3362 return []
3363
3364 return list(model.pooler.get_supported_tasks())
3365
3366 def get_supported_tasks(self) -> tuple[SupportedTask, ...]:
3367 tasks = list[SupportedTask]()
3368
3369 if self.model_config.runner_type == "generate":
3370 tasks.extend(self.get_supported_generation_tasks())
3371 if self.model_config.runner_type == "pooling":
3372 tasks.extend(self.get_supported_pooling_tasks())
3373
3374 return tuple(tasks)

3376 def sync_and_gather_intermediate_tensors(
3377 self,
3378 num_tokens: int,
3379 intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None,
3380 sync_self: bool,
3381 ) -> IntermediateTensors:
3382 assert self.intermediate_tensors is not None
3383
3384 tp = self.vllm_config.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size
3385 is_rs = is_residual_scattered_for_sp(self.vllm_config, num_tokens)
3386
3387 # When sequence parallelism is enabled, the "residual" tensor is
3388 # sharded across TP ranks. All-gather it here because downstream
3389 # QKV + Attention needs the full residual before the SP split point.
3390 if sync_self:
3391 assert intermediate_tensors is not None
3392 for k, v in intermediate_tensors.items():
3393 is_scattered = k == "residual" and is_rs
3394 if is_scattered:
3395 local_len = num_tokens // tp
3396 v = get_tp_group().all_gather(v[:local_len], dim=0)
3397
3398 self.intermediate_tensors[k][:num_tokens].copy_(
3399 v[:num_tokens], non_blocking=True
3400 )
3401
3402 return IntermediateTensors(
3403 {k: v[:num_tokens] for k, v in self.intermediate_tensors.items()}
3404 )

3406 def eplb_step(self, is_dummy: bool = False, is_profile: bool = False) -> None:
3407 """
3408 Step for the EPLB (Expert Parallelism Load Balancing) state.
3409 """
3410 if not self.parallel_config.enable_eplb or self.eep_eplb_suppressed:
3411 return
3412
3413 assert self.eplb_state is not None
3414 assert self._moe_model is not None
3415 self.eplb_state.step(
3416 is_dummy,
3417 is_profile,
3418 log_stats=self.parallel_config.eplb_config.log_balancedness,
3419 )

3425 assert self.eplb_state is not None
3426 self.eplb_state.update_mapping(

3428 expanded_physical_to_logical,

3431 def _pool(
3432 self,
3433 hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
3434 num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3435 num_scheduled_tokens_np: np.ndarray,
3436 kv_connector_output: KVConnectorOutput | None,
3437 ) -> ModelRunnerOutput | AsyncModelRunnerOutput:
3438 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
3439 assert num_reqs == len(self.input_batch.pooling_params), (
3440 "Either all or none of the requests in a batch must be pooling request"
3441 )
3442
3443 hidden_states = hidden_states[:num_scheduled_tokens]
3444 seq_lens_cpu = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs]
3445
3446 pooling_metadata = self.input_batch.get_pooling_metadata()
3447 pooling_metadata.build_pooling_cursor(
3448 num_scheduled_tokens_np,
3449 seq_lens_cpu,
3450 device=hidden_states.device,
3451 query_start_loc_gpu=self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs + 1],
3452 )
3453
3454 model = cast(VllmModelForPooling, self.model)
3455 raw_pooler_output: PoolerOutput = model.pooler(
3456 hidden_states=hidden_states, pooling_metadata=pooling_metadata
3457 )
3458
3459 finished_mask = pooling_metadata.get_pooling_cursor().get_finished_mask()
3460 raw_pooler_output = self.late_interaction_runner.postprocess_pooler_output(
3461 raw_pooler_output=raw_pooler_output,
3462 pooling_params=pooling_metadata.pooling_params,
3463 req_ids=self.input_batch.req_ids,
3464 finished_mask=finished_mask,
3465 )
3466
3467 model_runner_output = ModelRunnerOutput(
3468 req_ids=self.input_batch.req_ids.copy(),
3469 req_id_to_index=self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.copy(),
3470 kv_connector_output=kv_connector_output,
3471 )
3472
3473 if raw_pooler_output is None or not any(finished_mask):
3474 self._sync_device()
3475 model_runner_output.pooler_output = [None] * num_reqs
3476 return model_runner_output
3477
3478 if not current_platform.is_cuda_alike():
3479 # cpu/xpu runners cannot use the CUDA stream/event-based wrapper.
3480 model_runner_output.pooler_output = _copy_pooler_output_to_cpu(
3481 raw_pooler_output=raw_pooler_output,
3482 finished_mask=finished_mask,
3483 )
3484 self._sync_device()
3485 return model_runner_output
3486
3487 return AsyncGPUPoolingModelRunnerOutput(
3488 model_runner_output=model_runner_output,
3489 raw_pooler_output=raw_pooler_output,
3490 finished_mask=finished_mask,
3491 async_output_copy_stream=self._get_or_create_async_output_copy_stream(),
3492 )
3493
3494 def _pad_for_sequence_parallelism(self, num_scheduled_tokens: int) -> int:
3495 # Pad tokens to multiple of tensor_parallel_size when
3496 # enabled collective fusion for SP
3497 tp_size = self.vllm_config.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size
3498 if self.compilation_config.pass_config.enable_sp and tp_size > 1:
3499 return round_up(num_scheduled_tokens, tp_size)
3500 return num_scheduled_tokens
3501
3502 def _prepare_mm_inputs(
3503 self, num_tokens: int
3504 ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor]:
3505 if self.model.requires_raw_input_tokens:
3506 input_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_tokens]
3507 else:
3508 input_ids = None
3509
3510 inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_tokens]
3511 return input_ids, inputs_embeds
3512
3513 def _preprocess(
3514 self,
3515 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
3516 num_input_tokens: int, # Padded
3517 intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None = None,
3518 ) -> tuple[
3519 torch.Tensor | None,
3520 torch.Tensor | None,
3521 torch.Tensor,
3522 IntermediateTensors | None,
3523 dict[str, Any],
3524 ECConnectorOutput | None,
3525 ]:
3526 num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
3527 is_first_rank = get_pp_group().is_first_rank
3528 is_encoder_decoder = self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder
3529
3530 # Clamp speculative scheduler placeholders (-1) before embedding lookup.
3531 if self.speculative_config is not None:
3532 self.input_ids.gpu[:num_input_tokens].clamp_(min=0)
3533
3534 # _prepare_inputs may reorder the batch, so we must gather multi
3535 # modal outputs after that to ensure the correct order
3536 ec_connector_output = None
3537
3538 if self.supports_mm_inputs and is_first_rank and not is_encoder_decoder:
3539 # Run the multimodal encoder if any.
3540 with self.maybe_get_ec_connector_output(
3541 scheduler_output,
3542 encoder_cache=self.encoder_cache,
3543 ) as ec_connector_output:
3544 self._execute_mm_encoder(scheduler_output)
3545 mm_embeds, is_mm_embed = self._gather_mm_embeddings(scheduler_output)
3546
3547 # NOTE(woosuk): To unify token ids and soft tokens (vision
3548 # embeddings), we always use embeddings (rather than token ids)
3549 # as input to the multimodal model, even when the input is text.
3550 if self.enable_prompt_embeds and self.input_batch.req_prompt_embeds:
3551 # Some positions carry precomputed prompt_embeds: they are
3552 # already in self.inputs_embeds and marked is_token_ids=False.
3553 # Embed only the token-id positions (zeroing the placeholder ids
3554 # at prompt_embeds positions so the embedding gather cannot read
3555 # out-of-range ids), and write them back without clobbering the
3556 # prompt_embeds positions.
3557 is_token_ids = self.is_token_ids.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens]
3558 safe_input_ids = torch.where(
3559 is_token_ids,
3560 self.input_ids.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens],
3561 0,
3562 )
3563 inputs_embeds_scheduled = self.model.embed_input_ids(
3564 safe_input_ids,
3565 multimodal_embeddings=mm_embeds,
3566 is_multimodal=is_mm_embed,
3567 )
3568 target = self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens]
3569 self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens] = torch.where(
3570 is_token_ids.unsqueeze(-1),
3571 inputs_embeds_scheduled,
3572 target,
3573 )
3574 else:
3575 inputs_embeds_scheduled = self.model.embed_input_ids(
3576 self.input_ids.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens],
3577 multimodal_embeddings=mm_embeds,
3578 is_multimodal=is_mm_embed,
3579 )
3580
3581 # TODO(woosuk): Avoid the copy. Optimize.
3582 self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(
3583 inputs_embeds_scheduled
3584 )
3585
3586 input_ids, inputs_embeds = self._prepare_mm_inputs(num_input_tokens)
3587 model_kwargs = {
3588 **self._init_model_kwargs(),
3589 **self._extract_mm_kwargs(scheduler_output),
3590 }
3591 elif self.enable_prompt_embeds and is_first_rank:
3592 # Get the input embeddings for the tokens that are not input embeds,
3593 # then put them into the appropriate positions.
3594 # TODO(qthequartermasterman): Since even when prompt embeds are
3595 # enabled, (a) not all requests will use prompt embeds, and (b)
3596 # after the initial prompt is processed, the rest of the generated
3597 # tokens will be token ids, it is not desirable to have the
3598 # embedding layer outside of the CUDA graph all the time. The v0
3599 # engine avoids this by "double compiling" the CUDA graph, once
3600 # with input_ids and again with inputs_embeds, for all num_tokens.
3601 # If a batch only has token ids, then including the embedding layer
3602 # in the CUDA graph will be more performant (like in the else case
3603 # below).
3604 is_token_ids = self.is_token_ids.np[:num_scheduled_tokens]
3605 token_ids_idx_np = np.nonzero(is_token_ids)[0]
3606 # Some tokens ids may need to become embeds
3607 if token_ids_idx_np.size > 0:
3608 token_ids_idx = async_tensor_h2d(token_ids_idx_np, device=self.device)
3609 token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[token_ids_idx]
3610 tokens_to_embeds = self.model.embed_input_ids(input_ids=token_ids)
3611 self.inputs_embeds.gpu[token_ids_idx] = tokens_to_embeds
3612
3613 inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_input_tokens]
3614 model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs()
3615 input_ids = None
3616 else:
3617 # For text-only models, we use token ids as input.
3618 # While it is possible to use embeddings as input just like the
3619 # multimodal models, it is not desirable for performance since
3620 # then the embedding layer is not included in the CUDA graph.
3621 input_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_input_tokens]
3622 inputs_embeds = None
3623 model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs()
3624
3625 if self.uses_mrope:
3626 positions = self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_input_tokens]
3627 else:
3628 positions = self.positions[:num_input_tokens]
3629 if num_input_tokens > num_scheduled_tokens:
3630 self.positions[num_scheduled_tokens:num_input_tokens].zero_()
3631
3632 if is_first_rank:
3633 intermediate_tensors = None
3634 else:
3635 assert intermediate_tensors is not None
3636 intermediate_tensors = self.sync_and_gather_intermediate_tensors(
3637 num_input_tokens, intermediate_tensors, True
3638 )
3639
3640 if is_encoder_decoder and scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs:
3641 # Run the encoder, just like we do with other multimodal inputs.
3642 # For an encoder-decoder model, our processing here is a bit
3643 # simpler, because the outputs are just passed to the decoder.
3644 # We are not doing any prompt replacement. We also will only
3645 # ever have a single encoder input.
3646 encoder_outputs = self._execute_mm_encoder(scheduler_output)
3647 model_kwargs.update({"encoder_outputs": encoder_outputs})
3648
3649 return (
3650 input_ids,
3651 inputs_embeds,
3652 positions,
3653 intermediate_tensors,
3654 model_kwargs,
3655 ec_connector_output,
3656 )
3657
3658 def _sample(
3659 self,
3660 logits: torch.Tensor | None,
3661 spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata | None,
3662 ) -> SamplerOutput:
3663 # Sample the next token and get logprobs if needed.
3664 sampling_metadata = self.input_batch.sampling_metadata
3665 # Update output token ids with tokens sampled in last step
3666 # if async scheduling and required by current sampling params.
3667 self.input_batch.update_async_output_token_ids()
3668 if spec_decode_metadata is None:
3669 return self.sampler(
3670 logits=logits,
3671 sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,
3672 )
3673
3674 # Update spec_token_ids with real draft tokens from pre step only when
3675 # output_token_ids is needed (penalties or bad_words are in use).
3676 if self.use_async_scheduling and self._draft_token_req_ids is not None:
3677 draft_token_ids_cpu, _ = self._get_draft_token_ids_cpu()
3678 self.input_batch.update_async_spec_token_ids(draft_token_ids_cpu)
3679
3680 draft_probs = self._get_spec_decode_draft_probs(spec_decode_metadata)
3681 sampler_output = self.rejection_sampler(
3682 spec_decode_metadata,
3683 draft_probs,
3684 logits,
3685 sampling_metadata,
3686 )
3687 return sampler_output
3688
3689 def _bookkeeping_sync(
3690 self,
3691 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
3692 sampler_output: SamplerOutput,
3693 logits: torch.Tensor | None,
3694 hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
3695 num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3696 ) -> tuple[
3697 dict[str, int],
3698 torch.Tensor | None,
3699 LogprobsLists | None,
3700 list[list[int]],
3701 dict[str, LogprobsTensors | None],
3702 list[str],
3703 dict[str, int],
3704 list[int],
3705 ]:
3706 num_nans: torch.Tensor | None = None
3707 num_nans_in_logits: dict[str, int] = {}
3708 if envs.VLLM_COMPUTE_NANS_IN_LOGITS:
3709 if self.use_async_scheduling:
3710 # Keep the counts on device; they ride the async output copy
3711 # stream rather than blocking here.
3712 num_nans = None if logits is None else count_nans_per_row(logits)
3713 else:
3714 num_nans_in_logits = self._get_nans_in_logits(logits)
3715
3716 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
3717 discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices = np.nonzero(
3718 self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs]
3719 )[0]
3720 for i in discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices:
3721 gen = self.input_batch.generators.get(int(i))
3722 if gen is not None:
3723 gen.set_offset(gen.get_offset() - 4)
3724
3725 # Copy some objects so they don't get modified after returning.
3726 # This is important when using async scheduling.
3727 req_ids_output_copy = self.input_batch.req_ids.copy()
3728 req_id_to_index_output_copy = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.copy()
3729
3730 num_sampled_tokens = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids.shape[0]
3731 sampled_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
3732 logprobs_tensors = sampler_output.logprobs_tensors
3733 invalid_req_indices = []
3734 logprobs_lists = None
3735 if not self.use_async_scheduling:
3736 # Sync scheduling: issue routed experts D2H into the pinned
3737 # CPU buffer BEFORE ``_to_list`` below. ``_to_list`` does
3738 # ``event.synchronize()`` on the async copy stream which
3739 # waits for every D2H queued on the default stream since
3740 # the last sync, so this enqueue is naturally covered
3741 # without requiring its own synchronize.
3742 if self.routed_experts_initialized:
3743 buf = self.routed_experts_capturer.get_device_buffer()
3744 total = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
3745 self.routed_experts_cpu[:total].copy_(buf[:total], non_blocking=True)
3746 self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_cpu[:total].copy_(
3747 self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device[:total],
3748 non_blocking=True,
3749 )
3750 with gpu_sync_allowed():
3751 # Get the valid generated tokens.
3752 max_gen_len = sampled_token_ids.shape[-1]
3753 if max_gen_len == 1:
3754 # No spec decode tokens.
3755 valid_sampled_token_ids = self._to_list(sampled_token_ids)
3756 # Mask out the sampled tokens that should not be sampled.
3757 for i in discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices:
3758 valid_sampled_token_ids[int(i)].clear()
3759
3760 if logprobs_tensors is not None:
3761 logprobs_lists = logprobs_tensors.tolists()
3762 else:
3763 # Includes spec decode tokens.
3764 valid_sampled_token_ids, logprobs_lists = (
3765 RejectionSampler.parse_output(
3766 sampled_token_ids,
3767 self.input_batch.vocab_size,
3768 discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices,
3769 logprobs_tensors=logprobs_tensors,
3770 )
3771 )
3772 else:
3773 valid_sampled_token_ids = []
3774 invalid_req_indices = discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices.tolist()
3775 invalid_req_indices_set = set(invalid_req_indices)
3776
3777 # Cache the sampled tokens on the GPU and avoid CPU sync.
3778 # These will be copied into input_ids in the next step
3779 # when preparing inputs.
3780 # With spec decoding, this is done in propose_draft_token_ids().
3781 if self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids is None:
3782 assert sampled_token_ids.shape[-1] == 1
3783 self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = sampled_token_ids
3784 self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index = {
3785 req_id: i
3786 for i, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids)
3787 if i not in invalid_req_indices_set
3788 }
3789
3790 # Cache the sampled tokens in the model runner, so that the scheduler
3791 # doesn't need to send them back.
3792 # NOTE(woosuk): As an exception, when using PP, the scheduler sends
3793 # the sampled tokens back, because there's no direct communication
3794 # between the first-stage worker and the last-stage worker.
3795 req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids
3796 for req_idx in range(num_sampled_tokens):
3797 if self.use_async_scheduling:
3798 sampled_ids = [-1] if req_idx not in invalid_req_indices_set else None
3799 else:
3800 sampled_ids = valid_sampled_token_ids[req_idx]
3801
3802 num_sampled_ids: int = len(sampled_ids) if sampled_ids else 0
3803
3804 if not sampled_ids:
3805 continue
3806
3807 start_idx = self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_idx]
3808 end_idx = start_idx + num_sampled_ids
3809 assert end_idx <= self.max_model_len, (
3810 "Sampled token IDs exceed the max model length. "
3811 f"Total number of tokens: {end_idx} > max_model_len: "
3812 f"{self.max_model_len}"
3813 )
3814
3815 self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu[req_idx, start_idx:end_idx] = sampled_ids
3816 self.input_batch.is_token_ids[req_idx, start_idx:end_idx] = True
3817 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_idx] = end_idx
3818
3819 req_id = req_ids[req_idx]
3820 req_state = self.requests[req_id]
3821 req_state.output_token_ids.extend(sampled_ids)
3822
3823 # Compute prompt logprobs if needed.
3824 prompt_logprobs_dict = self._get_prompt_logprobs_dict(
3825 hidden_states[:num_scheduled_tokens],
3826 scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens,
3827 )
3828
3829 return (
3830 num_nans_in_logits,
3831 num_nans,
3832 logprobs_lists,
3833 valid_sampled_token_ids,
3834 prompt_logprobs_dict,
3835 req_ids_output_copy,
3836 req_id_to_index_output_copy,
3837 invalid_req_indices,
3838 )
3839
3840 @contextmanager
3841 def synchronize_input_prep(self):
3842 if self.prepare_inputs_event is None:
3843 yield
3844 return
3845
3846 # Ensure prior step has finished with reused CPU tensors.
3847 # This is required in the async scheduling case because
3848 # the CPU->GPU transfer happens async.
3849 self.prepare_inputs_event.synchronize()
3850 try:
3851 yield
3852 finally:
3853 self.prepare_inputs_event.record()
3854
3855 def _model_forward(
3856 self,
3857 input_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
3858 positions: torch.Tensor | None = None,
3859 intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None = None,
3860 inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor | None = None,
3861 **model_kwargs: dict[str, Any],
3862 ) -> Any:
3863 """Helper method to call the model forward pass.
3864
3865 This method can be overridden by subclasses for model execution.
3866 Motivation: We can inspect only this method versus
3867 the whole execute_model, which has additional logic.
3868
3869 Args:
3870 input_ids: Input token IDs
3871 positions: Token positions
3872 intermediate_tensors: Tensors from previous pipeline stages
3873 inputs_embeds: Input embeddings (alternative to input_ids)
3874 **model_kwargs: Additional model arguments
3875
3876 Returns:
3877 Model output tensor
3878 """
3879 return self.model(
3880 input_ids=input_ids,
3881 positions=positions,
3882 intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,
3883 inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
3884 **model_kwargs,
3885 )
3886
3887 @staticmethod
3888 def _is_uniform_decode(
3889 max_num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3890 uniform_decode_query_len: int,
3891 num_tokens: int,
3892 num_reqs: int,
3893 force_uniform_decode: bool | None = None,
3894 ) -> bool:
3895 """
3896 Checks if it's a decode batch with same amount scheduled tokens
3897 across all requests.
3898 """
3899 return (
3900 (
3901 (max_num_scheduled_tokens == uniform_decode_query_len)
3902 and (num_tokens == max_num_scheduled_tokens * num_reqs)
3903 )
3904 if force_uniform_decode is None
3905 else force_uniform_decode
3906 )
3907
3908 def _allow_microbatching(
3909 self, num_reqs: int, num_scheduled_tokens_np: np.ndarray
3910 ) -> bool:
3911 """Refuse to microbatch a step that splits a prefix from its writer.
3912
3913 A request can be admitted on a prefix cache hit against blocks another
3914 request in the same batch is only computing now. Run whole, the step
3915 issues every KV cache write before any attention read and the hit
3916 holds; split, a reader in the first half would attend over blocks the
3917 writer in the second half has not filled in yet. Vetoing on one rank
3918 settles it for all, since ranks agree on microbatching collectively.
3919 """
3920 if not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching or num_reqs < 2:
3921 return True
3922 computed = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs]
3923 query_lens = num_scheduled_tokens_np[:num_reqs]
3924 # A decode reads nothing it was not already given in an earlier step.
3925 readers = np.flatnonzero(
3926 (query_lens > (self.reorder_batch_threshold or 1)) & (computed > 0)
3927 )
3928 if readers.size == 0:
3929 return True
3930
3931 for block_table in self.input_batch.block_table.block_tables:
3932 block_size = block_table.block_size
3933 table = block_table.get_numpy_array()
3934 start = computed // block_size
3935 stop = (computed + query_lens + block_size - 1) // block_size
3936 span = int((stop - start).max())
3937 columns = start[:, None] + np.arange(span)[None, :]
3938 written = np.unique(
3939 np.take_along_axis(
3940 table[:num_reqs],
3941 np.minimum(columns, table.shape[1] - 1),
3942 axis=1,
3943 )[columns < stop[:, None]]
3944 )
3945 for reader in readers:
3946 # Whole blocks only: a reader ends inside a partly filled one,
3947 # which is private to it and which it fills before reading.
3948 whole = computed[reader] // block_size
3949 if np.isin(table[reader, :whole], written).any():
3950 return False
3951 return True
3952
3953 def _determine_batch_execution_and_padding(
3954 self,
3955 num_tokens: int,
3956 num_reqs: int,
3957 num_scheduled_tokens_np: np.ndarray,
3958 max_num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3959 use_cascade_attn: bool,
3960 allow_microbatching: bool = True,
3961 force_eager: bool = False,
3962 # For cudagraph capture TODO(lucas): Refactor how we capture cudagraphs (will
3963 # be improved in model runner v2)
3964 force_uniform_decode: bool | None = None,
3965 force_has_lora: bool | None = None,
3966 force_num_active_loras: int | None = None,
3967 num_encoder_reqs: int = 0,
3968 ) -> tuple[
3969 CUDAGraphMode,
3970 BatchDescriptor,
3971 bool,
3972 torch.Tensor | None,
3973 CUDAGraphStat | None,
3974 ]:
3975 uniform_decode = self._is_uniform_decode(
3976 max_num_scheduled_tokens=max_num_scheduled_tokens,
3977 uniform_decode_query_len=self.uniform_decode_query_len,
3978 num_tokens=num_tokens,
3979 num_reqs=num_reqs,
3980 force_uniform_decode=force_uniform_decode,
3981 )
3982 # Encoder-decoder models only support CG for decoder_step > 0 (no enc_output
3983 # is present). Also, chunked-prefill is disabled, so batch are uniform.
3984 has_encoder_output = (
3985 self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder and num_encoder_reqs > 0
3986 )
3987
3988 # Compute LoRA state for cudagraph dispatch
3989 num_active_loras = (
3990 force_num_active_loras
3991 if force_num_active_loras is not None
3992 else len(self.input_batch.lora_id_to_lora_request)
3993 )
3994 has_lora = num_active_loras > 0 if force_has_lora is None else force_has_lora
3995
3996 num_tokens_padded = self._pad_for_sequence_parallelism(num_tokens)
3997
3998 def dispatch_cudagraph(num_tokens, disable_full=False, valid_modes=None):
3999 return self.cudagraph_dispatcher.dispatch(
4000 num_tokens=num_tokens,
4001 has_lora=has_lora,
4002 uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
4003 num_active_loras=num_active_loras,
4004 valid_modes={CUDAGraphMode.NONE} if force_eager else valid_modes,
4005 invalid_modes={CUDAGraphMode.FULL} if disable_full else None,
4006 )
4007
4008 cudagraph_mode, batch_descriptor = dispatch_cudagraph(
4009 num_tokens_padded, disable_full=use_cascade_attn or has_encoder_output
4010 )
4011 num_tokens_padded = batch_descriptor.num_tokens
4012 if self.compilation_config.pass_config.enable_sp:
4013 assert (
4014 batch_descriptor.num_tokens
4015 % self.vllm_config.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size
4016 == 0
4017 ), (
4018 "Sequence parallelism requires num_tokens to be "
4019 "a multiple of tensor parallel size"
4020 )
4021
4022 # Extra coordination when running data-parallel since we need to coordinate
4023 # across ranks
4024 should_ubatch, num_tokens_across_dp = False, None
4025 if self.vllm_config.parallel_config.data_parallel_size > 1:
4026 should_ubatch, num_tokens_across_dp, synced_cudagraph_mode = (
4027 coordinate_batch_across_dp(
4028 num_tokens_unpadded=num_tokens,
4029 parallel_config=self.parallel_config,
4030 allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
4031 num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded,
4032 uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
4033 cudagraph_mode=cudagraph_mode.value,
4034 )
4035 )
4036
4037 # Extract DP-synced values
4038 if num_tokens_across_dp is not None:
4039 dp_rank = self.parallel_config.data_parallel_rank
4040 num_tokens_padded = int(num_tokens_across_dp[dp_rank].item())
4041 # Re-dispatch with DP padding so we have the correct batch_descriptor
4042 cudagraph_mode, batch_descriptor = dispatch_cudagraph(
4043 num_tokens_padded,
4044 valid_modes={CUDAGraphMode(synced_cudagraph_mode)},
4045 )
4046 # Assert to make sure the agreed upon token count is correct otherwise
4047 # num_tokens_across_dp will no-longer be valid
4048 assert batch_descriptor.num_tokens == num_tokens_padded
4049
4050 cudagraph_stats = None
4051 if self.vllm_config.observability_config.cudagraph_metrics:
4052 cudagraph_stats = CUDAGraphStat(
4053 num_unpadded_tokens=num_tokens,
4054 num_padded_tokens=batch_descriptor.num_tokens,
4055 num_paddings=batch_descriptor.num_tokens - num_tokens,
4056 runtime_mode=str(cudagraph_mode),
4057 )
4058
4059 return (
4060 cudagraph_mode,
4061 batch_descriptor,
4062 should_ubatch,
4063 num_tokens_across_dp,
4064 cudagraph_stats,
4065 )
4066
4067 def _register_layerwise_nvtx_hooks(self) -> None:
4068 """
4069 Register layerwise NVTX hooks if --enable-layerwise-nvtx-tracing is enabled
4070 to trace detailed information of each layer or module in the model.
4071 """
4072
4073 if (
4074 self.vllm_config.observability_config.enable_layerwise_nvtx_tracing
4075 and not self.layerwise_nvtx_hooks_registered
4076 ):
4077 if self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE:
4078 logger.debug_once(
4079 "layerwise NVTX tracing is not supported when CUDA graph is "
4080 "turned off; you may observe part or all of the model "
4081 "missing NVTX markers"
4082 )
4083
4084 # In STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE mode, after registering hooks here,
4085 # the __call__ function of nn.module will be recompiled with
4086 # fullgraph=True. Since nvtx.range_push/pop are not traceable
4087 # by torch dynamo, we can't register hook functions here
4088 # because hook functions will also be traced by torch dynamo.
4089 if (
4090 self.vllm_config.compilation_config.mode
4091 == CompilationMode.STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE
4092 ):
4093 logger.debug_once(
4094 "layerwise NVTX tracing is not supported when "
4095 "CompilationMode is STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE, skipping "
4096 "function hooks registration"
4097 )
4098 else:
4099 pyt_hooks = PytHooks()
4100 pyt_hooks.register_hooks(self.model, self.model.__class__.__name__)
4101 self.layerwise_nvtx_hooks_registered = True
4102
4103 def _get_slot_mappings(
4104 self,
4105 num_tokens_padded: int,
4106 num_reqs_padded: int,
4107 num_tokens_unpadded: int,
4108 ubatch_slices: "UBatchSlices | None" = None,
4109 ) -> tuple[
4110 dict[int, torch.Tensor] | None,
4111 dict[str, torch.Tensor] | list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None,
4112 ]:
4113 """
4114 Build slot mappings in both formats needed by the system.
4115
4116 Args:
4117 num_tokens_padded: Total number of tokens (padded)
4118 num_reqs_padded: Total number of requests (padded)
4119 num_tokens_unpadded: Actual number of tokens (unpadded)
4120 ubatch_slices: Optional ubatch slicing info for DBO
4121
4122 Returns:
4123 A tuple of:
4124 - slot_mappings_by_gid: dict[int, torch.Tensor] for attention metadata
4125 - slot_mappings_by_layer: dict[str, torch.Tensor] or list for ForwardContext
4126 """
4127 if not (
4128 hasattr(self, "kv_cache_config")
4129 and self.kv_cache_config is not None
4130 and len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups) > 0
4131 ):
4132 return None, None
4133
4134 def _get_slot_mapping(kv_cache_gid: int):
4135 assert num_reqs_padded is not None and num_tokens_padded is not None
4136 kv_cache_spec = self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups[
4137 kv_cache_gid
4138 ].kv_cache_spec
4139 if isinstance(kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec):
4140 slot_mapping = torch.zeros(
4141 (num_tokens_padded,),
4142 dtype=torch.int64,
4143 device=self.device,
4144 )
4145 else:
4146 blk_table = self.input_batch.block_table[kv_cache_gid]
4147 slot_mapping = blk_table.slot_mapping.gpu[:num_tokens_padded]
4148
4149 # Fill unused with -1. Needed for reshape_and_cache in full cuda
4150 # graph mode. `blk_table_tensor` -1 to match mamba PAD_SLOT_ID
4151 slot_mapping[num_tokens_unpadded:num_tokens_padded].fill_(-1)
4152
4153 return slot_mapping
4154
4155 slot_mappings_by_gid = {
4156 gid: _get_slot_mapping(gid)
4157 for gid, _ in enumerate(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)
4158 }
4159
4160 slot_mappings_by_layer: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
4161 for gid, kv_cache_group in enumerate(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups):
4162 slot_mapping = slot_mappings_by_gid[gid]
4163 for layer_name in kv_cache_group.layer_names:
4164 slot_mappings_by_layer[layer_name] = slot_mapping
4165
4166 if ubatch_slices is not None:
4167 result: list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] = []
4168 for ubatch in ubatch_slices:
4169 sliced_mappings: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
4170 for layer_name, slot_mapping in slot_mappings_by_layer.items():
4171 sliced_mappings[layer_name] = slot_mapping[ubatch.token_slice]
4172 result.append(sliced_mappings)
4173 return slot_mappings_by_gid, result
4174
4175 return slot_mappings_by_gid, slot_mappings_by_layer
4176
4177 def _is_all_reqs_chunked_prefill(self) -> bool:
4178 """Check if all scheduled requests are marked to discard sampled tokens.
4179
4180 This is true when `discard_request_mask` is set for every scheduled
4181 request (e.g., for chunked prefill requests that are not the last
4182 prefill chunk)."""
4183 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
4184 return bool(self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs].all())
4185
4186 @torch.inference_mode()
4187 def execute_model(
4188 self,
4189 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
4190 intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None = None,
4191 ) -> ModelRunnerOutput | AsyncModelRunnerOutput | IntermediateTensors | None:
4192 if self.execute_model_state is not None:
4193 raise RuntimeError(
4194 "State error: sample_tokens() must be called "
4195 "after execute_model() returns None."
4196 )
4197
4198 # If ngram_gpu is used, we need to copy the scheduler_output to avoid
4199 # the modification has influence on the scheduler_output in engine core process.
4200 # The replace is much faster than deepcopy.
4201 if (
4202 self.speculative_config is not None
4203 and self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu()
4204 ):
4205 num_scheduled_tokens_copy = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens.copy()
4206 spec_decode_tokens_copy = (
4207 scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens.copy()
4208 )
4209 scheduler_output = replace(
4210 scheduler_output,
4211 num_scheduled_tokens=num_scheduled_tokens_copy,
4212 scheduled_spec_decode_tokens=spec_decode_tokens_copy,
4213 )
4214
4215 if has_kv_transfer_group():
4216 kv_connector_metadata = scheduler_output.kv_connector_metadata
4217 assert kv_connector_metadata is not None
4218 get_kv_transfer_group().handle_preemptions(kv_connector_metadata)
4219
4220 num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4221 with (
4222 record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: preprocess"),
4223 self.synchronize_input_prep(),
4224 ):
4225 # Update persistent batch states.
4226 deferred_state_corrections_fn = self._update_states(scheduler_output)
4227
4228 if has_ec_transfer() and not get_ec_transfer().is_consumer:
4229 with self.maybe_get_ec_connector_output(
4230 scheduler_output,
4231 encoder_cache=self.encoder_cache,
4232 ) as ec_connector_output:
4233 self._execute_mm_encoder(scheduler_output)
4234 return make_empty_encoder_model_runner_output(scheduler_output)
4235
4236 if not num_scheduled_tokens:
4237 if (
4238 self.parallel_config.distributed_executor_backend
4239 == "external_launcher"
4240 and self.parallel_config.data_parallel_size > 1
4241 ):
4242 # this is a corner case when both external launcher
4243 # and DP are enabled, num_scheduled_tokens could be
4244 # 0, and has_unfinished_requests in the outer loop
4245 # returns True. before returning early here we call
4246 # dummy run to ensure coordinate_batch_across_dp
4247 # is called into to avoid out of sync issues.
4248 self._dummy_run(1)
4249 if not has_kv_transfer_group():
4250 # Return empty ModelRunnerOutput if no work to do.
4251 return EMPTY_MODEL_RUNNER_OUTPUT
4252 return self.kv_connector_no_forward(scheduler_output, self.vllm_config)
4253
4254 if self.cache_config.kv_sharing_fast_prefill:
4255 assert not self.num_prompt_logprobs, (
4256 "--kv-sharing-fast-prefill produces incorrect "
4257 "logprobs for prompt tokens, tokens, please disable "
4258 "it when the requests need prompt logprobs"
4259 )
4260
4261 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
4262 req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids
4263 tokens = [scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[i] for i in req_ids]
4264 num_scheduled_tokens_np = np.array(tokens, dtype=np.int32)
4265 max_num_scheduled_tokens = int(num_scheduled_tokens_np.max())
4266 num_tokens_unpadded = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4267
4268 logits_indices, spec_decode_metadata, max_num_sampled_tokens = (
4269 self._prepare_inputs(scheduler_output, num_scheduled_tokens_np)
4270 )
4271
4272 cascade_attn_prefix_lens = None
4273 # Disable cascade attention when using microbatching (DBO)
4274 if self.cascade_attn_enabled and not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching:
4275 # Pre-compute cascade attention prefix lengths
4276 cascade_attn_prefix_lens = self._compute_cascade_attn_prefix_lens(
4277 num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4278 self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs],
4279 scheduler_output.num_common_prefix_blocks,
4280 )
4281
4282 (
4283 cudagraph_mode,
4284 batch_desc,
4285 should_ubatch,
4286 num_tokens_across_dp,
4287 cudagraph_stats,
4288 ) = self._determine_batch_execution_and_padding(
4289 num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
4290 num_reqs=num_reqs,
4291 num_scheduled_tokens_np=num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4292 max_num_scheduled_tokens=max_num_scheduled_tokens,
4293 use_cascade_attn=cascade_attn_prefix_lens is not None,
4294 num_encoder_reqs=len(scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs),
4295 allow_microbatching=self._allow_microbatching(
4296 num_reqs, num_scheduled_tokens_np
4297 ),
4298 )
4299
4300 logger.debug(
4301 "Running batch with cudagraph_mode: %s, batch_descriptor: %s, "
4302 "should_ubatch: %s, num_tokens_across_dp: %s",
4303 cudagraph_mode,
4304 batch_desc,
4305 should_ubatch,
4306 num_tokens_across_dp,
4307 )
4308
4309 num_tokens_padded = batch_desc.num_tokens
4310 num_reqs_padded = (
4311 batch_desc.num_reqs if batch_desc.num_reqs is not None else num_reqs
4312 )
4313 ubatch_slices, ubatch_slices_padded = maybe_create_ubatch_slices(
4314 should_ubatch,
4315 num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4316 num_tokens_padded,
4317 num_reqs_padded,
4318 self.parallel_config.num_ubatches,
4319 )
4320
4321 logger.debug(
4322 "ubatch_slices: %s, ubatch_slices_padded: %s",
4323 ubatch_slices,
4324 ubatch_slices_padded,
4325 )
4326
4327 # True if any attention backend handles KV cache update separately
4328 # from forward() (i.e., forward_includes_kv_cache_update=False). When true,
4329 # slot_mappings must use padded dimensions to match the key/value tensors.
4330 has_separate_kv_update = not all(
4331 all(
4332 g.backend.forward_includes_kv_cache_update
4333 for g in self.attn_groups[id]
4334 )
4335 for id, spec in enumerate(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)
4336 if not isinstance(spec.kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec)
4337 )
4338 pad_attn = cudagraph_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
4339
4340 if self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "align":
4341 # preprocess_mamba reads req_state.num_computed_tokens (CPU)
4342 # to decide copy operations, so we must apply deferred
4343 # corrections before it runs.
4344 if deferred_state_corrections_fn:
4345 deferred_state_corrections_fn()
4346 deferred_state_corrections_fn = None
4347 mamba_bufs = self._get_mamba_bufs()
4348 mamba_utils.preprocess_mamba(
4349 scheduler_output,
4350 self.kv_cache_config,
4351 self.cache_config,
4352 self.mamba_state_idx,
4353 self.input_batch,
4354 self.requests,
4355 self.compilation_config.static_forward_context,
4356 self._get_mamba_state_copy_funcs(),
4357 mamba_bufs.preprocess,
4358 align_ctx=mamba_bufs.postprocess_align,
4359 )
4360 # preprocess_mamba resets num_accepted_tokens_cpu to 1
4361 # for requests whose state was copied to a new block.
4362 # Re-sync to GPU so the mamba kernel reads from the
4363 # correct initial state slot (init_token_idx = 0).
4364 self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = (
4365 self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs]
4366 )
4367 self.num_accepted_tokens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
4368
4369 # Stage per-request inputs for the fused postprocess kernel
4370 # only when that kernel will actually run. The kernel is
4371 # gated on spec-decode + hybrid (see MambaBuffers.create);
4372 # without it, ``mamba_bufs.postprocess_align`` is None and
4373 # the staging buffers don't exist.
4374 if mamba_bufs.postprocess_align is not None:
4375 mamba_utils.stage_postprocess_inputs_to_gpu(
4376 mamba_bufs.postprocess_align,
4377 scheduler_output,
4378 self.input_batch.req_ids,
4379 num_reqs,
4380 self.requests,
4381 self.mamba_state_idx,
4382 )
4383
4384 use_spec_decode = len(scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens) > 0
4385 ubatch_slices_attn = ubatch_slices_padded if pad_attn else ubatch_slices
4386
4387 slot_mappings_by_group, slot_mappings = self._get_slot_mappings(
4388 num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded
4389 if pad_attn or has_separate_kv_update
4390 else num_tokens_unpadded,
4391 num_reqs_padded=(
4392 num_reqs_padded if pad_attn or has_separate_kv_update else num_reqs
4393 ),
4394 num_tokens_unpadded=num_tokens_unpadded,
4395 ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
4396 )
4397
4398 attn_metadata, spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = (
4399 self._build_attention_metadata(
4400 num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
4401 num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded if pad_attn else None,
4402 num_reqs=num_reqs,
4403 num_reqs_padded=num_reqs_padded if pad_attn else None,
4404 max_query_len=max_num_scheduled_tokens,
4405 ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_attn,
4406 logits_indices=logits_indices,
4407 max_num_sampled_tokens=max_num_sampled_tokens,
4408 use_spec_decode=use_spec_decode,
4409 num_scheduled_tokens=scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens,
4410 cascade_attn_prefix_lens=cascade_attn_prefix_lens,
4411 slot_mappings=slot_mappings_by_group,
4412 )
4413 )
4414
4415 (
4416 input_ids,
4417 inputs_embeds,
4418 positions,
4419 intermediate_tensors,
4420 model_kwargs,
4421 ec_connector_output,
4422 ) = self._preprocess(
4423 scheduler_output, num_tokens_padded, intermediate_tensors
4424 )
4425
4426 # Encoder-decoder models can only compile the pure decode steps where no
4427 # encoder inputs are present. Use eager for the first pass.
4428 num_encoder_reqs = len(scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs)
4429 has_encoder_input = (
4430 self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder and num_encoder_reqs > 0
4431 )
4432
4433 # Run the model.
4434 # Use persistent buffers for CUDA graphs.
4435 # When spec decode is enabled, defer connector finalization
4436 # (wait_for_save + clear metadata) until after draft model runs.
4437 defer_kv_connector_finalize = self.speculative_config is not None
4438 # Update the EPLB meta.
4439 if self.eplb_state is not None:
4440 self.eplb_state.prepare_forward(
4441 self.model_config,
4442 num_tokens_unpadded,
4443 ubatch_slices_padded,
4444 )
4445 with (
4446 set_forward_context(
4447 attn_metadata,
4448 self.vllm_config,
4449 num_tokens=num_tokens_padded,
4450 num_tokens_across_dp=num_tokens_across_dp,
4451 cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_mode,
4452 batch_descriptor=batch_desc,
4453 ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
4454 slot_mapping=slot_mappings,
4455 skip_compiled=has_encoder_input,
4456 ),
4457 record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: forward"),
4458 self.maybe_get_kv_connector_output(
4459 scheduler_output,
4460 defer_finalize=defer_kv_connector_finalize,
4461 ) as kv_connector_output,
4462 ):
4463 model_output = self._model_forward(
4464 input_ids=input_ids,
4465 positions=positions,
4466 intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,
4467 inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
4468 **model_kwargs,
4469 )
4470
4471 with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: postprocess"):
4472 if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
4473 # True when EAGLE 3 is used.
4474 hidden_states, aux_hidden_states = model_output
4475 else:
4476 # Common case.
4477 hidden_states = model_output
4478 aux_hidden_states = None
4479
4480 if not self.broadcast_pp_output:
4481 # Common case.
4482 if not get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
4483 # Return the intermediate tensors.
4484 assert isinstance(hidden_states, IntermediateTensors)
4485 self.kv_connector_output = kv_connector_output
4486 return hidden_states
4487
4488 if self.is_pooling_model:
4489 # Return the pooling output.
4490 return self._pool(
4491 hidden_states,
4492 num_scheduled_tokens,
4493 num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4494 kv_connector_output,
4495 )
4496
4497 sample_hidden_states = hidden_states[logits_indices]
4498 logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
4499 else:
4500 # Rare case.
4501 assert not self.is_pooling_model
4502
4503 sample_hidden_states = hidden_states[logits_indices]
4504 if not get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
4505 all_gather_tensors = {
4506 "residual": not is_residual_scattered_for_sp(
4507 self.vllm_config, num_tokens_padded
4508 )
4509 }
4510 get_pp_group().send_tensor_dict(
4511 hidden_states.tensors,
4512 all_gather_group=get_tp_group(),
4513 all_gather_tensors=all_gather_tensors,
4514 )
4515 logits = None
4516 else:
4517 logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
4518
4519 model_output_broadcast_data: dict[str, Any] = {}
4520 if logits is not None:
4521 model_output_broadcast_data["logits"] = logits.contiguous()
4522
4523 broadcasted = get_pp_group().broadcast_tensor_dict(
4524 model_output_broadcast_data, src=len(get_pp_group().ranks) - 1
4525 )
4526 assert broadcasted is not None
4527 logits = broadcasted["logits"]
4528
4529 self.execute_model_state = ExecuteModelState(
4530 scheduler_output,
4531 logits,
4532 spec_decode_metadata,
4533 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata,
4534 hidden_states,
4535 sample_hidden_states,
4536 aux_hidden_states,
4537 ec_connector_output,
4538 cudagraph_stats,
4539 slot_mappings,
4540 )
4541 self.kv_connector_output = kv_connector_output
4542
4543 # Now the batch has been launched we can wait for corrections from the
4544 # previous model forward without breaking async scheduling.
4545 if deferred_state_corrections_fn:
4546 deferred_state_corrections_fn()
4547
4548 return None
4549
4550 def _input_fits_in_drafter(
4551 self, common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata | None
4552 ) -> bool:
4553 if common_attn_metadata is None:
4554 return False
4555 assert self.speculative_config is not None
4556 # DFlash queries one extra token (the bonus token) beyond num_spec_tokens
4557 num_drafter_query_tokens = self.num_spec_tokens + (
4558 1 if self.speculative_config.use_dflash() else 0
4559 )
4560 return (
4561 common_attn_metadata.max_seq_len + num_drafter_query_tokens
4562 <= self.effective_drafter_max_model_len
4563 )
4564
4565 @torch.inference_mode
4566 def sample_tokens(
4567 self, grammar_output: "GrammarOutput | None"
4568 ) -> ModelRunnerOutput | AsyncModelRunnerOutput | IntermediateTensors:
4569 if self.execute_model_state is None:
4570 kv_connector_output = self.kv_connector_output
4571 self.kv_connector_output = None
4572 # receive sampled token ids from the last PP rank.
4573 if self.use_async_scheduling and not get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
4574 self._pp_receive_prev_sampled_token_ids_to_input_batch()
4575 # In case of PP with kv transfer, we need to pass through the
4576 # kv_connector_output
4577 return ModelRunnerOutput.with_kv_conn_output_only(kv_connector_output)
4578
4579 # Unpack ephemeral state.
4580 (
4581 scheduler_output,
4582 logits,
4583 spec_decode_metadata,
4584 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata,
4585 hidden_states,
4586 sample_hidden_states,
4587 aux_hidden_states,
4588 ec_connector_output,
4589 cudagraph_stats,
4590 slot_mappings,
4591 ) = self.execute_model_state
4592 # Clear ephemeral state.
4593 self.execute_model_state = None
4594
4595 # Apply structured output bitmasks if present.
4596 if grammar_output is not None:
4597 apply_grammar_bitmask(
4598 scheduler_output, grammar_output, self.input_batch, logits
4599 )
4600
4601 with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: sample"):
4602 sampler_output = self._sample(logits, spec_decode_metadata)
4603
4604 self._update_states_after_model_execute(
4605 sampler_output.sampled_token_ids, scheduler_output
4606 )
4607 if self.use_async_scheduling:
4608 pp = get_pp_group()
4609 # For torchrun external_launcher PP mode with broadcast_pp_output=True,
4610 # PP outputs have been broadcasted to all ranks at logits computation.
4611 # Therefore, here is no need to send sampled token ids again in this case.
4612 if not self.broadcast_pp_output and pp.world_size > 1 and pp.is_last_rank:
4613 self._pp_broadcast_prev_sampled_token_ids(
4614 sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
4615 )
4616
4617 self._draft_token_ids = None
4618 self._draft_probs = None
4619 self._draft_prob_req_ids = None
4620 self._draft_token_req_ids = None
4621 self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu = None
4622 self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = None
4623
4624 def propose_draft_token_ids(sampled_token_ids):
4625 assert spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None
4626 with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: draft"):
4627 self._draft_token_ids = self.propose_draft_token_ids(
4628 scheduler_output,
4629 sampled_token_ids,
4630 self.input_batch.sampling_metadata,
4631 hidden_states,
4632 sample_hidden_states,
4633 aux_hidden_states,
4634 spec_decode_metadata,
4635 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata,
4636 slot_mappings,
4637 )
4638 self._copy_draft_token_ids_to_cpu(scheduler_output)
4639
4640 spec_config = self.speculative_config
4641 draft_after_bookkeeping = False
4642 if spec_config is not None:
4643 # Decide whether to run the drafter or zero out draft tokens.
4644 input_fits_in_drafter = self._input_fits_in_drafter(
4645 spec_decode_common_attn_metadata
4646 )
4647 # Whether the drafter runs a GPU model forward (and thus carries
4648 # TP/EP/DP collectives), independent of padded-batch timing.
4649 drafter_runs_model_forward = (
4650 spec_config.use_eagle()
4651 or spec_config.uses_draft_model()
4652 or spec_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
4653 )
4654 use_gpu_toks = (
4655 drafter_runs_model_forward
4656 and not spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch
4657 )
4658 if use_gpu_toks:
4659 # EAGLE/DraftModel speculative decoding can use the GPU sampled tokens
4660 # as inputs, and does not need to wait for bookkeeping to finish.
4661 assert isinstance(
4662 self.drafter,
4663 EagleProposer
4664 | DFlashProposer
4665 | DraftModelProposer
4666 | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
4667 | Gemma4Proposer,
4668 )
4669 sampled_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
4670 if input_fits_in_drafter:
4671 propose_draft_token_ids(sampled_token_ids)
4672 else:
4673 if self.valid_sampled_token_count_event is not None:
4674 assert spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None
4675 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count = (
4676 self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_padded(
4677 sampled_token_ids,
4678 self.requests,
4679 self.input_batch,
4680 self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
4681 )
4682 )
4683 self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4684 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
4685 )
4686 if self.parallel_config.data_parallel_size > 1:
4687 # Prevent hang when DP ranks disagree on input_fits_in_drafter
4688 self.drafter.dummy_run(num_tokens=1)
4689 elif (
4690 spec_config.use_ngram_gpu()
4691 and not spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch
4692 ):
4693 assert isinstance(self.drafter, NgramProposerGPU)
4694 sampled_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
4695 if input_fits_in_drafter:
4696 propose_draft_token_ids(sampled_token_ids)
4697 elif self.valid_sampled_token_count_event is not None:
4698 assert spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None
4699 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count, _ = (
4700 self.drafter.update_token_ids_ngram(
4701 sampled_token_ids,
4702 self.input_batch,
4703 self.token_ids_gpu_tensor,
4704 self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu,
4705 self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
4706 )
4707 )
4708 self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4709 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
4710 )
4711 else:
4712 # These drafters consume CPU sampled tokens, so they run
4713 # after bookkeeping.
4714 draft_after_bookkeeping = True
4715
4716 if not input_fits_in_drafter:
4717 # Zero out draft tokens so the scheduler doesn't schedule
4718 # stale drafts from the previous step.
4719 # For Nemotron-H: it is necessary to zero out the draft tokens,
4720 # otherwise the stale tokens will corrupt Mamba recurrent
4721 # state and logprobs for sequences near max_model_len.
4722 self._draft_token_ids = torch.zeros(
4723 1, device=self.device, dtype=torch.int32
4724 ).expand(len(self.input_batch.req_ids), self.num_spec_tokens)
4725 self._draft_probs = None
4726 self._draft_prob_req_ids = None
4727 self._copy_draft_token_ids_to_cpu(scheduler_output, zeros_only=True)
4728
4729 with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: bookkeep"):
4730 (
4731 num_nans_in_logits,
4732 num_nans_device,
4733 logprobs_lists,
4734 valid_sampled_token_ids,
4735 prompt_logprobs_dict,
4736 req_ids_output_copy,
4737 req_id_to_index_output_copy,
4738 invalid_req_indices,
4739 ) = self._bookkeeping_sync(
4740 scheduler_output,
4741 sampler_output,
4742 logits,
4743 hidden_states,
4744 scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens,
4745 )
4746
4747 if draft_after_bookkeeping:
4748 # ngram and other speculative decoding methods use the sampled
4749 # tokens on the CPU, so they are run after bookkeeping.
4750 if input_fits_in_drafter:
4751 propose_draft_token_ids(valid_sampled_token_ids)
4752 elif (
4753 drafter_runs_model_forward
4754 and self.parallel_config.data_parallel_size > 1
4755 ):
4756 # Prevent hang when DP ranks disagree on input_fits_in_drafter
4757 assert isinstance(
4758 self.drafter,
4759 EagleProposer
4760 | DFlashProposer
4761 | DraftModelProposer
4762 | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
4763 | Gemma4Proposer,
4764 )
4765 self.drafter.dummy_run(num_tokens=1)
4766
4767 # Finalize KV connector (wait_for_save + clear metadata) after
4768 # draft model runs. Deferred from target model forward to allow
4769 # draft model to also save its KV cache.
4770 if spec_config is not None:
4771 self.finalize_kv_connector()
4772
4773 with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: eplb"):
4774 self.eplb_step()
4775
