4776 # self.kv_connector_output may be modified during drafting
4777 kv_connector_output = self.kv_connector_output
4778 self.kv_connector_output = None
4779
4780 with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: ModelRunnerOutput"):
4781 output = ModelRunnerOutput(
4782 req_ids=req_ids_output_copy,
4783 req_id_to_index=req_id_to_index_output_copy,
4784 sampled_token_ids=valid_sampled_token_ids,
4785 logprobs=logprobs_lists,
4786 prompt_logprobs_dict=prompt_logprobs_dict,
4787 kv_connector_output=kv_connector_output,
4788 ec_connector_output=ec_connector_output
4789 if self.supports_mm_inputs
4790 else None,
4791 num_nans_in_logits=num_nans_in_logits,
4792 cudagraph_stats=cudagraph_stats,
4793 routed_experts=None,
4794 )
4795
4796 if not self.use_async_scheduling:
4797 if self.routed_experts_initialized:
4798 # Sync path: D2H was issued in ``_bookkeeping_sync`` and
4799 # synchronized by ``_to_list``'s event.synchronize(), so
4800 # the pinned buffers are ready to be wrapped as numpy.
4801 total = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4802 output.routed_experts = RoutedExpertsLists(
4803 routing_data=self.routed_experts_cpu[:total].numpy(),
4804 slot_mapping=self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_cpu[:total].numpy(),
4805 )
4806 return output
4807
4808 with record_function_or_nullcontext(
4809 "gpu_model_runner: AsyncGPUModelRunnerOutput"
4810 ):
4811 # Async path: produce a device-side snapshot that the async
4812 # copy stream can D2H later. Both tensors must be private
4813 # clones because:
4814 # - ``routing_data`` source is the shared capturer buffer,
4815 # which the next forward overwrites on the default stream.
4816 # - ``slot_mapping`` source is our own
4817 # ``routed_experts_slot_mapping_device``, which the
4818 # next ``_prepare_inputs`` overwrites on the default
4819 # stream while the D2H is still pending on the copy
4820 # stream.
4821 # Without clones, the copy stream would read torn data.
4822 routed_experts_snapshot = self.get_routed_experts(
4823 scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4824 )
4825
4826 async_output = AsyncGPUModelRunnerOutput(
4827 model_runner_output=output,
4828 sampled_token_ids=sampler_output.sampled_token_ids,
4829 logprobs_tensors=sampler_output.logprobs_tensors,
4830 invalid_req_indices=invalid_req_indices,
4831 async_output_copy_stream=self._get_or_create_async_output_copy_stream(),
4832 vocab_size=self.input_batch.vocab_size,
4833 routed_experts=routed_experts_snapshot,
4834 check_ep_fault=self.check_ep_fault,
4835 num_nans=num_nans_device,
4836 )
4837 with record_function_or_nullcontext(
4838 "gpu_model_runner: set_async_sampled_token_ids"
4839 ):
4840 # Save ref of sampled_token_ids CPU tensor if the batch contains
4841 # any requests with sampling params that require output ids.
4842 self.input_batch.set_async_sampled_token_ids(
4843 async_output.sampled_token_ids_cpu,
4844 async_output.async_copy_ready_event,
4845 )
4846
4847 return async_output
4848
4849 def _pp_broadcast_prev_sampled_token_ids(
4850 self, sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor
4851 ) -> None:
4852 """Broadcast sampled token ids (GPU) from last PP stage"""
4853 pp = get_pp_group()
4854 assert pp.is_last_rank
4855 # `prev_sampled_token_ids` is expected to have shape [num_reqs, 1].
4856 assert sampled_token_ids.dim() == 2 and sampled_token_ids.shape[-1] == 1, (
4857 "PP+async expects sampled_token_ids to have shape [num_reqs, 1]"
4858 )
4859 # Skip for chunked prefill: sampled tokens are dummy
4860 # and will be discarded, no need to broadcast.
4861 if not self._is_all_reqs_chunked_prefill():
4862 torch.distributed.broadcast(
4863 sampled_token_ids, src=pp.rank, group=pp.device_group
4864 )
4865
4866 def _pp_receive_prev_sampled_token_ids_to_input_batch(self) -> None:
4867 """Receive sampled token ids broadcast from last PP stage"""
4868 pp = get_pp_group()
4869 assert not pp.is_last_rank
4870 num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
4871 # `prev_sampled_token_ids` is expected to have shape [num_reqs, 1].
4872 recv = torch.empty((num_reqs, 1), dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device)
4873 # skip for chunked prefill.
4874 if not self._is_all_reqs_chunked_prefill():
4875 self._pp_recv_work = torch.distributed.broadcast(
4876 recv, src=pp.last_rank, group=pp.device_group, async_op=True
4877 )
4878 self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = recv
4879
4880 # construct `prev_req_id_to_index` here so `_prepare_input_ids`
4881 # can map req_id -> previous batch row
4882 discard_req_indices = np.nonzero(self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs])[0]
4883 discard_req_indices_set = set(discard_req_indices)
4884 prev_req_id_to_index: dict[str, int] = {}
4885 for i, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids):
4886 if i in discard_req_indices_set:
4887 continue
4888 prev_req_id_to_index[req_id] = i
4889 # PP+async scheduling: advance per-request local cached output length by
4890 # appending a placeholder (-1) token id.
4891 if (req_state := self.requests.get(req_id)) is not None:
4892 req_state.output_token_ids.append(-1)
4893 pos = self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[i]
4894 self.input_batch.is_token_ids[i, pos] = True
4895 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[i] = pos + 1
4896 self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index = prev_req_id_to_index
4897
4898 def take_draft_token_ids(self) -> DraftTokenIds | None:
4899 if not self.num_spec_tokens or not self._draft_token_req_ids:
4900 return None
4901 draft_token_ids, req_ids = self._get_draft_token_ids_cpu()
4902 return DraftTokenIds(req_ids, draft_token_ids)
4903
4904 def _copy_draft_token_ids_to_cpu(
4905 self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput", zeros_only: bool = False
4906 ) -> None:
4907 if torch.is_tensor(self._draft_token_ids):
4908 assert isinstance(self._draft_token_ids, torch.Tensor)
4909 self.prev_num_spec_tokens = self._draft_token_ids.shape[1]
4910 # Check if we need to copy draft tokens to CPU. In async scheduling,
4911 # we only copy when needed for structured output, penalties or bad_words.
4912 if self.use_async_scheduling and not (
4913 scheduler_output.has_structured_output_requests
4914 or self.input_batch.sampling_metadata.output_token_ids
4915 ):
4916 return
4917 # We must also set the corresponding request ids.
4918 self._draft_token_req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids.copy()
4919
4920 draft_token_ids: torch.Tensor = self._draft_token_ids
4921 if not torch.is_tensor(draft_token_ids):
4922 return
4923 assert self.draft_token_ids_event is not None
4924 assert self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream is not None
4925 assert self.draft_token_ids_cpu is not None
4926 default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
4927 num_reqs = draft_token_ids.shape[0]
4928 num_spec_tokens = draft_token_ids.shape[1]
4929 with torch.cuda.stream(self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream):
4930 if not zeros_only:
4931 # Trigger async copy of draft token ids to cpu.
4932 self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream)
4933 self.draft_token_ids_cpu[:num_reqs, :num_spec_tokens].copy_(
4934 draft_token_ids, non_blocking=True
4935 )
4936 else:
4937 # No copy needed, just zero-out cpu tensor.
4938 self.draft_token_ids_cpu[:num_reqs, :num_spec_tokens] = 0
4939 self.draft_token_ids_event.record()
4940
4941 def _get_draft_token_ids_cpu(self) -> tuple[list[list[int]], list[str]]:
4942 if isinstance(self._draft_token_ids, list):
4943 return self._draft_token_ids, self.input_batch.req_ids
4944 req_ids = self._draft_token_req_ids
4945 if req_ids is None:
4946 return [], []
4947 assert self.draft_token_ids_event is not None
4948 assert self.draft_token_ids_cpu is not None
4949 self.draft_token_ids_event.synchronize()
4950 assert isinstance(self._draft_token_ids, torch.Tensor)
4951 num_spec_tokens = self._draft_token_ids.shape[1]
4952 return self.draft_token_ids_cpu[
4953 : len(req_ids), :num_spec_tokens
4954 ].tolist(), req_ids
4955
4956 def _copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4957 self, next_token_ids: torch.Tensor, valid_sampled_tokens_count: torch.Tensor
4958 ) -> None:
4959 if self.valid_sampled_token_count_event is None:
4960 return
4961
4962 default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
4963 # Initialize a new stream to overlap the copy operation with
4964 # prepare_input of draft model.
4965 with torch.cuda.stream(self.valid_sampled_token_count_copy_stream):
4966 self.valid_sampled_token_count_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream) # type: ignore
4967 counts = valid_sampled_tokens_count
4968 counts_cpu = self.valid_sampled_token_count_cpu
4969 assert counts_cpu is not None
4970 counts_cpu[: counts.shape[0]].copy_(counts, non_blocking=True)
4971 self.valid_sampled_token_count_event.record()
4972
4973 if self.use_async_spec_decode:
4974 # Stash for GPU-side correction in _prepare_inputs.
4975 self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu = valid_sampled_tokens_count
4976 self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = next_token_ids.unsqueeze(1)
4977
4978 def _get_valid_sampled_token_count(self) -> list[int]:
4979 # Wait until valid_sampled_tokens_count is copied to cpu,
4980 prev_sampled_token_ids = self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids
4981 sampled_count_event = self.valid_sampled_token_count_event
4982 if sampled_count_event is None or prev_sampled_token_ids is None:
4983 return []
4984
4985 counts_cpu = self.valid_sampled_token_count_cpu
4986 assert counts_cpu is not None
4987 sampled_count_event.synchronize()
4988 return counts_cpu[: prev_sampled_token_ids.shape[0]].tolist()
4989
4990 def _get_spec_decode_draft_probs(
4991 self, spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata
4992 ) -> torch.Tensor | None:
4993 if self._draft_probs is None or self._draft_prob_req_ids is None:
4994 return None
4995
4996 row_by_req_id = {
4997 req_id: idx for idx, req_id in enumerate(self._draft_prob_req_ids)
4998 }
4999 draft_probs_rows: list[torch.Tensor] = []
5000 for req_id, num_draft in zip(
5001 self.input_batch.req_ids, spec_decode_metadata.num_draft_tokens
5002 ):
5003 if num_draft == 0:
5004 continue
5005 row_idx = row_by_req_id.get(req_id)
5006 if row_idx is None:
5007 logger.warning(
5008 "Missing cached draft probabilities for request %s; "
5009 "falling back to legacy speculative rejection behavior.",
5010 req_id,
5011 )
5012 return None
5013 draft_probs_rows.append(self._draft_probs[row_idx, :num_draft])
5014
5015 if not draft_probs_rows:
5016 return None
5017 return torch.cat(draft_probs_rows, dim=0).contiguous()
5018
5019 def propose_draft_token_ids(
5020 self,
5021 scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
5022 sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor | list[list[int]],
5023 sampling_metadata: SamplingMetadata,
5024 hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
5025 sample_hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
5026 aux_hidden_states: list[torch.Tensor] | None,
5027 spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata | None,
5028 common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata,
5029 slot_mappings: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None,
5030 ) -> list[list[int]] | torch.Tensor:
5031 num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
5032 spec_config = self.speculative_config
5033 assert spec_config is not None
5034 num_spec_tokens_to_schedule = scheduler_output.num_spec_tokens_to_schedule
5035 self._draft_probs = None
5036 self._draft_prob_req_ids = None
5037 if spec_config.method == "ngram":
5038 from vllm.v1.spec_decode.ngram_proposer import NgramProposer
5039
5040 assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
5041 assert isinstance(self.drafter, NgramProposer)
5042 draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
5043 num_spec_tokens_to_schedule,
5044 sampled_token_ids,
5045 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec,
5046 self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu,
5047 slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5048 )
5049 elif spec_config.method == "custom_class":
5050 assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
5051 draft_token_ids = cast(Any, self.drafter).propose(
5052 sampled_token_ids,
5053 self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec,
5054 self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu,
5055 slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5056 )
5057 elif spec_config.use_ngram_gpu():
5058 assert isinstance(self.drafter, NgramProposerGPU)
5059 (
5060 next_token_ids,
5061 valid_sampled_tokens_count,
5062 valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu,
5063 ) = self.drafter.update_token_ids_ngram(
5064 sampled_token_ids,
5065 self.input_batch,
5066 self.token_ids_gpu_tensor,
5067 self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu,
5068 self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
5069 )
5070 self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
5071 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
5072 )
5073
5074 batch_size = next_token_ids.shape[0]
5075
5076 draft_token_ids, num_valid_draft_tokens = self.drafter.propose(
5077 num_spec_tokens_to_schedule,
5078 self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu[:batch_size],
5079 self.token_ids_gpu_tensor[:batch_size],
5080 valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu,
5081 valid_sampled_tokens_count,
5082 )
5083
5084 # Cache valid draft counts for scheduler-side trimming.
5085 self._num_valid_draft_tokens = num_valid_draft_tokens
5086
5087 # Async D2H copy on a dedicated stream.
5088 copy_num_valid_draft_tokens(
5089 self._num_valid_draft_tokens_cpu,
5090 self._num_valid_draft_tokens_copy_stream,
5091 self._num_valid_draft_tokens_event,
5092 self._num_valid_draft_tokens,
5093 self.input_batch.num_reqs,
5094 )
5095 elif spec_config.method == "suffix":
5096 assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
5097 assert isinstance(self.drafter, SuffixDecodingProposer)
5098 draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
5099 num_spec_tokens_to_schedule,
5100 self.input_batch,
5101 sampled_token_ids,
5102 slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5103 )
5104 elif spec_config.method == "medusa":
5105 assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
5106 assert isinstance(self.drafter, MedusaProposer)
5107
5108 if sample_hidden_states.shape[0] == len(sampled_token_ids):
5109 # The input to the target model does not include draft tokens.
5110 hidden_states = sample_hidden_states
5111 else:
5112 indices = []
5113 offset = 0
5114 assert spec_decode_metadata is not None, (
5115 "No spec decode metadata for medusa"
5116 )
5117 for num_draft, tokens in zip(
5118 spec_decode_metadata.num_draft_tokens, sampled_token_ids
5119 ):
5120 indices.append(offset + len(tokens) - 1)
5121 offset += num_draft + 1
5122 indices = async_tensor_h2d(indices, device=self.device)
5123 hidden_states = sample_hidden_states[indices]
5124
5125 draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
5126 num_speculative_tokens=num_spec_tokens_to_schedule,
5127 target_hidden_states=hidden_states,
5128 sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,
5129 slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5130 )
5131 elif spec_config.uses_extract_hidden_states():
5132 assert isinstance(self.drafter, ExtractHiddenStatesProposer)
5133 assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, torch.Tensor), (
5134 "sampled_token_ids should be a torch.Tensor for "
5135 "extract_hidden_states method."
5136 )
5137 if not self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs or aux_hidden_states is None:
5138 raise ValueError(
5139 "aux_hidden_states are required when using `extract_hidden_states`"
5140 )
5141 target_hidden_states = [h[:num_scheduled_tokens] for h in aux_hidden_states]
5142
5143 draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
5144 num_speculative_tokens=num_spec_tokens_to_schedule,
5145 sampled_token_ids=sampled_token_ids,
5146 target_hidden_states=target_hidden_states,
5147 common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,
5148 slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5149 )
5150 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count = (
5151 self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_padded(
5152 sampled_token_ids,
5153 self.requests,
5154 self.input_batch,
5155 self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
5156 )
5157 )
5158 self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
5159 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
5160 )
5161
5162 elif (
5163 spec_config.use_eagle()
5164 or spec_config.use_dflash()
5165 or spec_config.uses_draft_model()
5166 ):
5167 assert isinstance(
5168 self.drafter,
5169 EagleProposer | DFlashProposer | DraftModelProposer | Gemma4Proposer,
5170 )
5171
5172 if spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch:
5173 # When padded-batch is disabled, the sampled_token_ids should be
5174 # the cpu-side list[list[int]] of valid sampled tokens for each
5175 # request, with invalid requests having empty lists.
5176 assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list), (
5177 "sampled_token_ids should be a python list when"
5178 "padded-batch is disabled."
5179 )
5180 next_token_ids = self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_cpu(
5181 sampled_token_ids,
5182 self.requests,
5183 self.input_batch,
5184 scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens,
5185 )
5186 else:
5187 # When using padded-batch, the sampled_token_ids should be
5188 # the gpu tensor of sampled tokens for each request, of shape
5189 # (num_reqs, num_spec_tokens + 1) with rejected tokens having
5190 # value -1.
5191 assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, torch.Tensor), (
5192 "sampled_token_ids should be a torch.Tensor when"
5193 "padded-batch is enabled."
5194 )
5195 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count = (
5196 self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_padded(
5197 sampled_token_ids,
5198 self.requests,
5199 self.input_batch,
5200 self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
5201 )
5202 )
5203 self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
5204 next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
5205 )
5206
5207 # Let the target override the hidden state fed to the drafter
5208 # (e.g. DeepSeek V4 MTP needs the pre-hc_head residual). Safe to
5209 # rebind here: hidden_states was already consumed for sampling
5210 # above and is not used again in this branch.
5211 alt = getattr(
5212 self.get_model(), "get_mtp_target_hidden_states", lambda: None
5213 )()
5214 if alt is not None:
5215 hidden_states = alt
5216
5217 num_rejected_tokens_gpu = None
5218 if spec_decode_metadata is None:
5219 token_indices_to_sample = None
5220 # input_ids can be None for multimodal models.
5221 target_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens]
5222 target_positions = self._get_positions(num_scheduled_tokens)
5223 if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5224 assert aux_hidden_states is not None
5225 target_hidden_states = torch.cat(
5226 [h[:num_scheduled_tokens] for h in aux_hidden_states], dim=-1
5227 )
5228 else:
5229 target_hidden_states = hidden_states[:num_scheduled_tokens]
5230 else:
5231 if spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch:
5232 token_indices_to_sample = None
5233 common_attn_metadata, token_indices = self.drafter.prepare_inputs(
5234 common_attn_metadata,
5235 sampled_token_ids,
5236 spec_decode_metadata.num_draft_tokens,
5237 )
5238 target_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[token_indices]
5239 target_positions = self._get_positions(token_indices)
5240 if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5241 assert aux_hidden_states is not None
5242 target_hidden_states = torch.cat(
5243 [h[token_indices] for h in aux_hidden_states], dim=-1
5244 )
5245 else:
5246 target_hidden_states = hidden_states[token_indices]
5247 else:
5248 (
5249 common_attn_metadata,
5250 token_indices_to_sample,
5251 num_rejected_tokens_gpu,
5252 ) = self.drafter.prepare_inputs_padded(
5253 common_attn_metadata,
5254 spec_decode_metadata,
5255 valid_sampled_tokens_count,
5256 )
5257 total_num_tokens = common_attn_metadata.num_actual_tokens
5258 # When padding the batch, token_indices is just a range
5259 target_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:total_num_tokens]
5260 target_positions = self._get_positions(total_num_tokens)
5261 if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5262 assert aux_hidden_states is not None
5263 target_hidden_states = torch.cat(
5264 [h[:total_num_tokens] for h in aux_hidden_states], dim=-1
5265 )
5266 else:
5267 target_hidden_states = hidden_states[:total_num_tokens]
5268
5269 if self.supports_mm_inputs and self.drafter.supports_mm_inputs:
5270 mm_embed_inputs = self._gather_mm_embeddings(
5271 scheduler_output,
5272 shift_computed_tokens=1,
5273 )
5274 else:
5275 mm_embed_inputs = None
5276
5277 draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
5278 num_speculative_tokens=num_spec_tokens_to_schedule,
5279 target_token_ids=target_token_ids,
5280 target_positions=target_positions,
5281 target_hidden_states=target_hidden_states,
5282 next_token_ids=next_token_ids,
5283 token_indices_to_sample=token_indices_to_sample,
5284 sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,
5285 common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,
5286 mm_embed_inputs=mm_embed_inputs,
5287 num_rejected_tokens_gpu=num_rejected_tokens_gpu,
5288 slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5289 )
5290 if hasattr(self.drafter, "take_last_draft_probs"):
5291 draft_probs = self.drafter.take_last_draft_probs()
5292 if draft_probs is not None:
5293 self._draft_probs = draft_probs
5294 self._draft_prob_req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids.copy()
5295
5296 return draft_token_ids
5297
5298 def update_config(self, overrides: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
5299 allowed_config_names = {"load_config", "model_config"}
5300 for config_name, config_overrides in overrides.items():
5301 if config_name not in allowed_config_names:
5302 allowed = ", ".join(sorted(allowed_config_names))
5303 raise ValueError(
5304 f"Config override '{config_name}' is not supported. "
5305 f"Supported configs: {allowed}"
5306 )
5307 config = getattr(self, config_name)
5308 new_config = update_config(config, config_overrides)
5309 setattr(self, config_name, new_config)
5310
5311 @instrument(span_name="Loading (GPU)")
5312 def load_model(self, load_dummy_weights: bool = False) -> None:
5313 """
5314 Args:
5315 load_dummy_weights: load dummy weights instead of real weights.
5316 """
5317 logger.info_once(
5318 "Starting to load model %s...",
5319 self.model_config.model,
5320 scope="global",
5321 )
5322
5323 if self.parallel_config.enable_eplb:
5324 self.eplb_state = EplbState(self.parallel_config, self.device)
5325 eplb_models = 0
5326
5327 try:
5328 with DeviceMemoryProfiler() as m:
5329 time_before_load = time.perf_counter()
5330 if load_dummy_weights:
5331 self.load_config.load_format = "dummy"
5332 model_loader = get_model_loader(self.load_config)
5333 # Capture warmup providers selected while constructing the model.
5334 with self.jit_warmup_registry.activate(): # type: ignore[attr-defined]
5335 self.model = model_loader.load_model(
5336 vllm_config=self.vllm_config, model_config=self.model_config
5337 )
5338 lookback_depth = getattr(self.model, "token_lookback_depth", 0)
5339 if lookback_depth > 0:
5340 self.lookback_token_ids = self._make_buffer(
5341 self.max_num_reqs, lookback_depth, dtype=torch.int32
5342 )
5343 if self.lora_config:
5344 self.model = self.load_lora_model(
5345 self.model, self.vllm_config, self.device
5346 )
5347 if hasattr(self, "drafter"):
5348 logger.info_once("Loading drafter model...")
5349 if hasattr(self.drafter, "load_model"):
5350 self.drafter.load_model(self.model)
5351 if (
5352 self.parallel_config.enable_eplb
5353 and hasattr(self.drafter, "model")
5354 and (
5355 drafter_moe_model := get_mixture_of_experts_model(
5356 self.drafter.model
5357 )
5358 )
5359 is not None
5360 ):
5361 assert not self.parallel_config.enable_elastic_ep, (
5362 "Elastic EP is not supported with drafter model."
5363 )
5364 spec_config = self.vllm_config.speculative_config
5365 assert spec_config is not None
5366 assert spec_config.draft_model_config is not None
5367 logger.info_once(
5368 "EPLB is enabled for MoE part of drafter model %s.",
5369 spec_config.draft_model_config.model,
5370 )
5371 if self.eplb_state is None:
5372 self.eplb_state = EplbState(
5373 self.parallel_config, self.device
5374 )
5375 self.eplb_state.add_model(
5376 drafter_moe_model,
5377 spec_config.draft_model_config,
5378 )
5379 assert hasattr(self.drafter, "set_eplb_state")
5380 self.drafter.set_eplb_state(self.eplb_state)
5381 eplb_models += 1
5382
5383 self._setup_eagle3_aux_hidden_state_outputs()
5384
5385 # Resolve the MoE model, unwrapping VLM wrappers if needed.
5386 # VLM models (e.g. KimiK25ForConditionalGeneration) wrap the
5387 # actual MoE language model but don't implement
5388 # MixtureOfExperts themselves.
5389 self._moe_model = get_mixture_of_experts_model(self.model)
5390
5391 if self._moe_model is not None and self.parallel_config.enable_eplb:
5392 logger.info_once(
5393 "EPLB is enabled for MoE part of model %s.",
5394 self.model_config.model,
5395 )
5396 assert self.eplb_state is not None
5397 self.eplb_state.add_model(
5398 self._moe_model,
5399 self.model_config,
5400 )
5401 eplb_models += 1
5402
5403 time_after_load = time.perf_counter()
5404 self.model_memory_usage = m.consumed_memory
5405 except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError as e:
5406 msg = (
5407 "Failed to load model - not enough GPU memory. "
5408 "Try lowering --gpu-memory-utilization to free memory for weights, "
5409 "increasing --tensor-parallel-size, or using --quantization. "
5410 "See https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/configuration/conserving_memory/ "
5411 "for more tips."
5412 )
5413 combined_msg = f"{msg} (original error: {e})"
5414 logger.error(combined_msg)
5415 raise e
5416 logger.info_once(
5417 "Model loading took %s GiB memory and %.6f seconds",
5418 format_gib(self.model_memory_usage),
5419 time_after_load - time_before_load,
5420 )
5421
5422 mm_config = self.model_config.multimodal_config
5423 self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled = (
5424 supports_multimodal_pruning(self.get_model())
5425 and mm_config is not None
5426 and mm_config.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled()
5427 )
5428 self.requires_sequential_video_encoding = hasattr(
5429 self.get_model(), "requires_sequential_video_encoding"
5430 ) # Temporary hack for dynamic res video w/o support for bs>1 yet
5431
5432 if (
5433 self.parallel_config.enable_eplb
5434 and self._moe_model is not None
5435 and not load_dummy_weights
5436 and self.eplb_state is not None
5437 and self.eplb_state.is_async
5438 ):
5439 self.eplb_state.start_async_loop()
5440
5441 if (
5442 self.vllm_config.compilation_config.mode
5443 == CompilationMode.STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE
5444 ):
5445 from vllm.env_override import _apply_constrain_to_fx_strides_patch
5446
5447 _apply_constrain_to_fx_strides_patch()
5448 backend = self.vllm_config.compilation_config.init_backend(self.vllm_config)
5449 compilation_counter.stock_torch_compile_count += 1
5450 self.model.compile(fullgraph=True, backend=backend)
5451 return
5452 # for other compilation modes, cudagraph behavior is controlled by
5453 # CudagraphWrapper and CudagraphDispatcher of vllm.
5454
5455 # wrap the model with full cudagraph wrapper if needed.
5456 cudagraph_mode = self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode
5457 assert cudagraph_mode is not None
5458 if (
5459 is_breakable_cudagraph_enabled()
5460 and cudagraph_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE
5461 and not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
5462 ):
5463 # Scoped to PIECEWISE dispatch; FULL cudagraphs (below) are
5464 # unaffected. PIECEWISE dispatch can also arise after wrapping
5465 # (drafters under a FULL target mode, or a later FULL ->
5466 # FULL_AND_PIECEWISE upgrade in _check_and_update_cudagraph_mode).
5467 self.model = BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper(
5468 self.model, self.vllm_config, runtime_mode=CUDAGraphMode.PIECEWISE
5469 )
5470 drafter = getattr(self, "drafter", None)
5471 if drafter is not None and hasattr(drafter, "model"):
5472 drafter.model = BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper(
5473 drafter.model,
5474 self.vllm_config,
5475 runtime_mode=CUDAGraphMode.PIECEWISE,
5476 )
5477
5478 if (
5479 cudagraph_mode.has_full_cudagraphs()
5480 and not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
5481 ):
5482 self.model = CUDAGraphWrapper(
5483 self.model, self.vllm_config, runtime_mode=CUDAGraphMode.FULL
5484 )
5485 elif self.parallel_config.use_ubatching:
5486 if cudagraph_mode.has_full_cudagraphs():
5487 self.model = UBatchWrapper(
5488 self.model, self.vllm_config, CUDAGraphMode.FULL, self.device
5489 )
5490 else:
5491 self.model = UBatchWrapper(
5492 self.model, self.vllm_config, CUDAGraphMode.NONE, self.device
5493 )
5494
5495 get_offloader().post_init()
5496
5497 def _setup_eagle3_aux_hidden_state_outputs(self) -> None:
5498 if not self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5499 return
5500
5501 if not supports_eagle3(self.get_model()):
5502 raise RuntimeError(
5503 "Model does not support EAGLE3 interface but "
5504 "aux_hidden_state_outputs was requested"
5505 )
5506 # Try to get auxiliary layers from speculative config,
5507 # otherwise use model's default layers
5508 aux_layers = self._get_eagle3_aux_layers_from_config()
5509 if aux_layers:
5510 logger.info(
5511 "Using auxiliary layers from speculative config: %s", aux_layers
5512 )
5513 else:
5514 aux_layers = self.model.get_eagle3_default_aux_hidden_state_layers()
5515
5516 self.model.set_aux_hidden_state_layers(aux_layers)
5517
5518 def _get_eagle3_aux_layers_from_config(self) -> tuple[int, ...] | None:
5519 """Extract Eagle3 auxiliary layer indices from speculative config.
5520
5521 These indices specify which hidden states from the base model should
5522 be used as auxiliary inputs for the Eagle3 drafter model during
5523 speculative decoding.
5524
5525 Returns:
5526 Tuple of layer indices if found in draft model config,
5527 None otherwise.
5528 """
5529 if not (self.speculative_config and self.speculative_config.draft_model_config):
5530 return None
5531
5532 hf_config = self.speculative_config.draft_model_config.hf_config
5533
5534 layer_ids = getattr(hf_config, "eagle_aux_hidden_state_layer_ids", None)
5535 if not layer_ids:
5536 dflash_config = getattr(hf_config, "dflash_config", None)
5537 eagle_config = getattr(hf_config, "eagle_config", None)
5538
5539 if dflash_config and isinstance(dflash_config, dict):
5540 # Add 1 to convert DFlash's aux layer id semantics
5541 layer_ids = [
5542 i + 1 for i in (dflash_config.get("target_layer_ids") or [])
5543 ]
5544
5545 if eagle_config and isinstance(eagle_config, dict):
5546 layer_ids = eagle_config.get("eagle_aux_hidden_state_layer_ids")
5547
5548 if layer_ids and isinstance(layer_ids, (list, tuple)):
5549 return tuple(layer_ids)
5550
5551 return None
5552
5553 def reload_weights(
5554 self,
5555 weights_iterator: Iterable[tuple[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,
5556 weights_path: str | None = None,
5557 is_checkpoint_format: bool = True,
5558 ) -> None:
5559 """
5560 Reload weights from a weights iterator or from disk
5561
5562 Args:
5563 weights_iterator: weights to load into model
5564 weights_path: path to load weights from if weights_iterator is not
5565 provided. Use path of original model if neither is provided.
5566 is_checkpoint_format: set to False if weights have already been
5567 processed into kernel format (repacking, renaming, etc.)
5568 """
5569 # TODO(@kylesayrs): generalize to all runners and loaders
5570 # argument validation
5571 if weights_iterator is None and not is_checkpoint_format:
5572 logger.warning(
5573 "Reloading from disk means that weights will be in checkpoint format. "
5574 "Please use `is_checkpoint_format=True` "
5575 "to avoid weight reloading errors"
5576 )
5577
5578 model = self.get_model()
5579 weights_to_load = {
5580 name.replace(".base_layer.", ".") if self.lora_config else name
5581 for name, _ in model.named_parameters()
5582 }
5583 counter_before_reloading = time.perf_counter()
5584
5585 # load weights from disk if none are provided
5586 if weights_iterator is None:
5587 model_loader = get_model_loader(self.load_config)
5588 if not hasattr(model_loader, "get_all_weights"):
5589 raise NotImplementedError(
5590 f"Model reloading with `{self.load_config.load_format}` format"
5591 )
5592
5593 if weights_path is not None:
5594 # The revision belongs to the model we are reloading away from,
5595 # so it must not be carried over to the new path.
5596 self.model_config.model = weights_path
5597 self.model_config.revision = None
5598 weights_iterator = model_loader.get_all_weights(self.model_config, model)
5599 weights_iterator = cast(
5600 Iterable[tuple[str, torch.Tensor]], weights_iterator
5601 )
5602
5603 # begin loading weights
5604 logger.info_once("Reloading weights inplace...")
5605 if is_checkpoint_format:
5606 # load weights from checkpoint/ original model format
5607 initialize_layerwise_reload(model)
5608 loaded_weights = model.load_weights(weights_iterator)
5609 finalize_layerwise_reload(model, self.model_config)
5610
5611 else:
5612 # load weights from kernel format
5613 logger.warning_once(
5614 "Reloading with `is_checkpoint_format=True` requires that "
5615 "weights be in kernel format and already sharded",
5616 )
5617 loaded_weights = set()
5618 for name, loaded_weight in weights_iterator:
5619 param = _get_parameter_for_reload(model, name) # TODO: buffers?
5620 param.copy_(loaded_weight)
5621 loaded_weights.add(name)
5622
5623 self.reset_lora_state()
5624
5625 # logging and validation
5626 counter_after_reloading = time.perf_counter()
5627 diff_seconds = counter_after_reloading - counter_before_reloading
5628 logger.info_once(
5629 "Reloading and processing weights took %.2f seconds",
5630 diff_seconds,
5631 )
5632 if self.model_config.quantization is None and loaded_weights is not None:
5633 weights_not_loaded = weights_to_load - loaded_weights
5634 if weights_not_loaded:
5635 logger.warning(
5636 "Following weights were not loaded from checkpoint: %s",
5637 weights_not_loaded,
5638 )
5639
5640 self.reset_encoder_cache()
5641 self.reset_mm_cache()
5642
5643 def _get_prompt_logprobs_dict(
5644 self,
5645 hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
5646 num_scheduled_tokens: dict[str, int],
5647 ) -> dict[str, LogprobsTensors | None]:
5648 num_prompt_logprobs_dict = self.num_prompt_logprobs
5649 if not num_prompt_logprobs_dict:
5650 return {}
5651
5652 prompt_logprobs_dict: dict[str, LogprobsTensors | None] = {}
5653
5654 # Since prompt logprobs are a rare feature, prioritize simple,
5655 # maintainable loop over optimal performance.
5656 completed_prefill_reqs = []
5657 for req_id, num_prompt_logprobs in num_prompt_logprobs_dict.items():
5658 num_tokens = num_scheduled_tokens.get(req_id)
5659 if num_tokens is None:
5660 # This can happen if the request was preempted in prefill stage.
5661 continue
5662
5663 # Get metadata for this request.
5664 request = self.requests[req_id]
5665 if request.prompt_token_ids is None:
5666 # Prompt logprobs is incompatible with prompt embeddings
5667 continue
5668
5669 num_prompt_tokens = len(request.prompt_token_ids)
5670 prompt_token_ids = async_tensor_h2d(
5671 request.prompt_token_ids, device=self.device
5672 )
5673
5674 # Set up target LogprobsTensors object.
5675 logprobs_tensors = request.in_progress_prompt_logprobs_cpu
5676 if logprobs_tensors is None:
5677 # Create empty logprobs CPU tensors for the entire prompt.
5678 # If chunked, we'll copy in slice by slice.
5679 logprobs_tensors = LogprobsTensors.empty_cpu(
5680 num_prompt_tokens - 1, num_prompt_logprobs + 1
5681 )
5682 request.in_progress_prompt_logprobs_cpu = logprobs_tensors
5683
5684 # Determine number of logits to retrieve.
5685 start_idx = request.num_computed_tokens
5686 start_tok = start_idx + 1
5687 num_remaining_tokens = num_prompt_tokens - start_tok
5688 if num_tokens <= num_remaining_tokens:
5689 # This is a chunk, more tokens remain.
5690 # In the == case, there are no more prompt logprobs to produce
5691 # but we want to defer returning them to the next step where we
5692 # have new generated tokens to return.
5693 num_logits = num_tokens
5694 else:
5695 # This is the last chunk of prompt tokens to return.
5696 num_logits = num_remaining_tokens
5697 completed_prefill_reqs.append(req_id)
5698 prompt_logprobs_dict[req_id] = logprobs_tensors
5699
5700 if num_logits <= 0:
5701 # This can happen for the final chunk if we prefilled exactly
5702 # (num_prompt_tokens - 1) tokens for this request in the prior
5703 # step. There are no more prompt logprobs to produce.
5704 continue
5705
5706 # Get the logits corresponding to this req's prompt tokens.
5707 # If this is a partial request (i.e. chunked prefill),
5708 # then there is prompt logprob generated for each index.
5709 req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
5710 offset = self.query_start_loc.np[req_idx].item()
5711 prompt_hidden_states = hidden_states[offset : offset + num_logits]
5712 logits = self.model.compute_logits(prompt_hidden_states)
5713
5714 # Get the "target" tokens for each index. For prompt at index i,
5715 # the token at prompt index i+1 is the "sampled" token we want
5716 # to gather the logprob for.
5717 tgt_token_ids = prompt_token_ids[start_tok : start_tok + num_logits]
5718
5719 # Compute prompt scores respecting logprobs_mode.
5720 # NOTE: prompt tokens skip sampling processors, so
5721 # processed_* and raw_* yield the same scores here.
5722 if self.model_config.logprobs_mode in ("raw_logits", "processed_logits"):
5723 scores = logits.to(torch.float32)
5724 else:
5725 scores = self.sampler.compute_logprobs(logits)
5726 token_ids, logprobs, ranks, *_ = self.sampler.gather_logprobs(
5727 scores, num_prompt_logprobs, tgt_token_ids
5728 )
5729
5730 # Transfer GPU->CPU async.
5731 chunk_slice = slice(start_idx, start_idx + num_logits)
5732 logprobs_tensors.logprob_token_ids[chunk_slice].copy_(
5733 token_ids, non_blocking=True
5734 )
5735 logprobs_tensors.logprobs[chunk_slice].copy_(logprobs, non_blocking=True)
5736 logprobs_tensors.selected_token_ranks[chunk_slice].copy_(
5737 ranks, non_blocking=True
5738 )
5739
5740 # Remove requests that have completed prefill from the batch
5741 # num_prompt_logprobs_dict.
5742 for req_id in completed_prefill_reqs:
5743 del num_prompt_logprobs_dict[req_id]
5744 self.requests[req_id].in_progress_prompt_logprobs_cpu = None
5745
5746 # Must synchronize the non-blocking GPU->CPU transfers.
5747 if prompt_logprobs_dict:
5748 self._sync_device()
5749
5750 return prompt_logprobs_dict
5751
5752 def _get_nans_in_logits(self, logits: torch.Tensor | None) -> dict[str, int]:
5753 """Count NaNs per request, reading the result back to the host.
5754
5755 Only used under sync scheduling, The async path keeps the counts
5756 on device instead; see`AsyncGPUModelRunnerOutput`.
5757 """
5758 try:
5759 # Reporting per-request NaN counts requires them on the host; this
5760 # path is opt-in diagnostics, so the D2H is intended.
5761 with gpu_sync_allowed():
5762 counts = [] if logits is None else count_nans_per_row(logits).tolist()
5763 num_nans_in_logits = nans_to_dict(counts, self.input_batch.req_id_to_index)
5764 if envs.VLLM_RAISE_ON_LOGIT_NANS:
5765 raise_if_nan_logits(num_nans_in_logits)
5766 return num_nans_in_logits
5767 except IndexError:
5768 return {}
5769
5770 @contextmanager
5771 def maybe_randomize_inputs(
5772 self,
5773 input_ids: torch.Tensor | None,
5774 inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor | None,
5775 randomize_inputs: bool = False,
5776 ):
5777 """
5778 Randomize input_ids if VLLM_RANDOMIZE_DP_DUMMY_INPUTS is set.
5779 This is to help balance expert-selection
5780 - during profile_run
5781 - during DP rank dummy run
5782 """
5783
5784 dp_size = self.vllm_config.parallel_config.data_parallel_size
5785 randomize_inputs = randomize_inputs or (
5786 envs.VLLM_RANDOMIZE_DP_DUMMY_INPUTS and dp_size > 1
5787 )
5788 if not randomize_inputs:
5789 yield
5790 elif input_ids is not None:
5791
5792 @functools.cache
5793 def rand_input_ids() -> torch.Tensor:
5794 return torch.randint_like(
5795 self.input_ids.gpu,
5796 low=0,
5797 high=self.model_config.get_vocab_size(),
5798 )
5799
5800 logger.debug_once("Randomizing dummy input_ids for DP Rank")
5801 input_ids.copy_(rand_input_ids()[: input_ids.size(0)], non_blocking=True)
5802 yield
5803 input_ids.fill_(0)
5804 else:
5805
5806 @functools.cache
5807 def rand_inputs_embeds() -> torch.Tensor:
5808 return torch.randn_like(
5809 self.inputs_embeds.gpu,
5810 )
5811
5812 assert inputs_embeds is not None
5813 logger.debug_once("Randomizing dummy inputs_embeds for DP Rank")
5814 inputs_embeds.copy_(
5815 rand_inputs_embeds()[: inputs_embeds.size(0)], non_blocking=True
5816 )
5817 yield
5818 inputs_embeds.fill_(0)
5819
5820 def _get_mm_dummy_batch(
5821 self,
5822 modality: str,
5823 max_items_per_batch: int,
5824 ) -> BatchedTensorInputs:
5825 """Dummy data for profiling and precompiling multimodal models."""
5826 assert self.mm_budget is not None
5827
5828 # Don't use `max_items_per_batch` here to avoid redundant computation
5829 dummy_mm_inputs = self.mm_registry.get_dummy_mm_inputs(
5830 self.model_config,
5831 mm_counts={modality: 1},
5832 cache=self.mm_budget.cache,
5833 )
5834 dummy_mm_item = dummy_mm_inputs["mm_kwargs"][modality][0]
5835
5836 # We use the cache so that the item is saved to the cache,
5837 # but not read from the cache
5838 assert dummy_mm_item is not None, "Item should not already be cached"
5839
5840 return next(
5841 mm_kwargs_batch
5842 for _, _, mm_kwargs_batch in group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
5843 [(modality, dummy_mm_item)] * max_items_per_batch,
5844 device=self.device,
5845 pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY,
5846 )
5847 )
5848
5849 @torch.inference_mode()
5850 def _dummy_run(
5851 self,
5852 num_tokens: int,
5853 cudagraph_runtime_mode: CUDAGraphMode | None = None,
5854 force_attention: bool = False,
5855 uniform_decode: bool = False,
5856 allow_microbatching: bool = True,
5857 skip_eplb: bool = False,
5858 is_profile: bool = False,
5859 create_mixed_batch: bool = False,
5860 remove_lora: bool = True,
5861 is_graph_capturing: bool = False,
5862 num_active_loras: int = 0,
5863 profile_seq_lens: int | None = None,
5864 randomize_inputs: bool = False,
5865 ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
5866 """
5867 Run a dummy forward pass to warm up/profile run or capture the
5868 CUDA graph for the model.
5869
5870 Args:
5871 num_tokens: Number of tokens to run the dummy forward pass.
5872 cudagraph_runtime_mode: used to control the behavior.
5873 - if not set will determine the cudagraph mode based on using
5874 the self.cudagraph_dispatcher.
5875 - CUDAGraphMode.NONE: No cudagraph, for warm up and profile run
5876 - CUDAGraphMode.PIECEWISE: Piecewise cudagraph.
5877 - CUDAGraphMode.FULL: Full cudagraph, attention metadata is
5878 needed.
5879 force_attention: If True, always create attention metadata. Used to
5880 warm up attention backend when mode is NONE.
5881 uniform_decode: If True, the batch is a uniform decode batch.
5882 skip_eplb: If True, skip EPLB state update.
5883 is_profile: If True, this is a profile run.
5884 create_mixed_batch: If True, create a mixed batch with both decode
5885 (1 token) and prefill (multiple tokens) requests.
5886 remove_lora: If False, dummy LoRAs are not destroyed after the run
5887 num_active_loras: Number of distinct active LoRAs to capture for.
5888 LoRA is activated when num_active_loras > 0.
5889 profile_seq_lens: If provided, use this value for seq_lens instead
5890 of max_query_len. Used to profile attention workspace that
5891 scales with context length.
5892 """
5893 mm_config = self.vllm_config.model_config.multimodal_config
5894 if mm_config and mm_config.mm_encoder_only:
5895 # The current dummy run only covers LM execution, so we can skip it.
5896 # mm encoder dummy run may need to add in the future.
5897 return torch.tensor([]), torch.tensor([])
5898
5899 assert (
5900 cudagraph_runtime_mode is None
5901 or cudagraph_runtime_mode.is_valid_runtime_mode()
5902 )
5903
5904 # If cudagraph_mode.decode_mode() == FULL and
5905 # cudagraph_mode.separate_routine(). This means that we are using
5906 # different graphs and/or modes for mixed prefill-decode batches vs.
5907 # uniform decode batches. A uniform decode batch means that all
5908 # requests have identical query length, except a potential virtual
5909 # request (shorter) in the batch account for padding.
5910 # Uniform decode batch could either be common pure decode, where
5911 # max_query_len == 1, or speculative decode, where
5912 # max_query_len == 1 + num_spec_decode_tokens.
5913
5914 # When setting max_query_len = 1, we switch to and capture the optimized
5915 # routine of FA2 for pure decode, i.e., Flashdecode + an optimization
5916 # for GQA/MQA.
5917 max_query_len = self.uniform_decode_query_len if uniform_decode else num_tokens
5918
5919 # Set num_scheduled_tokens based on num_tokens and max_num_seqs
5920 # for dummy run with LoRA so that the num_reqs collectively
5921 # has num_tokens in total.
5922 assert num_tokens <= self.max_num_tokens
5923 max_num_reqs = self.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs
5924 if create_mixed_batch:
5925 assert not uniform_decode
5926 # Create mixed batch:
5927 # first half decode tokens, second half one prefill
5928 num_decode_tokens = min(max_num_reqs - 1, num_tokens // 2)
5929 num_prefill_tokens = num_tokens - num_decode_tokens
5930 num_reqs = num_decode_tokens + 1
5931
5932 # Create decode requests (1 token each) followed by prefill request
5933 num_scheduled_tokens_list = [1] * num_decode_tokens + [num_prefill_tokens]
5934 # Note: Overriding max_query_len to be the prefill tokens
5935 max_query_len = num_prefill_tokens
5936 elif uniform_decode:
5937 assert not create_mixed_batch
5938 num_reqs = min(max_num_reqs, cdiv(num_tokens, max_query_len))
5939 num_scheduled_tokens_list = [max_query_len] * num_reqs
5940 if num_tokens % max_query_len != 0:
5941 num_scheduled_tokens_list[-1] = num_tokens % max_query_len
5942 else:
5943 num_reqs = min(num_tokens, max_num_reqs)
5944 min_tokens_per_req = num_tokens // num_reqs
5945 num_scheduled_tokens_list = [min_tokens_per_req] * num_reqs
5946 num_scheduled_tokens_list[-1] += num_tokens % num_reqs
5947
5948 assert sum(num_scheduled_tokens_list) == num_tokens
5949 assert len(num_scheduled_tokens_list) == num_reqs
5950 num_scheduled_tokens = np.array(num_scheduled_tokens_list, dtype=np.int32)
5951 num_tokens_unpadded = int(num_scheduled_tokens.sum())
5952
5953 num_sampled_tokens = np.ones(num_reqs, dtype=np.int32)
5954
5955 _cudagraph_mode, batch_desc, should_ubatch, num_tokens_across_dp, _ = (
5956 self._determine_batch_execution_and_padding(
5957 num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
5958 num_reqs=num_reqs,
5959 num_scheduled_tokens_np=num_scheduled_tokens,
5960 max_num_scheduled_tokens=max_query_len,
5961 use_cascade_attn=False,
5962 allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
5963 force_eager=is_profile
5964 or (cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.NONE),
5965 # `force_uniform_decode` is used for cudagraph capture; because for
5966 # capturing mixed prefill-decode batches, we sometimes use
5967 # num_tokens == num_reqs which looks like a uniform decode batch to the
5968 # dispatcher; but we actually want to capture a piecewise cudagraph
5969 force_uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
5970 # `force_has_lora` is used for cudagraph capture; because LoRA is
5971 # activated later in the context manager, but we need to know the
5972 # LoRA state when determining the batch descriptor for capture
5973 force_has_lora=num_active_loras > 0,
5974 # `force_num_active_loras` is used for cudagraph capture; because we
5975 # need to capture graphs for specific num_active_loras counts
5976 force_num_active_loras=num_active_loras,
5977 )
5978 )
5979
5980 if cudagraph_runtime_mode is None:
5981 cudagraph_runtime_mode = _cudagraph_mode
5982 else:
5983 assert cudagraph_runtime_mode == _cudagraph_mode, (
5984 f"Cudagraph runtime mode mismatch in dummy_run. "
5985 f"Expected {_cudagraph_mode}, but got {cudagraph_runtime_mode}."
5986 )
5987
5988 num_tokens_padded = batch_desc.num_tokens
5989 num_reqs_padded = (
5990 batch_desc.num_reqs if batch_desc.num_reqs is not None else num_reqs
5991 )
5992 dcp_dummy_context_len = get_dcp_dummy_context_len(
5993 self.dcp_world_size,
5994 self.parallel_config.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size,
5995 hasattr(self, "kv_cache_config"),
5996 create_mixed_batch,
5997 is_graph_capturing,
5998 uniform_decode,
5999 )
6000 ubatch_slices, ubatch_slices_padded = maybe_create_ubatch_slices(
6001 should_ubatch,
6002 num_scheduled_tokens,
6003 num_tokens_padded,
6004 num_reqs_padded,
6005 self.vllm_config.parallel_config.num_ubatches,
6006 )
6007 logger.debug(
6008 "ubatch_slices: %s, ubatch_slices_padded: %s",
6009 ubatch_slices,
6010 ubatch_slices_padded,
6011 )
6012
6013 attn_metadata: PerLayerAttnMetadata | None = None
6014
6015 slot_mappings_by_group, slot_mappings = self._get_slot_mappings(
6016 num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded,
6017 num_reqs_padded=num_reqs_padded,
6018 num_tokens_unpadded=num_tokens_unpadded,
6019 ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
6020 )
6021
6022 # Dummy runs have no real slot assignments — fill with -1 so
6023 # concat_and_cache kernels skip the KV write.
6024 if slot_mappings_by_group is not None:
6025 for sm in slot_mappings_by_group.values():
6026 sm.fill_(-1)
6027
6028 # _dummy_run shares pinned CPU buffers (seq_lens, query_start_loc,
6029 # etc.) with execute_model. It must participate in the same event
6030 # protocol so that back-to-back dummy/real steps don't overwrite
6031 # pinned memory while a prior non_blocking H2D DMA is still reading.
6032 with self.synchronize_input_prep():
6033 # If force_attention is True, we always capture attention.
6034 # Otherwise, it only happens for cudagraph_runtime_mode=FULL.
6035 if force_attention or cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL:
6036 if profile_seq_lens is not None:
6037 seq_lens = profile_seq_lens # type: ignore[assignment]
6038 elif create_mixed_batch:
6039 # In the mixed batch mode (used for FI warmup), we use
6040 # shorter sequence lengths to run faster.
6041 # TODO(luka) better system for describing dummy batches
6042 if dcp_dummy_context_len > 0:
6043 seq_lens = torch.tensor( # type: ignore[assignment]
6044 [1 + dcp_dummy_context_len] * num_decode_tokens
6045 + [num_prefill_tokens + dcp_dummy_context_len],
6046 dtype=torch.int,
6047 )
6048 else:
6049 seq_lens = torch.tensor( # type: ignore[assignment]
6050 [1] * num_decode_tokens + [num_prefill_tokens + 1],
6051 dtype=torch.int,
6052 )
6053 elif dcp_dummy_context_len > 0:
6054 seq_lens = max_query_len + dcp_dummy_context_len # type: ignore[assignment]
6055 else:
6056 seq_lens = max_query_len # type: ignore[assignment]
6057 self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs] = seq_lens
6058 self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
6059 self.seq_lens.copy_(self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu, non_blocking=True)
6060
6061 cum_num_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
6062 num_scheduled_tokens, self.query_pos.np
6063 )
6064 self.query_start_loc.np[1 : num_reqs + 1] = cum_num_tokens
6065 self.query_start_loc.np[num_reqs + 1 : num_reqs_padded + 1].fill(
6066 cum_num_tokens[-1]
6067 )
6068 self.query_start_loc.copy_to_gpu()
6069
6070 prepare_dcp_dummy_context_metadata(
6071 input_batch=self.input_batch,
6072 kv_cache_config=getattr(self, "kv_cache_config", None),
6073 query_pos=self.query_pos,
6074 positions=self.positions,
6075 query_start_loc=self.query_start_loc,
6076 num_reqs=num_reqs,
6077 num_tokens_unpadded=num_tokens_unpadded,
6078 dcp_dummy_context_len=dcp_dummy_context_len,
6079 )
6080
6081 # Sync block table CPU->GPU so cleared rows from
6082 # remove_request() are visible to the attention metadata
6083 # builder. Without this, stale block IDs from finished
6084 # requests can corrupt Mamba state.
6085 self.input_batch.block_table.commit_block_table(num_reqs_padded)
6086
6087 pad_attn = cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
6088 attn_metadata, _ = self._build_attention_metadata(
6089 num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
6090 num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded if pad_attn else None,
6091 num_reqs=num_reqs_padded,
6092 max_query_len=max_query_len,
6093 ubatch_slices=(ubatch_slices_padded if pad_attn else ubatch_slices),
6094 # FULL replay reads capture-time metadata buffers. Re-stage them
6095 # from the zeroed dummy block tables instead of retaining state
6096 # indices from the previous real batch.
6097 for_cudagraph_capture=(
6098 is_graph_capturing
6099 or cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
6100 ),
6101 slot_mappings=slot_mappings_by_group,
6102 use_spec_decode=self.speculative_config is not None,
6103 )
6104
6105 with self.maybe_dummy_run_with_lora(
6106 self.lora_config,
6107 num_scheduled_tokens,
6108 num_sampled_tokens,
6109 remove_lora,
6110 num_active_loras,
6111 ):
6112 # Make sure padding doesn't exceed max_num_tokens
6113 assert num_tokens_padded <= self.max_num_tokens
6114 model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs(num_reqs=0)
6115 if self.supports_mm_inputs and not self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder:
6116 input_ids, inputs_embeds = self._prepare_mm_inputs(num_tokens_padded)
6117
6118 model_kwargs = {
6119 **model_kwargs,
6120 **self._dummy_mm_kwargs(num_reqs),
6121 }
6122 elif self.enable_prompt_embeds:
6123 input_ids = None
6124 inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_tokens_padded]
6125 model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs(num_reqs=0)
6126 else:
6127 input_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_tokens_padded]
6128 inputs_embeds = None
6129
6130 if self.uses_mrope:
6131 positions = self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_tokens_padded]
6132 else:
6133 positions = self.positions[:num_tokens_padded]
6134
6135 if get_pp_group().is_first_rank:
6136 intermediate_tensors = None
6137 else:
6138 if self.intermediate_tensors is None:
6139 self.intermediate_tensors = (
6140 self.model.make_empty_intermediate_tensors(
6141 batch_size=self.max_num_tokens,
6142 dtype=self.model_config.dtype,
6143 device=self.device,
6144 )
6145 )
6146
6147 intermediate_tensors = self.sync_and_gather_intermediate_tensors(
6148 num_tokens_padded, None, False
6149 )
6150
6151 if ubatch_slices_padded is not None:
6152 # Adjust values to reflect a single ubatch.
6153 # TODO(sage,lucas): this is cruft that should be addressed in
6154 # the padding refactor.
6155 num_tokens_padded = ubatch_slices_padded[0].num_tokens
6156 if num_tokens_across_dp is not None:
6157 num_tokens_across_dp[:] = num_tokens_padded
6158
6159 with (
6160 self.maybe_randomize_inputs(
6161 input_ids, inputs_embeds, randomize_inputs=randomize_inputs
6162 ),
6163 set_forward_context(
6164 attn_metadata,
6165 self.vllm_config,
6166 num_tokens=num_tokens_padded,
6167 num_tokens_across_dp=num_tokens_across_dp,
6168 cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_runtime_mode,
6169 batch_descriptor=batch_desc,
6170 ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
6171 slot_mapping=slot_mappings,
6172 ),
6173 ):
6174 outputs = self.model(
6175 input_ids=input_ids,
6176 positions=positions,
6177 intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,
6178 inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
6179 **model_kwargs,
6180 )
6181
6182 if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
6183 hidden_states, _ = outputs
6184 else:
6185 hidden_states = outputs
6186
6187 if self.speculative_config and (
6188 self.speculative_config.use_eagle()
6189 or self.speculative_config.uses_draft_model()
6190 or self.speculative_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
6191 ):
6192 assert isinstance(
6193 self.drafter,
6194 EagleProposer
6195 | DFlashProposer
6196 | DraftModelProposer
6197 | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
6198 | Gemma4Proposer,
6199 )
6200 assert self.speculative_config is not None
6201 # Eagle currently only supports PIECEWISE cudagraphs.
6202 # Therefore only use cudagraphs if the main model uses PIECEWISE
6203 # NOTE(lucas): this is a hack, need to clean up.
6204 use_cudagraphs = (
6205 (
6206 is_graph_capturing
6207 and cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.PIECEWISE
6208 )
6209 or (
6210 not is_graph_capturing
6211 and cudagraph_runtime_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE
6212 )
6213 ) and not self.speculative_config.enforce_eager
6214
6215 # Note(gnovack) - We need to disable cudagraphs for one of the two
6216 # lora cases when cudagraph_specialize_lora is enabled. This is a
6217 # short term mitigation for issue mentioned in
6218 # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/28334
6219 if (
6220 self.compilation_config.cudagraph_specialize_lora
6221 and num_active_loras > 0
6222 ):
6223 use_cudagraphs = False
6224
6225 self.drafter.dummy_run(
6226 num_tokens,
6227 use_cudagraphs=use_cudagraphs,
6228 is_graph_capturing=is_graph_capturing,
6229 slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
6230 )
6231
6232 # We register layerwise NVTX hooks here after the first dynamo tracing is
6233 # done to avoid nvtx operations in hook functions being traced by
6234 # torch dynamo and causing graph breaks.
6235 # Note that for DYNAMO_ONCE and VLLM_COMPILE mode,
6236 # compiled model's dynamo tracing is only done once and the compiled model's
6237 # __call__ function is replaced by calling the compiled function.
6238 # So it's safe to register hooks here. Hooks will be registered to
6239 # both compiled and uncompiled models but they will never
6240 # be called on the compiled model execution path.
6241 self._register_layerwise_nvtx_hooks()
6242
6243 # This is necessary to avoid blocking DP.
6244 # For dummy runs, we typically skip EPLB since we don't have any real
6245 # requests to process.
6246 # However, in DP settings, there may be cases when some DP ranks do
6247 # not have any requests to process, so they're executing dummy batches.
6248 # In such cases, we still have to trigger EPLB to make sure
6249 # ranks execute the rearrangement in synchronization.
6250 if not skip_eplb:
6251 self.eplb_step(is_dummy=True, is_profile=is_profile)
6252
6253 logit_indices = np.cumsum(num_scheduled_tokens) - 1
6254 logit_indices_device = async_tensor_h2d(logit_indices, device=self.device)
6255 return hidden_states, hidden_states[logit_indices_device]
6256
6257 @torch.inference_mode()
6258 def _dummy_sampler_run(
6259 self,
6260 hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
6261 ) -> torch.Tensor:
6262 # The dummy hidden states may contain special values,
6263 # like `inf` or `nan`.
6264 # To avoid breaking the sampler, we use a random tensor here instead.
6265
6266 mm_config = self.vllm_config.model_config.multimodal_config
6267 if mm_config and mm_config.mm_encoder_only:
6268 # MM Encoder only model no need to run sampler.
6269 return torch.tensor([])
6270
6271 hidden_states = torch.rand_like(hidden_states)
6272
6273 logits = self.model.compute_logits(hidden_states)
6274 num_reqs = logits.size(0)
6275
6276 dummy_tensors = lambda v: torch.full((num_reqs,), v, device=self.device)
6277
6278 dummy_metadata = SamplingMetadata(
6279 temperature=dummy_tensors(0.5),
6280 all_greedy=False,
6281 all_random=False,
6282 top_p=dummy_tensors(0.9),
6283 top_k=dummy_tensors(logits.size(1) - 1),
6284 generators={},
6285 max_num_logprobs=None,
6286 logprob_token_ids=None,
6287 no_penalties=True,
6288 prompt_token_ids=None,
6289 frequency_penalties=dummy_tensors(0.1),
6290 presence_penalties=dummy_tensors(0.1),
6291 repetition_penalties=dummy_tensors(0.1),
6292 output_token_ids=[[] for _ in range(num_reqs)],
6293 spec_token_ids=[[] for _ in range(num_reqs)],
6294 allowed_token_ids_mask=None,
6295 bad_words_token_ids={},
6296 logitsprocs=LogitsProcessors(),
6297 )
6298 try:
6299 sampler_output = self.sampler(
6300 logits=logits, sampling_metadata=dummy_metadata
6301 )
6302 # Also warm forward_native (taken when generators dict is non-empty),
6303 # but skip the extra call in 'processed_logits' / 'processed_logprobs'
6304 # modes — there TopKTopPSampler binds forward = forward_native at
6305 # init time, so the warmup call is redundant and only inflates peak
6306 # memory during profile_run.
6307 # No .clone() of logits: warmup output is discarded, so any in-place
6308 # mutation by forward_native does not affect correctness.
6309 if self.sampler.logprobs_mode not in PROCESSED_LOGPROBS_MODES:
6310 self.sampler(
6311 logits=logits,
6312 sampling_metadata=replace(
6313 dummy_metadata,
6314 generators={
6315 0: torch.Generator(device=self.device).manual_seed(0)
6316 },
6317 ),
6318 )
6319 except RuntimeError as e:
6320 if "out of memory" in str(e):
6321 raise RuntimeError(
6322 "CUDA out of memory occurred when warming up sampler with "
6323 f"{num_reqs} dummy requests. Please try lowering "
6324 "`max_num_seqs` or `gpu_memory_utilization` when "
6325 "initializing the engine."
6326 ) from e
6327 else:
6328 raise e
6329 if self.speculative_config:
6330 draft_token_ids = [[0] for _ in range(num_reqs)]
6331 dummy_spec_decode_metadata = SpecDecodeMetadata.make_dummy(
6332 draft_token_ids, self.device
6333 )
6334
6335 num_tokens = sum(len(ids) for ids in draft_token_ids)
6336 draft_probs = None
6337 if (
6338 self.speculative_config.rejection_sample_method == "standard"
6339 and self.speculative_config.draft_sample_method == "probabilistic"
6340 ):
6341 draft_probs = torch.rand(
6342 num_tokens,
6343 logits.shape[-1],
6344 device=self.device,
6345 dtype=torch.float32,
6346 )
6347 draft_probs = torch.softmax(draft_probs, dim=-1)
6348 logits = torch.randn(
6349 num_tokens + num_reqs,
6350 logits.shape[-1],
6351 device=self.device,
6352 dtype=logits.dtype,
6353 )
6354 self.rejection_sampler(
6355 dummy_spec_decode_metadata,
6356 draft_probs,
6357 logits,
6358 dummy_metadata,
6359 )
6360 # All-greedy is a separate Triton specialization from the
6361 # mixed-sampling path above. Compile and exercise it before serving.
6362 all_greedy_metadata = replace(
6363 dummy_metadata,
6364 temperature=None,
6365 all_greedy=True,
6366 all_random=False,
6367 )
6368 self.rejection_sampler(
6369 dummy_spec_decode_metadata,
6370 draft_probs,
6371 logits,
6372 all_greedy_metadata,
6373 )
6374 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6375 return sampler_output
6376
6377 def _dummy_pooler_run_task(
6378 self,
6379 hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
6380 task: PoolingTask,
6381 ) -> PoolerOutput:
6382 num_tokens = hidden_states.shape[0]
6383 max_num_reqs = self.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs
6384 num_reqs = min(num_tokens, max_num_reqs)
6385 min_tokens_per_req = num_tokens // num_reqs
6386 num_scheduled_tokens_np = np.full(num_reqs, min_tokens_per_req)
6387 num_scheduled_tokens_np[-1] += num_tokens % num_reqs
6388 assert np.sum(num_scheduled_tokens_np) == num_tokens
6389 assert len(num_scheduled_tokens_np) == num_reqs
6390
6391 req_num_tokens = num_tokens // num_reqs
6392
6393 dummy_prompt_lens = torch.from_numpy(num_scheduled_tokens_np)
6394 dummy_token_ids = torch.zeros(
6395 (num_reqs, req_num_tokens), dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device
6396 )
6397
6398 model = cast(VllmModelForPooling, self.get_model())
6399 dummy_pooling_params = PoolingParams(task=task)
6400 dummy_pooling_params.verify(self.model_config)
6401 to_update = model.pooler.get_pooling_updates(task)
6402 to_update.apply(dummy_pooling_params)
6403
6404 dummy_metadata = PoolingMetadata(
6405 prompt_lens=dummy_prompt_lens,
6406 prompt_token_ids=dummy_token_ids,
6407 prompt_token_ids_cpu=dummy_token_ids.cpu(),
6408 pooling_params=[dummy_pooling_params] * num_reqs,
6409 pooling_states=[PoolingStates() for i in range(num_reqs)],
6410 )
6411
6412 dummy_metadata.build_pooling_cursor(
6413 num_scheduled_tokens_np,
6414 seq_lens_cpu=dummy_prompt_lens,
6415 device=hidden_states.device,
6416 )
6417
6418 try:
6419 return model.pooler(
6420 hidden_states=hidden_states, pooling_metadata=dummy_metadata
6421 )
6422 except RuntimeError as e:
6423 if "out of memory" in str(e):
6424 raise RuntimeError(
6425 "CUDA out of memory occurred when warming up pooler "
6426 f"({task=}) with {num_reqs} dummy requests. Please try "
6427 "lowering `max_num_seqs` or `gpu_memory_utilization` when "
6428 "initializing the engine."
6429 ) from e
6430 else:
6431 raise e
6432
6433 @torch.inference_mode()
6434 def _dummy_pooler_run(
6435 self,
6436 hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
6437 ) -> PoolerOutput:
6438 mm_config = self.vllm_config.model_config.multimodal_config
6439 if mm_config and mm_config.mm_encoder_only:
6440 # MM Encoder only model not need to run pooler.
6441 return torch.tensor([])
6442
6443 # Find the task that has the largest output for subsequent steps
6444 supported_pooling_tasks = self.get_supported_pooling_tasks()
6445
6446 if not supported_pooling_tasks:
6447 raise RuntimeError(
6448 f"Model {self.model_config.model} does not support "
6449 "any pooling tasks. See "
6450 "https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/models/pooling_models.html "
6451 "to learn more."
6452 )
6453
6454 output_size = dict[PoolingTask, float]()
6455 for task in supported_pooling_tasks:
6456 # Run a full batch with each task to ensure none of them OOMs
6457 output = self._dummy_pooler_run_task(hidden_states, task)
6458 output_size[task] = sum(o.nbytes for o in output if o is not None)
6459 del output # Allow GC
6460
6461 max_task = max(output_size.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0]
6462 return self._dummy_pooler_run_task(hidden_states, max_task)
6463
6464 def profile_run(self) -> None:
6465 # Profile with multimodal encoder & encoder cache.
6466 if self.supports_mm_inputs:
6467 mm_config = self.model_config.multimodal_config
6468 if mm_config is not None and mm_config.skip_mm_profiling:
6469 logger.info(
6470 "Skipping memory profiling for multimodal encoder and "
6471 "encoder cache."
6472 )
6473 else:
6474 mm_budget = self.mm_budget
6475 assert mm_budget is not None
6476
6477 if (encoder_budget := mm_budget.get_encoder_budget()) > 0:
6478 if not mm_budget.mm_max_toks_per_item:
6479 # All modality limits are 0 — embedding-only mode.
6480 # Budget is non-zero for embedding storage, but
6481 # there's no encoder to profile.
6482 logger.info(
6483 "Skipping encoder profiling for embedding-only "
6484 "mode (all modality limits=0 with "
6485 "enable_mm_embeds=True).",
6486 )
6487 else:
6488 # NOTE: Currently model is profiled with a single
6489 # non-text modality with the max possible input
6490 # tokens even when it supports multiple.
6491 dummy_modality = mm_budget.get_modality_with_max_tokens()
6492 max_mm_items_per_batch = mm_budget.mm_max_items_per_batch[
6493 dummy_modality
6494 ]
6495
6496 logger.info_once(
6497 "Encoder cache will be initialized with a "
6498 "budget of %s tokens, and profiled with "
6499 "%s %s items of the maximum feature size.",
6500 encoder_budget,
6501 max_mm_items_per_batch,
6502 dummy_modality,
6503 )
6504
6505 # Create dummy batch of multimodal inputs.
6506 batched_dummy_mm_inputs = self._get_mm_dummy_batch(
6507 dummy_modality,
6508 max_mm_items_per_batch,
6509 )
6510
6511 # Run multimodal encoder.
6512 dummy_encoder_outputs = self.model.embed_multimodal(
6513 **batched_dummy_mm_inputs
6514 )
6515
6516 sanity_check_mm_encoder_outputs(
6517 dummy_encoder_outputs,
6518 expected_num_items=max_mm_items_per_batch,
6519 )
6520 for i, output in enumerate(dummy_encoder_outputs):
6521 self.encoder_cache[f"tmp_{i}"] = output
6522
6523 # Add `is_profile` here to pre-allocate communication buffers
6524 hidden_states, last_hidden_states = self._dummy_run(
6525 self.max_num_tokens, is_profile=True
6526 )
6527 if get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
6528 if self.is_pooling_model:
6529 output = self._dummy_pooler_run(hidden_states)
6530 else:
6531 output = self._dummy_sampler_run(last_hidden_states)
6532 else:
6533 output = None
6534 self._sync_device()
6535 del hidden_states, output
6536 self.encoder_cache.clear()
6537 gc.collect()
6538
6539 def _init_minimal_kv_cache_for_profiling(self) -> None:
6540 from vllm.v1.core.kv_cache_utils import (
6541 get_kv_cache_config_from_groups,
6542 get_kv_cache_groups,
6543 )
6544
6545 kv_cache_spec = self.get_kv_cache_spec()
6546 KVCacheSpecRegistry.check_kv_cache_spec_registry(kv_cache_spec)
6547 kv_cache_groups = get_kv_cache_groups(self.vllm_config, kv_cache_spec)
6548 # the minimum number of blocks required is 1 block *per sequence*
6549 min_blocks = (
6550 min(self.max_num_reqs, self.compilation_config.max_cudagraph_capture_size)
6551 or 1
6552 )
6553
6554 # Temporarily change num_gpu_blocks_override to allocate a minimal KV cache
6555 saved_override = self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks_override
6556 self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks_override = min_blocks
6557 minimal_config = get_kv_cache_config_from_groups(
6558 self.vllm_config, kv_cache_groups, available_memory=0
6559 )
6560 self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks_override = saved_override
6561
6562 self.initialize_kv_cache(minimal_config, is_profiling=True)
6563 self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks = minimal_config.num_blocks
6564
6565 logger.debug("Initialized minimal KV cache for CUDA graph profiling")
6566
6567 _freeze_gc = staticmethod(freeze_gc_for_cudagraph_capture)
6568
6569 def shutdown(self) -> None:
6570 """Release GPU tensors (model weights, KV caches, workspace) so that
6571 memory is reclaimable when running in the same process."""
6572 from vllm.model_executor.layers.rotary_embedding import _ROPE_DICT
6573 from vllm.v1.worker.workspace import reset_workspace_manager
6574
6575 # Calls torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6576 self._cleanup_profiling_kv_cache()
6577 if current_platform.is_rocm():
6578 # Drop captured graphs before distributed teardown. On ROCm, delayed
6579 # graph destruction can surface HSA faults in the next engine startup.
6580 CUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6581 BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6582 self.encoder_cudagraph_manager = None
6583 self.compilation_config.static_forward_context.clear()
6584 self.model = None # type: ignore[assignment]
6585 _ROPE_DICT.clear()
6586
6587 reset_workspace_manager()
6588 if current_platform.is_rocm() or current_platform.is_xpu():
6589 gc.collect()
6590 torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6591 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6592
6593 def _cleanup_profiling_kv_cache(self) -> None:
6594 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6595 if hasattr(self, "kv_caches") and self.kv_caches:
6596 for i in range(len(self.kv_caches)):
6597 self.kv_caches[i] = None # type: ignore
6598 self.kv_caches.clear()
6599 if hasattr(self, "attn_groups"):
6600 self.attn_groups.clear()
6601 if hasattr(self, "kv_cache_config"):
6602 delattr(self, "kv_cache_config")
6603 self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks = None
6604
6605 for layer in self.compilation_config.static_forward_context.values():
6606 if hasattr(layer, "kv_cache"):
6607 kv_cache = layer.kv_cache
6608 layer.kv_cache = (
6609 torch.tensor([]) if isinstance(kv_cache, torch.Tensor) else []
6610 )
6611 # Clean up quantized KV cache scale views
6612 # (int8_per_token_head, fp8_per_token_head)
6613 if hasattr(layer, "impl"):
6614 if hasattr(layer.impl, "_k_scale_cache"):
6615 layer.impl._k_scale_cache = None
6616 if hasattr(layer.impl, "_v_scale_cache"):
6617 layer.impl._v_scale_cache = None
6618
6619 gc.collect()
6620 torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6621
6622 logger.debug("Cleaned up profiling KV cache and CUDA graphs")
6623
6624 @torch.inference_mode()
6625 def _create_encoder_cudagraph_manager(self) -> "EncoderCudaGraphManager | None":
6626 if not (
6627 self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mm_encoder and self.supports_mm_inputs
6628 ):
6629 return None
6630
6631 # Use get_model() to unwrap CUDAGraphWrapper/UBatchWrapper, because
6632 # @runtime_checkable Protocol isinstance() checks do not work through
6633 # __getattr__ forwarding.
6634 from vllm.model_executor.models.interfaces import (
6635 SupportsEncoderCudaGraph,
6636 supports_encoder_cudagraph,
6637 )
6638 from vllm.v1.worker.encoder_cudagraph import (
6639 EncoderCudaGraphManager,
6640 )
6641
6642 raw_model = self.get_model()
6643 if not supports_encoder_cudagraph(raw_model):
6644 return None
6645
6646 return EncoderCudaGraphManager(
6647 vllm_config=self.vllm_config,
6648 device=self.device,
6649 dtype=self.dtype,
6650 model=cast(SupportsEncoderCudaGraph, raw_model),
6651 )
6652
6653 @torch.inference_mode()
6654 def _maybe_init_encoder_cudagraph_manager(self) -> None:
6655 if self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is None:
6656 self.encoder_cudagraph_manager = self._create_encoder_cudagraph_manager()
6657 if self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6658 logger.info("Initialized EncoderCudaGraphManager for vision encoder")
6659
6660 @torch.inference_mode()
6661 def profile_cudagraph_memory(self) -> int:
6662 with set_current_vllm_config(self.vllm_config):
6663 self._init_minimal_kv_cache_for_profiling()
6664
6665 saved_num_cudagraph_captured = compilation_counter.num_cudagraph_captured
6666
6667 capture_descs = self.cudagraph_dispatcher.get_capture_descs()
6668 # Use a temporary manager for memory profiling. The persistent manager
6669 # is initialized later so it does not keep profiling-only graph state.
6670 encoder_cudagraph_manager = self._create_encoder_cudagraph_manager()
6671
6672 decoder_graphs = sum(len(descs) for _, descs in capture_descs)
6673 encoder_graphs = (
6674 encoder_cudagraph_manager.get_num_graphs_to_capture()
6675 if encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None
6676 else 0
6677 )
6678 total_graphs = decoder_graphs + encoder_graphs
6679 if total_graphs == 0:
6680 logger.debug("No CUDA graphs will be captured, skipping profiling")
6681 self._cleanup_profiling_kv_cache()
6682 return 0
6683
6684 graph_groups = [
6685 *(
6686 f"{mode.name}={len(descs)} (largest={descs[0].num_tokens})"
6687 for mode, descs in capture_descs
6688 if descs
6689 ),
6690 ]
6691 if encoder_graphs > 0:
6692 graph_groups.append(
6693 f"ENCODER={encoder_graphs} "
6694 f"(largest={encoder_cudagraph_manager.token_budgets[-1]})"
6695 )
6696
6697 logger.info("Profiling CUDA graph memory: %s", ", ".join(graph_groups))
6698
6699 # Use a temporary pool for profiling to avoid fragmentation in the main pool.
6700 profiling_pool = current_platform.graph_pool_handle()
6701 encoder_profiling_pool = current_platform.graph_pool_handle()
6702 original_pools: dict[int, Any] = {}
6703 all_wrappers = list(CUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances) + list(
6704 BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances
6705 )
6706 for instance in all_wrappers:
6707 original_pools[id(instance)] = instance.graph_pool
6708 instance.graph_pool = profiling_pool
6709
6710 shared_memory_estimate = {}
6711 per_graph_estimate = {}
6712 encoder_memory_estimate = 0
6713
6714 # On ROCm, capture these throwaway profiling graphs on vLLM's dedicated
6715 # compute stream instead of the fresh side stream graph_capture()
6716 # allocates by default. torch's allocator pools free blocks per stream,
6717 # so a side-stream forward strands a persistent aiter scratch buffer in
6718 # a separate pool, shifting the physical placement of the real KV cache
6719 # allocated afterward and slowing bandwidth-bound decode ~20%. The
6720 # graphs are discarded, so a side stream is unnecessary here.
6721 # Use current_stream(), not torch.cuda.current_stream(): before vLLM
6722 # initializes its dedicated stream, torch returns the per-thread default
6723 # stream (cuda_stream=0), which cannot be used for cudagraph capture.
6724 # cap_ctx=None keeps the side-stream path on CUDA.
6725 cap_ctx = (
6726 GraphCaptureContext(current_stream())
6727 if current_platform.is_rocm()
6728 else None
6729 )
6730
6731 # Cleanup-only guard: CUDA graph capture errors should still propagate
6732 # because encoder graph capture is opt-in.
6733 try:
6734 set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(True)
6735 with (
6736 self._freeze_gc(),
6737 graph_capture(device=self.device, graph_capture_context=cap_ctx),
6738 ):
6739 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6740 torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6741
6742 for mode, descs in capture_descs:
6743 profile_descs = descs[:2]
6744 mem_samples: list[int] = []
6745
6746 for i, desc in enumerate(profile_descs):
6747 mem_before = torch.accelerator.get_memory_info()[0]
6748 self._warmup_and_capture(
6749 desc,
6750 cudagraph_runtime_mode=mode,
6751 profile_seq_lens=(
6752 min(
6753 self.max_model_len,
6754 self.max_num_tokens // desc.num_tokens,
6755 )
6756 if mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL and i == 0
6757 else None
6758 ),
6759 )
6760 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6761 free_after = torch.accelerator.get_memory_info()[0]
6762 mem_samples.append(mem_before - free_after)
6763
6764 first_capture = mem_samples[0]
6765 # Use at least 1 MiB per graph for driver overhead
6766 per_graph = max(
6767 mem_samples[1] if len(mem_samples) > 1 else 0, 1 << 20
6768 )
6769
6770 shared_memory_estimate[mode] = first_capture
6771 per_graph_estimate[mode] = per_graph * (len(descs) - 1)
6772
6773 logger.debug(
6774 "Estimated %s CUDA graph memory: "
6775 "%.2f MiB first-capture + (%d-1) × %.2f MiB per-graph",
6776 mode.name,
6777 first_capture / (1 << 20),
6778 len(descs),
6779 per_graph / (1 << 20),
6780 )
6781
6782 if encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6783 mem_before = torch.accelerator.get_memory_info()[0]
6784 encoder_cudagraph_manager.capture(graph_pool=encoder_profiling_pool)
6785 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6786 free_after = torch.accelerator.get_memory_info()[0]
6787 encoder_memory_estimate = max(mem_before - free_after, 0)
6788
6789 logger.debug(
6790 "Estimated encoder CUDA graph memory: %.2f MiB for %d graphs",
6791 encoder_memory_estimate / (1 << 20),
6792 encoder_graphs,
6793 )
6794 finally:
6795 set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(False)
6796 CUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6797 BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6798 if encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6799 encoder_cudagraph_manager.clear()
6800 all_wrappers = list(CUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances) + list(
6801 BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances
6802 )
6803 for instance in all_wrappers:
6804 if id(instance) in original_pools:
6805 instance.graph_pool = original_pools[id(instance)]
6806 for key_set in self.cudagraph_dispatcher.cudagraph_keys.values():
6807 key_set.clear()
6808 self.cudagraph_dispatcher.keys_initialized = False
6809 self.maybe_remove_all_loras(self.lora_config)
6810 self._cleanup_profiling_kv_cache()
6811 compilation_counter.num_cudagraph_captured = saved_num_cudagraph_captured
6812
6813 # FULL and PIECEWISE graphs share the global pool at runtime and are
6814 # never replayed concurrently, so the pool overlays their memory.
6815 # Take the max to avoid double-counting the overlap.
6816 decoder_estimate = max(shared_memory_estimate.values(), default=0) + sum(
6817 per_graph_estimate.values()
6818 )
6819 # Encoder graphs use a manager-local pool at runtime, separate from the
6820 # decoder pool, so add their estimate instead of overlaying it.
6821 total_estimate = decoder_estimate + encoder_memory_estimate
6822 logger.info(
6823 "Estimated CUDA graph memory: %.2f GiB total",
6824 total_estimate / (1 << 30),
6825 )
6826
6827 return int(total_estimate)
6828
6829 @instrument(span_name="Capture model")
6830 def capture_model(self) -> int:
6831 if self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode == CUDAGraphMode.NONE:
6832 logger.warning(
6833 "Skipping CUDA graph capture. To turn on CUDA graph capture, "
6834 "ensure `cudagraph_mode` was not manually set to `NONE`"
6835 )
6836 return 0
6837
6838 # Initialize encoder CUDA graph manager if enabled.
6839 self._maybe_init_encoder_cudagraph_manager()
6840
6841 compilation_counter.num_gpu_runner_capture_triggers += 1
6842
6843 start_time = time.perf_counter()
6844
6845 # Trigger CUDA graph capture for specific shapes.
6846 # Capture the large shapes first so that the smaller shapes
6847 # can reuse the memory pool allocated for the large shapes.
6848 set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(True)
6849
6850 # Setup torch profiler for graph capture traces (conditional)
6851 from vllm.distributed.parallel_state import get_world_group
6852
6853 local_rank = get_world_group().local_rank
6854 enable_profiler = (
6855 local_rank == 0
6856 ) and self.vllm_config.profiler_config.capture_torch_profiler
6857 if enable_profiler:
6858 trace_dir = (
6859 self.vllm_config.profiler_config.torch_profiler_dir + "/capture_traces"
6860 )
6861 profiler = torch.profiler.profile(
6862 activities=[
6863 torch.profiler.ProfilerActivity.CPU,
6864 torch.profiler.ProfilerActivity.CUDA,
6865 ],
6866 record_shapes=True,
6867 profile_memory=True,
6868 with_stack=True,
6869 on_trace_ready=torch.profiler.tensorboard_trace_handler(
6870 trace_dir,
6871 worker_name=f"graph_capture_rank_{local_rank}",
6872 use_gzip=True,
6873 ),
6874 )
6875 logger.info_once(
6876 "Rank %d: Torch profiler enabled for CUDA graph capture, "
6877 "traces will be saved to: %s",
6878 local_rank,
6879 trace_dir,
6880 )
6881 else:
6882 profiler = nullcontext()
6883 logger.info_once(
6884 "Rank %d: Torch profiler disabled for CUDA graph capture", local_rank
6885 )
6886
6887 with self._freeze_gc(), graph_capture(device=self.device):
6888 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6889 torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6890 start_free_gpu_memory = torch.accelerator.get_memory_info()[0]
6891
6892 for (
6893 runtime_mode,
6894 batch_descs,
6895 ) in self.cudagraph_dispatcher.get_capture_descs():
6896 self._capture_cudagraphs(
6897 batch_descriptors=batch_descs,
6898 cudagraph_runtime_mode=runtime_mode,
6899 profiler=profiler,
6900 )
