6901 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6902
6903 # Capture encoder CUDA graphs if enabled
6904 if self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6905 encoder_graph_pool = current_platform.graph_pool_handle()
6906 self.encoder_cudagraph_manager.capture(graph_pool=encoder_graph_pool)
6907
6908 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6909 end_free_gpu_memory = torch.accelerator.get_memory_info()[0]
6910
6911 # Disable cudagraph capturing globally, so any unexpected cudagraph
6912 # capturing will be detected and raise an error after here.
6913 # Note: We don't put it into graph_capture context manager because
6914 # we may do lazy capturing in future that still allows capturing
6915 # after here.
6916 set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(False)
6917
6918 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6919 torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6920
6921 # Lock workspace to prevent resizing during execution.
6922 # Max workspace sizes should have been captured during warmup/profiling.
6923 lock_workspace()
6924
6925 end_time = time.perf_counter()
6926 elapsed_time = end_time - start_time
6927 cuda_graph_size = start_free_gpu_memory - end_free_gpu_memory
6928 # This usually takes 5~20 seconds.
6929 logger.info_once(
6930 "Graph capturing finished in %.0f secs, took %.2f GiB",
6931 elapsed_time,
6932 cuda_graph_size / (1 << 30),
6933 )
6934 return cuda_graph_size
6935
6936 def _warmup_and_capture(
6937 self,
6938 desc: BatchDescriptor,
6939 cudagraph_runtime_mode: CUDAGraphMode,
6940 profile_seq_lens: int | None = None,
6941 allow_microbatching: bool = False,
6942 num_warmups: int | None = None,
6943 profiler: AbstractContextManager[Any] | None = None,
6944 ):
6945 if profiler is None:
6946 profiler = nullcontext()
6947 if num_warmups is None:
6948 num_warmups = self.compilation_config.cudagraph_num_of_warmups
6949 force_attention = cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
6950 for _ in range(num_warmups):
6951 self._dummy_run(
6952 desc.num_tokens,
6953 cudagraph_runtime_mode=CUDAGraphMode.NONE,
6954 force_attention=force_attention,
6955 uniform_decode=desc.uniform,
6956 allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
6957 skip_eplb=True,
6958 remove_lora=False,
6959 num_active_loras=desc.num_active_loras,
6960 profile_seq_lens=profile_seq_lens,
6961 )
6962 if num_warmups > 0:
6963 # Warmups may use auxiliary streams. Ensure all of their work has
6964 # completed before beginning CUDA graph capture.
6965 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6966 with (
6967 profiler,
6968 torch.profiler.record_function(
6969 f"capture_{desc.num_tokens}_{cudagraph_runtime_mode.name}"
6970 ),
6971 ):
6972 self._dummy_run(
6973 desc.num_tokens,
6974 cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_runtime_mode,
6975 uniform_decode=desc.uniform,
6976 allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
6977 skip_eplb=True,
6978 remove_lora=False,
6979 num_active_loras=desc.num_active_loras,
6980 is_graph_capturing=True,
6981 profile_seq_lens=profile_seq_lens,
6982 )
6983
6984 def _capture_cudagraphs(
6985 self,
6986 batch_descriptors: list[BatchDescriptor],
6987 cudagraph_runtime_mode: CUDAGraphMode,
6988 profiler: AbstractContextManager[Any] | None = None,
6989 ):
6990 assert (
6991 cudagraph_runtime_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE
6992 and cudagraph_runtime_mode.is_valid_runtime_mode()
6993 ), f"Invalid cudagraph runtime mode: {cudagraph_runtime_mode}"
6994
6995 if not batch_descriptors:
6996 return
6997
6998 uniform_decode = batch_descriptors[0].uniform
6999
7000 # Only rank 0 should print progress bar during capture
7001 if is_global_first_rank():
7002 batch_descriptors = tqdm(
7003 batch_descriptors,
7004 disable=not self.load_config.use_tqdm_on_load,
7005 desc="Capturing CUDA graphs ({}, {})".format(
7006 "decode" if uniform_decode else "mixed prefill-decode",
7007 cudagraph_runtime_mode.name,
7008 ),
7009 )
7010
7011 # We skip EPLB here since we don't want to record dummy metrics
7012 for batch_desc in batch_descriptors:
7013 # We currently only capture ubatched graphs when its a FULL
7014 # cudagraph, a uniform decode batch, and the number of tokens
7015 # is above the threshold. Otherwise we just capture a non-ubatched
7016 # version of the graph
7017 allow_microbatching = (
7018 self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
7019 and cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
7020 and uniform_decode
7021 and check_ubatch_thresholds(
7022 config=self.vllm_config.parallel_config,
7023 num_tokens=batch_desc.num_tokens,
7024 uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
7025 )
7026 )
7027 self._warmup_and_capture(
7028 batch_desc,
7029 cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_runtime_mode,
7030 allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
7031 profiler=profiler,
7032 )
7033 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
7034 self.maybe_remove_all_loras(self.lora_config)
7035
7036 def initialize_attn_backend(
7037 self,
7038 kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig,
7039 is_profiling: bool = False,
7040 ) -> None:
7041 """
7042 Initialize the attention backends and attention metadata builders.
7043 """
7044 assert len(self.attn_groups) == 0, "Attention backends are already initialized"
7045
7046 class AttentionGroupKey(NamedTuple):
7047 """Deduplication key for attention groups within a KV cache group.
7048
7049 Splits on per-rank ``num_heads_q`` in addition to backend + spec
7050 so layers with different Q-head counts (e.g. a spec-decode draft
7051 with fewer attention heads than its target) get separate metadata
7052 builders. The builders' scratch (e.g. ``softmax_segm_*`` in
7053 ``triton_attn``, ``num_qo_heads`` in FlashInfer) is sized by
7054 ``num_heads_q`` and assumes uniformity within the group; see
7055 ``get_num_attention_heads_from_layers`` in
7056 ``vllm/v1/attention/backends/utils.py``.
7057 """
7058
7059 attn_backend: type[AttentionBackend]
7060 kv_cache_spec: KVCacheSpec
7061 num_heads_q: int
7062
7063 def get_attn_backends_for_group(
7064 kv_cache_group_spec: KVCacheGroupSpec,
7065 ) -> tuple[dict[AttentionGroupKey, list[str]], set[type[AttentionBackend]]]:
7066 layer_type = cast(type[Any], AttentionLayerBase)
7067 layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(
7068 self.vllm_config, layer_type, kv_cache_group_spec.layer_names
7069 )
7070 attn_backends = {}
7071 attn_backend_layers = defaultdict(list)
7072 # Dedupe based on full class name; this is a bit safer than
7073 # using the class itself as the key because when we create dynamic
7074 # attention backend subclasses (e.g. ChunkedLocalAttention) unless
7075 # they are cached correctly, there will be different objects per
7076 # layer.
7077 for layer_name in kv_cache_group_spec.layer_names:
7078 attn_backend = layers[layer_name].get_attn_backend()
7079
7080 if layer_name in self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_eligible_layers:
7081 attn_backend = create_fast_prefill_custom_backend(
7082 "FastPrefill",
7083 attn_backend, # type: ignore[arg-type]
7084 )
7085
7086 full_cls_name = attn_backend.full_cls_name()
7087 layer_kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_group_spec.kv_cache_spec
7088 if isinstance(layer_kv_cache_spec, UniformTypeKVCacheSpecs):
7089 layer_kv_cache_spec = layer_kv_cache_spec.kv_cache_specs[layer_name]
7090 # Non-Attention layer types (e.g. Mamba1, ShortConv) do not
7091 # expose ``num_heads``; fall back to 0 so they cluster as
7092 # before. Such layers never coexist with Attention in a
7093 # single KV cache group (different KVCacheSpec), so the
7094 # fallback can never spuriously merge them with attention
7095 # layers.
7096 num_heads_q = getattr(layers[layer_name], "num_heads", 0)
7097 key = (full_cls_name, layer_kv_cache_spec, num_heads_q)
7098 attn_backends[key] = AttentionGroupKey(
7099 attn_backend, layer_kv_cache_spec, num_heads_q
7100 )
7101 attn_backend_layers[key].append(layer_name)
7102 return (
7103 {attn_backends[k]: v for k, v in attn_backend_layers.items()},
7104 set(group_key.attn_backend for group_key in attn_backends.values()),
7105 )
7106
7107 def create_attn_groups(
7108 attn_backends_map: dict[AttentionGroupKey, list[str]],
7109 kv_cache_group_id: int,
7110 ) -> list[AttentionGroup]:
7111 attn_groups: list[AttentionGroup] = []
7112 for key, layer_names in attn_backends_map.items():
7113 attn_group = AttentionGroup(
7114 key.attn_backend,
7115 layer_names,
7116 key.kv_cache_spec,
7117 kv_cache_group_id,
7118 )
7119
7120 attn_groups.append(attn_group)
7121 return attn_groups
7122
7123 attention_backend_maps = []
7124 attention_backend_list = []
7125 for kv_cache_group_spec in kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:
7126 attn_backends = get_attn_backends_for_group(kv_cache_group_spec)
7127 attention_backend_maps.append(attn_backends[0])
7128 attention_backend_list.append(attn_backends[1])
7129
7130 # Resolve cudagraph_mode before actually initialize metadata_builders
7131 self._check_and_update_cudagraph_mode(
7132 attention_backend_list,
7133 kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups,
7134 is_profiling=is_profiling,
7135 )
7136
7137 # Check if attention backend supports PCP&DCP and related features.
7138 check_attention_cp_compatibility(self.vllm_config)
7139
7140 for i, attn_backend_map in enumerate(attention_backend_maps):
7141 self.attn_groups.append(create_attn_groups(attn_backend_map, i))
7142
7143 def initialize_metadata_builders(
7144 self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig, kernel_block_sizes: list[int]
7145 ) -> None:
7146 """
7147 Create the metadata builders for all KV cache groups and attn groups.
7148 """
7149 for kv_cache_group_id in range(len(kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)):
7150 for attn_group in self.attn_groups[kv_cache_group_id]:
7151 attn_group.create_metadata_builders(
7152 self.vllm_config,
7153 self.device,
7154 kernel_block_sizes[kv_cache_group_id]
7155 if kv_cache_group_id < len(kernel_block_sizes)
7156 else None,
7157 num_metadata_builders=1
7158 if not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
7159 else self.parallel_config.num_ubatches,
7160 )
7161 # Calculate reorder batch threshold (if needed)
7162 # Note (tdoublep): do this *after* constructing builders,
7163 # because some of them change the threshold at init time.
7164 self.calculate_reorder_batch_threshold()
7165
7166 # Initialize drafter attention backend
7167 if self.speculative_config and (
7168 self.speculative_config.use_eagle()
7169 or self.speculative_config.uses_draft_model()
7170 ):
7171 assert isinstance(
7172 self.drafter,
7173 EagleProposer | DFlashProposer | DraftModelProposer | Gemma4Proposer,
7174 )
7175 self.drafter.initialize_attn_backend(kv_cache_config, kernel_block_sizes)
7176
7177 def _check_and_update_cudagraph_mode(
7178 self,
7179 attention_backends: list[set[type[AttentionBackend]]],
7180 kv_cache_groups: list[KVCacheGroupSpec],
7181 is_profiling: bool = False,
7182 ) -> None:
7183 """
7184 Resolve the cudagraph_mode when there are multiple attention
7185 groups with potential conflicting CUDA graph support.
7186 Then initialize the cudagraph_dispatcher based on the resolved
7187 cudagraph_mode.
7188 """
7189 min_cg_support = AttentionCGSupport.ALWAYS
7190 min_cg_attn_backend = None
7191
7192 for attn_backend_set, kv_cache_group in zip(
7193 attention_backends, kv_cache_groups
7194 ):
7195 for attn_backend in attn_backend_set:
7196 builder_cls = attn_backend.get_builder_cls()
7197
7198 cg_support = builder_cls.get_cudagraph_support(
7199 self.vllm_config, kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec
7200 )
7201 if cg_support.value < min_cg_support.value:
7202 min_cg_support = cg_support
7203 min_cg_attn_backend = attn_backend.__name__
7204 cudagraph_mode = self.compilation_config.resolve_cudagraph_mode_and_sizes(
7205 min_cg_support,
7206 min_cg_attn_backend,
7207 self.uniform_decode_query_len,
7208 use_v2_model_runner=False,
7209 tensor_parallel_size=self.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size,
7210 kv_cache_config=self.kv_cache_config,
7211 max_num_reqs=self.max_num_reqs,
7212 is_profiling=is_profiling,
7213 )
7214 # Trigger cudagraph dispatching keys initialization after
7215 # resolved cudagraph mode.
7216 self.cudagraph_dispatcher.initialize_cudagraph_keys(
7217 cudagraph_mode, self.uniform_decode_query_len
7218 )
7219
7220 # Initialize drafter's cudagraph dispatcher if using spec decode.
7221 if self.speculative_config and (
7222 self.speculative_config.use_eagle()
7223 or self.speculative_config.uses_draft_model()
7224 or self.speculative_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
7225 ):
7226 assert isinstance(
7227 self.drafter,
7228 EagleProposer
7229 | DFlashProposer
7230 | DraftModelProposer
7231 | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
7232 | Gemma4Proposer,
7233 )
7234 self.drafter.initialize_cudagraph_keys(cudagraph_mode)
7235
7236 def calculate_reorder_batch_threshold(self) -> None:
7237 """
7238 Choose the minimum reorder batch threshold from all attention groups.
7239 Backends should be able to support lower threshold then what they request
7240 just may have a performance penalty due to that backend treating decodes
7241 as prefills.
7242 """
7243 min_none_high = lambda a, b: a if b is None else b if a is None else min(a, b)
7244
7245 reorder_batch_thresholds: list[int | None] = [
7246 group.get_metadata_builder().reorder_batch_threshold
7247 for group in self._attn_group_iterator()
7248 ]
7249 # If there are no attention groups (attention-free model) or no backend
7250 # reports a threshold, leave reordering disabled.
7251 if len(reorder_batch_thresholds) == 0:
7252 self.reorder_batch_threshold = None
7253 return
7254 self.reorder_batch_threshold = reduce(min_none_high, reorder_batch_thresholds) # type: ignore[assignment]
7255
7256 def may_reinitialize_input_batch(
7257 self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig, kernel_block_sizes: list[int]
7258 ) -> None:
7259 """
7260 Re-initialize the input batch if the block sizes are different from
7261 what it was originally created with. This happens when the final
7262 block size (determined after model loading) differs from the
7263 placeholder used during __init__, or when there are multiple
7264 KV cache groups.
7265
7266 Args:
7267 kv_cache_config: The KV cache configuration.
7268 kernel_block_sizes: The kernel block sizes for each KV cache group.
7269 """
7270 block_sizes = []
7271 max_num_blocks = []
7272 slot_mapping_modes = []
7273 max_model_len = max(self.max_model_len, self.max_encoder_len)
7274 for kv_cache_group in kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:
7275 kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec
7276 kv_cache_spec_kind = get_kv_cache_spec_kind(kv_cache_spec)
7277 if kv_cache_spec_kind == KVCacheSpecKind.ENCODER_ONLY_ATTENTION:
7278 continue
7279 block_size = kv_cache_spec.block_size
7280 block_sizes.append(block_size)
7281 if kv_cache_spec_kind == KVCacheSpecKind.MAMBA:
7282 slot_mapping_modes.append(SlotMappingMode.NONE)
7283 else:
7284 slot_mapping_modes.append(SlotMappingMode.TOKEN_TO_KV_SLOT)
7285 max_num_blocks_per_req = kv_cache_spec.max_num_blocks_per_req(
7286 self.vllm_config, max_model_len
7287 )
7288 max_num_blocks.append(max_num_blocks_per_req)
7289
7290 if (
7291 block_sizes != self._init_block_sizes
7292 or kernel_block_sizes != self._init_kernel_block_sizes
7293 or max_num_blocks != self._init_max_num_blocks
7294 or slot_mapping_modes != self._init_slot_mapping_modes
7295 or self.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size
7296 != self.parallel_config.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size
7297 ):
7298 self._init_block_sizes = block_sizes
7299 self._init_kernel_block_sizes = kernel_block_sizes
7300 self._init_max_num_blocks = max_num_blocks
7301 self._init_slot_mapping_modes = slot_mapping_modes
7302 self.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size = (
7303 self.parallel_config.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size
7304 )
7305 # Capture warmup providers registered after final KV-cache geometry is known
7306 with self.jit_warmup_registry.activate(): # type: ignore[attr-defined]
7307 self.input_batch = InputBatch(
7308 max_num_reqs=self.max_num_reqs,
7309 max_model_len=max_model_len,
7310 max_num_batched_tokens=self.max_num_tokens,
7311 device=self.device,
7312 vocab_size=self.model_config.get_vocab_size(),
7313 block_sizes=block_sizes,
7314 kernel_block_sizes=kernel_block_sizes,
7315 max_num_blocks_per_req=max_num_blocks,
7316 num_spec_tokens=self.num_spec_tokens,
7317 logitsprocs=self.input_batch.logitsprocs,
7318 logitsprocs_need_output_token_ids=self.input_batch.logitsprocs_need_output_token_ids,
7319 is_pooling_model=self.is_pooling_model,
7320 cp_kv_cache_interleave_size=self.parallel_config.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size,
7321 reasoning_config=self.vllm_config.reasoning_config,
7322 use_replayssm=self.cache_config.use_replayssm,
7323 slot_mapping_modes=slot_mapping_modes,
7324 )
7325
7326 assert self._init_block_sizes == block_sizes, (
7327 f"InputBatch block_sizes {self._init_block_sizes} != "
7328 f"kv_cache block_sizes {block_sizes}"
7329 )
7330 assert self._init_kernel_block_sizes == kernel_block_sizes, (
7331 f"InputBatch kernel_block_sizes {self._init_kernel_block_sizes} "
7332 f"!= kv_cache kernel_block_sizes {kernel_block_sizes}"
7333 )
7334
7335 def _attn_group_iterator(self) -> Iterator[AttentionGroup]:
7336 return itertools.chain.from_iterable(self.attn_groups)
7337
7338 def _kv_cache_spec_attn_group_iterator(self) -> Iterator[AttentionGroup]:
7339 if not self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:
7340 return
7341 for attn_groups in self.attn_groups:
7342 yield from attn_groups
7343
7344 def initialize_kv_cache_tensors(
7345 self,
7346 kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig,
7347 kernel_block_sizes: list[int],
7348 kv_cache_allocation_context: AbstractContextManager | None = None,
7349 ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
7350 """
7351 Initialize the memory buffer for KV cache.
7352
7353 Args:
7354 kv_cache_config: The KV cache config
7355 kernel_block_sizes: The kernel block sizes for each KV cache group.
7356
7357 Returns:
7358 Dict[str, torch.Tensor]: A map between layer names to their
7359 corresponding memory buffer for KV cache.
7360 """
7361
7362 allocation_context = kv_cache_allocation_context or nullcontext()
7363 with allocation_context:
7364 kv_caches = allocate_kv_cache(
7365 kv_cache_config,
7366 self.device,
7367 self.cache_config.get_resolved_kv_cache_layout(),
7368 kernel_block_sizes,
7369 )
7370
7371 # Set up cross-layer KV cache sharing
7372 for layer_name, target_layer_name in self.shared_kv_cache_layers.items():
7373 logger.debug("%s reuses KV cache of %s", layer_name, target_layer_name)
7374 kv_caches[layer_name] = kv_caches[target_layer_name]
7375
7376 num_attn_module = (
7377 2 if self.model_config.hf_config.model_type == "longcat_flash" else 1
7378 )
7379 bind_kv_cache(
7380 kv_caches,
7381 self.compilation_config.static_forward_context,
7382 self.kv_caches,
7383 num_attn_module,
7384 kv_cache_groups=kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups,
7385 )
7386 return kv_caches
7387
7388 def maybe_add_kv_sharing_layers_to_kv_cache_groups(
7389 self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig
7390 ) -> None:
7391 """
7392 Add layers that re-use KV cache to KV cache group of its target layer.
7393 Mapping of KV cache tensors happens in `initialize_kv_cache_tensors()`
7394 """
7395 if not self.shared_kv_cache_layers:
7396 # No cross-layer KV sharing, return
7397 return
7398
7399 add_kv_sharing_layers_to_kv_cache_groups(
7400 self.shared_kv_cache_layers,
7401 kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups,
7402 self.runner_only_attn_layers,
7403 )
7404
7405 if self.cache_config.kv_sharing_fast_prefill:
7406 # In You Only Cache Once (https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05254) or other
7407 # similar KV sharing setups, only the layers that generate KV caches
7408 # are involved in the prefill phase, enabling prefill to early exit.
7409 attn_layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention)
7410 for layer_name in reversed(attn_layers):
7411 if layer_name in self.shared_kv_cache_layers:
7412 self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_eligible_layers.add(layer_name)
7413 else:
7414 break
7415
7416 def initialize_kv_cache(
7417 self,
7418 kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig,
7419 is_profiling: bool = False,
7420 kv_cache_allocation_context: AbstractContextManager | None = None,
7421 ) -> None:
7422 """
7423 Initialize KV cache based on `kv_cache_config`.
7424 Args:
7425 kv_cache_config: Configuration for the KV cache, including the KV
7426 cache size of each layer
7427 """
7428 kv_cache_config = deepcopy(kv_cache_config)
7429 self.kv_cache_config = kv_cache_config
7430 self._mamba_bufs = None
7431 self._mamba_state_copy_funcs = None
7432 self.may_add_encoder_only_layers_to_kv_cache_config()
7433 self.maybe_add_kv_sharing_layers_to_kv_cache_groups(kv_cache_config)
7434 self.initialize_attn_backend(kv_cache_config, is_profiling=is_profiling)
7435 initialize_mamba_ssu_backend(
7436 self.vllm_config.mamba_config,
7437 self.kv_cache_config,
7438 use_replayssm=self.vllm_config.cache_config.use_replayssm,
7439 )
7440 # The kernel block size for all KV cache groups. For example, if
7441 # kv_cache_manager uses block_size 256 for a given group, but the attention
7442 # backends for that group only supports block_size 64, we will return
7443 # kernel_block_size 64 and split the 256-token-block to 4 blocks with 64
7444 # tokens each.
7445 kernel_block_sizes = prepare_kernel_block_sizes(
7446 kv_cache_config, self.attn_groups
7447 )
7448 self._kernel_block_sizes = kernel_block_sizes
7449
7450 # create metadata builders
7451 self.initialize_metadata_builders(kv_cache_config, kernel_block_sizes)
7452
7453 # Reinitialize need to after initialize_attn_backend
7454 self.may_reinitialize_input_batch(kv_cache_config, kernel_block_sizes)
7455 # Capture warmup providers that depend on allocated KV-cache strides.
7456 with self.jit_warmup_registry.activate(): # type: ignore[attr-defined]
7457 kv_caches = self.initialize_kv_cache_tensors(
7458 kv_cache_config,
7459 kernel_block_sizes,
7460 kv_cache_allocation_context=kv_cache_allocation_context,
7461 )
7462
7463 if (
7464 self.speculative_config
7465 and self.speculative_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
7466 ):
7467 assert isinstance(self.drafter, ExtractHiddenStatesProposer)
7468 # validate all draft model layers belong to the same kv cache
7469 # group
7470 self.drafter.validate_same_kv_cache_group(kv_cache_config)
7471
7472 if has_kv_transfer_group() and not is_profiling:
7473 kv_transfer_group = get_kv_transfer_group()
7474 kv_transfer_group.register_kv_caches(kv_caches)
7475 kv_transfer_group.set_host_xfer_buffer_ops(copy_kv_blocks)
7476
7477 def get_routed_experts(
7478 self,
7479 num_tokens: int,
7480 ) -> RoutedExpertsTensors | None:
7481 if not self.routed_experts_initialized:
7482 return None
7483
7484 device_buffer = self.routed_experts_capturer.get_device_buffer()
7485 return RoutedExpertsTensors(
7486 routing_data=device_buffer[:num_tokens].clone(),
7487 slot_mapping=self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device[:num_tokens].clone(),
7488 )
7489
7490 def init_routed_experts_capturer(self):
7491 logger.info(
7492 "Initializing routed experts capturer, enable_return_routed_experts: %s",
7493 self.model_config.enable_return_routed_experts,
7494 )
7495 self.routed_experts_capturer = RoutedExpertsCapturer(
7496 max_num_batched_tokens=self.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens,
7497 vllm_config=self.vllm_config,
7498 kv_cache_config=self.kv_cache_config,
7499 )
7500 bind_routed_experts_capturer(self.model, self.routed_experts_capturer)
7501
7502 # Pinned CPU buffer for non-blocking D2H of ``routing_data`` on
7503 # the sync scheduling path. Shape / dtype mirror the device
7504 # capturer exactly so ``copy_`` is a straight memcpy.
7505 self.routed_experts_cpu = torch.empty(
7506 self.routed_experts_capturer.device_buffer.shape,
7507 dtype=self.routed_experts_capturer.device_buffer.dtype,
7508 device="cpu",
7509 pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY,
7510 )
7511 # ``slot_mapping`` dtype is fixed to int64 by
7512 # ``block_table.slot_mapping``; we mirror that here.
7513 max_tokens = self.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens
7514 self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_cpu = torch.empty(
7515 (max_tokens,),
7516 dtype=torch.int64,
7517 device="cpu",
7518 pin_memory=PIN_MEMORY,
7519 )
7520 # Private device buffer so the shared ``block_table.slot_mapping``
7521 # can be overwritten by the next ``_prepare_inputs`` while the
7522 # D2H is still pending on the copy stream. Written in
7523 # ``_prepare_inputs``, read in ``_bookkeeping_sync`` (sync path)
7524 # or cloned into a snapshot (async path).
7525 self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device = torch.empty(
7526 (max_tokens,),
7527 dtype=torch.int64,
7528 device=self.device,
7529 )
7530 self.routed_experts_initialized = True
7531
7532 def may_add_encoder_only_layers_to_kv_cache_config(self) -> None:
7533 """
7534 Add encoder-only layers to the KV cache config.
7535 """
7536 block_size = self.vllm_config.cache_config.block_size
7537 encoder_only_attn_specs: dict[AttentionSpec, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
7538 attn_layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention)
7539 for layer_name, attn_module in attn_layers.items():
7540 if attn_module.attn_type == AttentionType.ENCODER_ONLY:
7541 attn_spec: AttentionSpec = EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec(
7542 block_size=block_size,
7543 num_kv_heads=attn_module.num_kv_heads,
7544 head_size=attn_module.head_size,
7545 dtype=self.kv_cache_dtype,
7546 )
7547 encoder_only_attn_specs[attn_spec].append(layer_name)
7548 self.runner_only_attn_layers.add(layer_name)
7549 if len(encoder_only_attn_specs) > 0:
7550 assert len(encoder_only_attn_specs) == 1, (
7551 "Only support one encoder-only attention spec now"
7552 )
7553 spec, layer_names = encoder_only_attn_specs.popitem()
7554 self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups.append(
7555 KVCacheGroupSpec(layer_names=layer_names, kv_cache_spec=spec)
7556 )
7557
7558 def get_kv_cache_spec(self) -> dict[str, KVCacheSpec]:
7559 """
7560 Generates the KVCacheSpec by parsing the kv cache format from each
7561 Attention module in the static forward context.
7562 Returns:
7563 KVCacheSpec: A dictionary mapping layer names to their KV cache
7564 format. Layers that do not need KV cache are not included.
7565 """
7566 if has_ec_transfer() and not get_ec_transfer().is_consumer:
7567 return {}
7568 kv_cache_spec: dict[str, KVCacheSpec] = {}
7569 layer_type = cast(type[Any], AttentionLayerBase)
7570 attn_layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, layer_type)
7571 for layer_name, attn_module in attn_layers.items():
7572 if isinstance(attn_module, Attention) and (
7573 kv_tgt_layer := attn_module.kv_sharing_target_layer_name
7574 ):
7575 # The layer doesn't need its own KV cache and will use that of
7576 # the target layer. We skip creating a KVCacheSpec for it, so
7577 # that KV cache management logic will act as this layer does
7578 # not exist, and doesn't allocate KV cache for the layer. This
7579 # enables the memory saving of cross-layer kv sharing, allowing
7580 # a given amount of memory to accommodate longer context lengths
7581 # or enable more requests to be processed simultaneously.
7582 self.shared_kv_cache_layers[layer_name] = kv_tgt_layer
7583 continue
7584 # Skip modules that don't need KV cache (eg encoder-only attention)
7585 if spec := attn_module.get_kv_cache_spec(self.vllm_config):
7586 if isinstance(spec, AttentionSpec):
7587 spec = attn_module.get_attn_backend().customize_spec(spec)
7588 kv_cache_spec[layer_name] = spec
7589
7590 return kv_cache_spec
7591
7592 def _to_list(self, sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor) -> list[list[int]]:
7593 # This is a short term mitigation for issue mentioned in
7594 # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/22754.
7595 # `tolist` would trigger a cuda wise stream sync, which
7596 # would block other copy ops from other cuda streams.
7597 # A cuda event sync would avoid such a situation. Since
7598 # this is in the critical path of every single model
7599 # forward loop, this has caused perf issue for a disagg
7600 # setup.
7601 pinned = self.sampled_token_ids_pinned_cpu[: sampled_token_ids.shape[0]]
7602 pinned.copy_(sampled_token_ids, non_blocking=True)
7603 self.transfer_event.record()
7604 self.transfer_event.synchronize()
7605 return pinned.tolist()
7606
7607 def get_encoder_timing_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, float | int]]:
7608 """
7609 Get encoder timing stats for all requests and clear the registry.
7610
7611 Returns:
7612 Dictionary mapping request_id to stats dict.
7613 """
7614 with self._encoder_timing_lock:
7615 stats = {
7616 req_id: stats_obj.to_dict()
7617 for req_id, stats_obj in self.encoder_timing_registry.items()
7618 }
7619 self.encoder_timing_registry.clear()
7620 return stats
7621
7622 @contextmanager
7623 def timed_encoder_operation(
7624 self,
7625 should_time: bool,
7626 group_lora_refs: list[tuple[str, Any]],
7627 current_item_idx: int,
7628 num_items: int,
7629 ):
7630 """
7631 Context manager to time encoder forward operations.
7632
7633 Args:
7634 should_time: Whether timing is enabled
7635 group_lora_refs: Full list of (request_id, pos_info) tuples
7636 current_item_idx: Starting index for this group
7637 num_items: Number of items in this group
7638 """
7639 if not should_time:
7640 yield
7641 return
7642
7643 group_refs = group_lora_refs[current_item_idx : current_item_idx + num_items]
7644 group_request_ids = {req_id for req_id, _ in group_refs}
7645
7646 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
7647 start_time = time.perf_counter()
7648
7649 try:
7650 yield
7651 finally:
7652 torch.accelerator.synchronize()
7653 elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time
7654
7655 per_request_time = elapsed / max(len(group_request_ids), 1)
7656
7657 with self._encoder_timing_lock:
7658 for req_id in group_request_ids:
7659 if req_id not in self.encoder_timing_registry:
7660 self.encoder_timing_registry[req_id] = EncoderTimingStats()
7661
7662 stats = self.encoder_timing_registry[req_id]
7663 stats.encoder_forward_secs += per_request_time
7664 stats.num_encoder_calls += 1
